# Brand Studio — Feature Log

Newest entry at the top. One entry per logical change, not per file.

- 2026-08-22: pmg-used-cars — derive theme neutrals from brand tokens (no fixed greys) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: app/themes/pmg-used-cars/styles/base.css, pages/sell-your-car/sell-your-car.css
  - Reason: the grey ramp (--c-50..900), --jet-raised/--jet-soft, --surface-page and --line-dark were fixed hex, so those neutrals never retinted with the brand. Derived them from --color-text/--color-bg/--jet via color-mix (byte-close to the PMG originals, and now shift coherently for any brand). Also bridged --pmg-red-deep-rgb → var(--color-primary-rgb) and the sell-your-car widget --sycw-bg → var(--surface). Kept intentionally-fixed literals: semantic status (--ok green, --star amber, stock tags), WhatsApp brand green, mask #000, UK-flag SVG, and var(--color-*, #hex) fallbacks (the token drives; hex is only the unset fallback).
  - Notes: color-contract literals 124→ mostly fallbacks/status/masks/vendored now; derived greys verified rendering on /used-cars. Build validated.

- 2026-08-22: pmg-used-cars — wire the CDN widgets (WhatsApp enquiry, gallery, enquiry, reserve) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: app/themes/pmg-used-cars/shell.tsx (re-enable shared CarousWhatsAppWidget), lib/vendor/dealer-shell/external-widgets.ts + index.ts (export WIDGETS_BASE_URL + gallery/enquiry/reserve SRC consts), pages/used-cars/[slug]/page.tsx (load the 3 detail bundles via <Script afterInteractive>)
  - Reason: PMG loaded NONE of the widgets.carous.co.uk bundles, so the entire external-widget layer was dead. The vendored dealer-shell deliberately omitted DealerWidgetScripts and the shell disabled the shared floating WhatsApp launcher (widgets.whatsApp:false) — yet VehicleDetail.tsx still calls useExternalVehicleGallery / openExternalVehicleEnquiry / openExternalReservation / useExternalWhatsAppEnquiryScope against window globals that were never published. Result: the vehicle gallery never mounted (empty #vehicle-gallery-mount), Enquire + Reserve buttons were no-ops, and the WhatsApp enquiry panel never appeared. Fix loads whatsapp-enquiry.js site-wide (shared widget, bottom-right — clears PMG's bottom-left consent bar and consumes the per-vehicle subject the detail page publishes) and gallery/enquiry/reserve bundles on the detail page.
  - Notes: shared CarousWhatsAppWidget reads brand.location phone/whatsapp → returns null (no launcher) if the brand has no number, so graceful for numberless previews. cookieBanner stays disabled (PMG uses its own PmgConsentBar).

- 2026-08-22: Content Contract v2 — every static slot is LLM/operator-fillable (text · image · video) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: brands/types.ts (brand.text + brand.media typed); app/themes/lib/theme-text.ts (new shared resolveText factory); app/themes/lib/theme-images.ts (media/video slot schema + resolveMedia); app/themes/components/BrandMedia.tsx (new img/video renderer); app/themes/{fbm-motors,kain-motors-bespoke,redgate-lodge-bespoke}/lib/brand-text.ts (folded onto shared resolver); tools/check-image-contract.mjs (media/video-aware), tools/check-text-contract.mjs (new copy-leakage gate), tools/check-theme-contract.mjs (ignore components/ dir), tools/theme-video-catalogue.json (new), tools/fetch-theme-images.mjs (media-recipe-aware, skip video), package.json (check:media/text/content scripts); app.py (_load_theme_media_recipe, _media_autofill, /api/themes/<id>/media-recipe, media auto-fill in /api/v1/preview/create); app/themes/pmg-used-cars/** (reference theme — media-recipe.json + hero video slot + text-recipe.json + copy wired to resolveText)
  - Reason: themes had two half-built dynamic layers (text-recipe on 3 themes; image-recipe on 2, no video, media never auto-filled at preview-create) so most previews looked templated. Unified into ONE enforced content contract: every editable atom is a slot (text or media = image|video), the backend fills all of it (text via LLM, images via curated catalogue, video via stock catalogue with poster fallback) so a dealer preview never renders a hardcoded string or path. Two recipes kept + extended (text-recipe.json, media-recipe.json with a `type` field; loaders fall back to image-recipe.json). resolveText de-duplicated into a shared factory (was copy-pasted per theme). Video via <BrandMedia>: progressive-enhancement (poster still on server/reduced-motion/≤640px, autoplay muted-inline clip on capable clients) — never blank, never janky.
  - Notes: enforcement is STAGED — gates + shared plumbing land now, pmg-used-cars is the fully-migrated reference; the required-files flip + migrating the other themes is a follow-on so main never 500s. theme-video-catalogue.json ships empty (poster fallback governs) until license-clear clips/brand uploads are added. AI text-to-video can drop in later behind the same brand.media/<BrandMedia> interface.

- 2026-08-22: pmg-used-cars — fix token-drive: emit --color-* on :root, not [data-theme-id] (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: app/themes/pmg-used-cars/context/BrandStyles.tsx
  - Reason: the previous token bridge silently didn't work — a real brand (Morris: blue primary, brown/yellow palette) still rendered jet+red. BrandStyles emitted the --color-* set scoped to [data-theme-id="pmg-used-cars"] (the <body>), but base.css declares the bridges (--pmg-red: var(--color-primary), --jet: var(--color-secondary), …) on :root. A custom property's var() resolves at its DECLARATION element, so at :root --color-primary was undefined and every bridge fell back to the PMG literal. Emitting to :root (the default, like all reference themes) puts the tokens on the same element as the bridges. Verified live: Morris's palette now fully retints (blue CTAs/banner, yellow accents). Note: brand.theme.colors uses *Color keys (primaryColor, …) — buildThemeTokens reads those; short keys are ignored.

- 2026-08-21: pmg-used-cars — token-drive the palette (bridge pmg tokens → canonical --color-*) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: app/themes/pmg-used-cars/styles/base.css
  - Reason: the theme emitted the full canonical --color-* set (BrandStyles/buildThemeTokens) but its CSS ran on hardcoded --pmg-red/--jet/--ink/--surface/--muted/--line literals, so only primary retinted per brand — dashboard edits to secondary/accent/bg/text/muted/border did nothing. Bridged the pmg semantic tokens (+ their rgb triples) to the canonical tokens with the pmg literal as fallback: --pmg-red-signal→accent, --pmg-red-deep→primary-strong, --jet→secondary, --ink→text, --muted→muted, --line→border, --surface→bg, *-rgb→*-rgb. Neutral grey ramp (--c-*) + status hues stay fixed.
  - Notes: brand colors currently null → all resolve to the pmg palette via BrandStyles defaults, so render is byte-identical; verified live (identical home render + chrome retints when secondary/accent are changed). CSS-only; build validated (45/45 pages).

- 2026-08-12: Live Monitor — star "special" previews + real-time engagement analytics (pages viewed, time spent, viewing-now) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `preview_store.py` (schema + analytics/aggregation methods), `app.py` (beacon + specials/metrics routes, `/specials` page, `serialize_preview_row`), `app/widgets/PreviewAnalytics/*` (new always-on beacon), `app/api/preview-analytics/[...path]/route.ts` (new proxy), `app/layout.tsx` (mount), `static/modules/ui-components.js` + `static/modules/app.js` (dashboard ★ toggle), `static/modules/specials.js` (new), `templates/specials.html` (new), `templates/base.html` (nav), `static/style.css`.
  - Reason: Boss wants to identify the hottest prospect leads and watch, live, whether the previews sent out are actually being opened — with pages viewed, time spent, and who's viewing right now — so sales can target the most engaged dealers first. A star in the dashboard Actions column marks a preview "special"; a new Live Monitor page shows those specials with live metrics and a per-row drill-down.
  - Notes: Observability is deliberately decoupled from the existing preview *access gate* (`preview_sessions`), which only tracks time when a preview is locked/limited. A new always-on `PreviewAnalytics` client widget (mounted beside `PreviewGate` in the root layout) opens a session on every preview render, reports a pageview on each `usePathname()` change, heartbeats every 10s **only while the tab is visible**, and finalises on `pagehide` via `sendBeacon` — so data flows for *every* preview and a preview starred later already has history + shows up live. New MySQL tables `preview_analytics_sessions` (visit-level: `active_seconds`, `page_count`, ip/ua/referrer/device) and `preview_analytics_pageviews` (path-level: `seconds_on_page`); both use the **capped-delta** accumulation trick from the gate (`LEAST(GREATEST(TIMESTAMPDIFF...),0),20)`) so a backgrounded tab can't bank hours. `previews.special TINYINT` is the star flag (dashboard focus filter only — never gates collection). A session is "live" when its last heartbeat is within `LIVE_WINDOW_SECONDS=30` (one dropped 10s beat tolerated). Public beacon routes (`/api/preview-analytics/{session,pageview,heartbeat,session/<id>/end}`) are proxied by Next and fail soft; dashboard reads (`/api/previews/<slug>/special`, `/api/specials`, `/api/specials/live`, `/api/previews/<slug>/metrics`) are `login_required`. Live Monitor page polls `/api/specials/live` every 5s (pausable). UI was rebuilt from scratch after a blank-page bug: the CSS lives in a linked, fully-namespaced `static/specials.css` (under `#lm-root` / `.lm-drawer`) and the drawer is toggled by a `.is-open` class — NOT the `hidden` attribute, which the global sheet's `display` rules overrode, leaving the drawer scrim covering the whole page (see [[feedback_hidden_attr_vs_display]]). Content wrapper matches `.dashboard-section` (full width, no extra padding) for page-to-page uniformity. Final layout: header with a live-count chip + Auto-refresh switch; a 3-tile KPI strip (Tracked / Total visits / Watch time); a compact **paginated table** (12/page, client-side windowed pager) with avatar + name/domain, live pill, and numeric Visits/Time/Pages/Last-seen columns; row-click opens an on-demand slide-over drawer (stat tiles, live sessions, a **pages breakdown where each page links to its live URL**, recent-visits timeline). Verified via rendered screenshots (desktop + mobile + drawer). Dashboard previews table was also compacted (denser rows, 30px icons, uppercase headers, tightened actions), set to 12 items/page, and had its Slug column removed (domain already shown; slug still searchable). **Two bugs caught in verification and fixed**: (1) the new tables defaulted to MySQL 8.4's `utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci` while legacy `previews` is `utf8mb4_unicode_ci`, so the specials JOIN threw error 1267 — pinned the analytics tables to `COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci`; (2) a same-second heartbeat returned a false 404 because it relied on the UPDATE's *changed*-row count (MySQL reports changed, not matched) — switched to a `SELECT status` existence check. Verified end-to-end against local MySQL 8.4 (store lifecycle + all aggregations), through the Flask stack via `test_client` (beacon 2xx, auth reads 401, `/specials` renders), response-shaping helpers, `tsc --noEmit` clean, and JS module syntax. Not yet driven in a real browser (star click / live polling / beacon on a running preview) — pending a Flask+Next boot.

- 2026-08-11: Infra — enable `carouswebsites.co.uk` as a preview base domain (wildcard DNS added; blocked on a Cloudflare redirect) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: Cloudflare DNS (zone `carouswebsites.co.uk`, id `e05b5b0e5b048d4829d02624b0d12244`); VPS `46.202.140.63` Apache vhosts (`/etc/apache2/sites-{available,enabled}/*.carouswebsites.co.uk.conf`).
  - Reason: brandstudio's settings automation had already generated + enabled per-dealer vhosts (`kainmotorslimited`, `kainmotors`, `stanleyautomotive`) proxying to Next on `:4013`, but preview subdomains never resolved and never rendered. Goal: make `<slug>.carouswebsites.co.uk` behave like `<slug>.carouspreviews.co.uk`.
  - Notes: Diagnosed three gaps vs the working `carouspreviews.co.uk` zone. (1) **No wildcard DNS** — `carouspreviews.co.uk` has `*.carouspreviews.co.uk → 46.202.140.63 (proxied)`, which is why any subdomain (incl. forbury, which has no vhost) works via the default `:443` vhost + `ProxyPreserveHost`. `carouswebsites.co.uk` had only apex + www, so every subdomain was NXDOMAIN. **Fixed**: created `*.carouswebsites.co.uk → 46.202.140.63 (proxied)` via the CF API (token has `dns_records:edit` on the zone). (2) **Origin cert** — the generated vhosts point at the generic `/etc/ssl/cloudflare/cloudflare-cert.pem` (SAN `*.carous.co.uk`) instead of a per-zone origin cert like `carouspreviews-origin.pem`. TLS handshake still succeeds through CF (no 526), so the zone is NOT Full-strict and the generic cert works for now — left as-is (do NOT repoint to a non-existent `carouswebsites-origin.pem` or Apache fails to start). Optional follow-up: mint a CF Origin CA cert for `*.carouswebsites.co.uk` and repoint. (3) **BLOCKER — Cloudflare zone redirect**: a Page/Redirect rule on `carouswebsites.co.uk` 301s the apex, www, and every subdomain to `https://carous.co.uk/` at the edge (`Server: cloudflare`, single hop) before reaching origin. Origin itself returns 200 for the slugs (verified via SNI curl to `127.0.0.1`). The scoped API token lacks Page Rules / Rulesets / SSL perms (9109 / auth error), so this must be removed in the CF dashboard by the account owner (Shajeel) — delete/scope the `carouswebsites.co.uk → carous.co.uk` forwarding rule. Once removed, previews render with no further VPS changes; new slugs need zero DNS/Apache work (wildcard + default vhost cover them).

- 2026-08-10: /create — stop the domain field hinting the previous active domain from a stale draft (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `templates/index.html` (`applyCreateDraftPlaceholders`).
  - Reason: After switching the active managed domain in Settings, /create still showed the old domain (e.g. `showroomshinecars.carouspreviews.co.uk` while `carouswebsites.co.uk` was active). The domain input starts empty, and the create-draft autosave had persisted the full old domain string, which `applyCreateDraftPlaceholders` restored verbatim as the field's placeholder — so the stale domain was what the operator saw.
  - Notes: The submitted domain was already correct (both the autosave and final submit recompute `computeDomainFromSlug(slug)` against the active base) — only the visual placeholder was stale. Fixed by special-casing the `domain` key in `applyCreateDraftPlaceholders`: instead of hinting the saved domain, recompute it from the saved slug + the current active base (which is already seeded from `serverActiveDomainBase` before placeholders are applied), and skip the hint entirely when it can't be derived. Follow-up to the same-day server-side active-domain fix below.

- 2026-08-10: /create — resolve the active managed domain server-side so it's picked everywhere (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app.py` (`get_active_managed_domain_base` new, `/create` route, `list_managed_domains` GET, `_resolve_preview_url_for_slug`); `templates/index.html` (create-mode domain seeding).
  - Reason: The /create page picked the active managed domain purely client-side — an async `/api/settings/domains?status=active` fetch with a "newest active" heuristic. The server never told the page which domain was active, so the domain field could render blank/stale before the fetch resolved, a browser-cached response could serve a stale active domain after switching it in Settings, and a failed fetch blanked the base entirely. Preview-URL resolution also fell back to a hardcoded `carouspreviews.co.uk` instead of the active domain.
  - Notes: New `get_active_managed_domain_base()` returns the single active base (bare host; falls back to the newest managed domain of any status). The `/create` route now injects it into the template as `serverActiveDomainBase`, which seeds `selectedManagedDomainBase` synchronously in create-mode init — so the domain is correct on the first slug sync, before/without the async fetch. The async loader still refreshes but now falls back to the server seed on fetch error instead of blanking. Added `Cache-Control: no-store` to the domains GET so switching the active domain in Settings is reflected immediately everywhere. `_resolve_preview_url_for_slug` now derives its base from the active managed domain (env `PREVIEW_BASE_DOMAIN` only as last resort). `serverActiveDomainBase` is a module-level JS global (not a scoped const) so the top-level loader can read it. Builds on the same-day activation fix below.

- 2026-08-10: Settings › Domains — let operators activate any domain (auto-deactivates the rest) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `templates/settings.html` (DomainManager modal only).
  - Reason: The Add/Edit Domain modal disabled the Status `<select>` whenever another domain was already active, so a newly-added or inactive domain could never be switched on — operators were stuck with whichever domain happened to be active first.
  - Notes: The backend already enforced single-active correctly — both `create_managed_domain` and `update_managed_domain` in `app.py` run `UPDATE managed_domains SET status='inactive' WHERE status='active'` before activating the target. Only the frontend was blocking it. Removed all `statusInput.disabled` gating from `openModal`; the field is now always selectable. New domains still default to `inactive` when an active one exists (so adding one doesn't silently steal the active slot) but remain fully activatable. Added a live `#modalDomainStatusHint` that warns "Activating this will deactivate \"<domain>\"" when the chosen status will trigger a switch (updates on the select's `change` event and on modal open; cleared on close).

- 2026-08-05: Dashboard brands table — Theme column now shows the theme name, not the primary hex (owner: Difatha)

The table cell renders `_theme_name` (keeping the color swatch as an accent); `.col-theme` widened 120→160px and a `.theme-name` style added. Verified `serialize_preview_row` output — `fbm-motors → FBM Motors`, missing theme → `classic-dealer → Classic Dealer`. Only the table view changed; the card-grid view was left as-is (out of scope).

- 2026-08-05: classic-dealer — fix broken full-screen gallery + wire CDN email-enquiry and reserve widgets (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/classic-dealer/components/Vehicle.tsx`; `app/themes/classic-dealer/styles/vehicle.css`; `app/themes/classic-dealer/pages/used-cars/[slug]/page.tsx`; `app/themes/classic-dealer/lib/external-widgets.ts` (new).
  - Reason: (1) The vehicle-detail "Full Screen" lightbox did nothing — clicking never showed the overlay. (2) The detail sidebar only had a local Email Enquiry modal and no Reserve action; the theme should use the hosted Carous widgets like fbm-motors.
  - Notes: **Root cause of the gallery bug was cross-theme CSS leakage** — `gilded-drive/styles/vehicle.css` ships an UNSCOPED global `.vehicle-lightbox { display:none }` (its legacy `.open`-based lightbox). That rule bundles into the shared CSS chunk and, being unscoped + later in source order, overrode classic's `.vehicle-lightbox { display:flex }`, so the overlay was `display:none` regardless of the `is-open` class. Fix: scoped classic's two lightbox container rules to `.classic-theme .vehicle-lightbox` / `.classic-theme .vehicle-lightbox.is-open` (specificity 0,2,0 beats the leaked global 0,1,0 regardless of order); classic's unique sub-element classes (`.vehicle-lightbox-backdrop/-inner/-close`) don't collide with gilded's `.lb-*` so they were left alone. gilded-drive itself was NOT touched (big legacy theme; no other theme is currently broken by its leak since the rest use CSS-module or `body[data-theme-id]` scoping) — flagged here as a latent landmine. (2) New `lib/external-widgets.ts` mirrors fbm-motors (CDN base `widgets.carous.co.uk`, `vehicle-enquiry` + `reserve-a-car` bundles) with added `isExternal*Ready()` guards; `page.tsx` loads both bundles via `<Script strategy="afterInteractive">`; `Vehicle.tsx` builds an `ExternalVehicleEnquirySummary` from the normalized vehicle and wires **Reserve This Vehicle** (CDN reserve, falls back to the enquiry flow) + **Email Enquiry** (CDN enquiry, **falls back to the existing local `EnquiryForm` modal** when the bundle hasn't loaded). Gallery stays local. Verified live against the axisautosltd preview (real AutoTrader stock): full-screen lightbox now opens with counter/close/nav; both CDN widget scripts inject and 200 from the CDN; all three sidebar buttons render; tsc exit 0.

- 2026-08-04: dual-stock-modern-bespoke — hide Featured cars/bikes rail when the dealer has none (no sample stock) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/components/sections/FeaturedStock.tsx`.
  - Reason: Difatha caught the homepage showing a "Featured Bikes" rail full of hardcoded placeholder motorcycles (Ducati/BMW/Yamaha/Harley Unsplash cards under a "PREVIEW STOCK" badge) on Bronte Vehicles, a car-only dealer. Wanted: if the dealer stocks no bikes, hide the bikes rail completely — and the same for cars.
  - Notes: Deleted the six-item `SAMPLE_BIKES` fallback (and the `bikesAreSamples`/`sampleBadge`/"Preview stock" tag path) entirely. `FeaturedStock` now renders each rail only when that vehicle class has real inventory: `showCars = cars.length > 0`, `showBikes = bikes.length > 0`. A car-only dealer shows just the cars rail; a bike-only dealer just the bikes rail; if neither exists the whole section returns null. Section heading adapts ("… cars and bikes" / "… bikes" / "…"). During the client fetch a single neutral "Featured stock" skeleton shows (not per-type) so an empty type never flashes fake cards. Removed the now-unreachable per-rail empty-state branch. tsc exit 0; verified against the dualstock-qa brand (12 cars, 0 bikes) — loads to the cars rail only, no bikes rail. NOTE (follow-up, not in this change): the Hero "cars · bikes" tabs, the StatsBar "BIKES 40+" figure, and the BrandsWeStock "BIKES" column are still hardcoded and would also mislead a car-only dealer — flagged to Difatha as a separate theme-identity decision.

- 2026-08-04: dual-stock-modern-bespoke — vehicle card media switched to 16:9 (matches AutoTrader feed) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/components/cards/{VehicleCard.tsx, VehicleCard.module.css}`.
  - Reason: Follow-up to the earlier `contain` fix — Difatha reported production cards STILL looked cropped. Root cause pinned by measuring the actual feed: the AutoTrader stock photos are 2048×1152 (**16:9**), not the 4:3 the card frame assumed — so `cover` in a 4:3 box cropped the front/rear off every exterior shot. (Some other dealers' feeds are 4:3, e.g. the QA/Columbus test brands at 2048×1536; a blurred-fill approach was prototyped for the mixed case but Difatha chose a single 16:9 frame everywhere for simplicity.)
  - Notes: Changed the shared `VehicleCard` media `aspect-ratio: 4/3 → 16/9` and kept `background-size: cover`, so 16:9 stock photos fill the frame edge-to-edge with no crop and no letterbox bars. Fixes every card in one place — homepage `FeaturedStock`/`RecentlySold`, `/used-cars` grid, wishlist, compare, detail "similar" rail. Trade-off Difatha accepted: a dealer whose feed is 4:3 gets a modest top/bottom crop in the 16:9 frame. Restored the hover `scale(1.04)` zoom (safe with cover). Kept the `{resize}` path-token normalization from the prior entry. Verified with an isolated A/B render on a real 16:9 Bronte photo — 16:9 frame fills the whole Focus front-to-rear, uncropped. tsc exit 0. Deployed to production (VPS) via deploy.yml.

- 2026-08-04: dual-stock-modern-bespoke — vehicle card images no longer cropped/cut (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/components/cards/VehicleCard.module.css`; `app/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/lib/inventory.ts`.
  - Reason: Difatha review of the Bronte Vehicles preview — inventory (and homepage) card photos looked "cut out." Two causes: (1) the shared `VehicleCard` media frame used `background-size: cover` in a fixed 4:3 box, so 16:9 dealer exterior shots got ~25% cropped off the sides; (2) dual-stock's `lib/inventory.ts` was the ONLY theme not normalizing the AutoTrader `{resize}` path token, so it rendered a literal-token URL (`/a/media/{resize}/hash.jpg`) that the CDN serves as a mis-sized/mis-cropped variant.
  - Notes: Fixes every card in one place — homepage `FeaturedStock` + `RecentlySoldRail`, `/used-cars` inventory grid, wishlist, compare and the detail-page "similar" rail all render through the shared `VehicleCard`. (1) `.mediaImage` switched `cover`→`contain` so the whole vehicle always shows regardless of source aspect; `.media` backdrop switched `--color-bg`→`--color-surface` so any letterbox space around a non-4:3 photo reads as clean card padding, not a visible frame; dropped the `scale(1.04)` image zoom-on-hover (it clips a contained image's edges — the card still lifts via `translateY`). (2) Added a `normalizeImageUrl` helper mirroring classic-dealer — decodes `%7Bresize%7D` then strips the `/{resize}/` or `/WxH/` size segment from the path so we request the true original, which Next's optimizer then resizes. Applied to both `collectImages` (gallery) and the primary `image`. Tokens-only, brand-immune (card is a locked light surface). Verified: tsc exit 0. (Live screenshot verification skipped at Difatha's request — ships to the VPS build.)

- 2026-08-04: dual-stock-modern-bespoke — futuristic swipeable vehicle gallery widget (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/components/{VehicleGallery.tsx, VehicleGallery.module.css}` (new); `pages/used-cars/[slug]/{page.tsx, page.module.css}`.
  - Reason: Difatha wanted the vehicle-detail photo widget rebuilt as a modern, futuristic gallery — smooth animated swiping back-and-forth with indicators — instead of the static "hero + 2×2 thumb mosaic + separate lightbox" it shipped with.
  - Notes: Extracted the whole gallery (main image, thumb mosaic, lightbox, keyboard nav, index/lightbox state) out of the detail page into a self-contained `VehicleGallery` island. New widget is a single transform-based sliding track (`translate3d`, expo cubic-bezier easing) with real pointer/touch drag — follow-the-finger while dragging, snap on release past a 56px commit threshold, tap-under-6px opens the lightbox. Modern affordances: glass blur nav arrows with a brand-primary glow on hover, animated pill dot indicators that stretch + glow on the active slide and auto-collapse to a gradient scrubber bar past 10 photos, an auto-centering thumbnail rail (`scrollIntoView` keeps the active thumb in view), a counter chip, and a keyboard-accessible "Full screen" button that opens a swipeable full-bleed lightbox (Esc/←/→, scroll-lock). Tokens-only + light/dark-palette safe (`color-mix` over `--color-primary`/`--surface-dark`/`--color-on-dark`); `prefers-reduced-motion` kills the slide + glow easing. Page shed ~120 lines of gallery markup + the dead `.galleryMain/.galleryThumbs/.lightbox*` CSS; the wrapper `.gallery` is now a plain centred container. Verified: tsc exit 0; theme-contract 15/15 PASS; image-contract 0 violations. (Live screenshot verification was interrupted before capture; ships to the VPS build where it renders on every dual-stock detail page.)

- 2026-08-04: dual-stock-modern-bespoke — Lenis site-wide smooth scrolling (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/components/{SmoothScroll.tsx, SmoothScroll.css, Shell.tsx}`.
  - Reason: Difatha wanted the dual-stock theme to match the smooth-scroll feel already shipped on fbm-motors / redgate / cnhcars / classic-dealer / axis-autos.
  - Notes: New `SmoothScroll` component mirrors the established theme pattern verbatim — `<ReactLenis root>` (renders no DOM) with the shared default options (duration 1.1, expo easing, smoothWheel on, syncTouch off so mobile keeps native touch scroll), plus the companion override sheet forcing `scroll-behavior:auto` + `height:auto` on `html.lenis` (native smooth-scroll and a pinned body height both fight Lenis's RAF scrollTop). Folded into the Shell's header fragment (`header={<><SmoothScroll /><Header /></>}`) so it mounts once and only when this theme is active. `lenis@^1.3.25` already in package.json. Verified: tsc exit 0; post-JS DOM dump of the Columbus Vehicles preview shows `<html class="lenis">` (Lenis initialised and active).

  - Reason: Difatha review of the Columbus Vehicles preview — (1) the primary header menu was 8 flat items and felt long; wanted it compacted with dropdowns "where possible"; (2) the /used-cars (All Stock) hero "looked shit" — a cramped left-aligned dark band — and should mirror the fbm-motors elevated small hero.
  - Notes: (1) Nav data model reworked to a `NavEntry = NavItem | NavGroup` union with an `isGroup` guard; only the four stock-browse links collapse into ONE **Stock ▾** dropdown (All Stock / Cars / Bikes / Recently Sold) whose trigger still links to /used-cars. Per Difatha's follow-ups, Home / About / Contact stay flat, Finance and Part Exchange stay flat, and "Sell your car" stays the standalone header CTA (NOT folded into a menu). Dropdown is pure CSS hover + `:focus-within` reveal (no gap to fall through — panel sits flush at `top:100%`), chevron rotates, `aria-haspopup`/`role=menu`/`aria-current` wired; mobile drawer renders the Stock group under a small heading and flat items as plain links. (2) Inventory hero rebuilt as bespoke markup in `UsedCarsClient` using local module classes instead of the shared `.dual-page-hero` band (so restyling never touches the other 12 inner pages that still use it) — centred breadcrumb → red/teal brand eyebrow → uppercase title → lead → hairline check-mark assurance strip (Every vehicle inspected · History-checked · Nationwide delivery), photo at 0.55 opacity behind a radial legibility scrim + one soft brand-primary glow top-right, layered `var(--brand-image-hero, none), url(theme default)` so a 404 reveals the default. Modest mobile floor: ≤640 tightens padding, ≤480 drops the lead + third assurance. Tokens-only + brand-agnostic; tsc exit 0; screenshot-verified against the Columbus Vehicles preview (dual-stock-modern-bespoke) at 505 + 1440.

- 2026-08-04: fbm-motors theme — hero/testimonials/header visual polish pass (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/fbm-motors/components/{Testimonials.tsx, Testimonials.module.css, Header.module.css, PageHero.tsx, PageHero.module.css}`; `pages/home/{page.tsx, page.module.css}`; `pages/used-cars/page.tsx`; `recipes/text-recipe.json`.
  - Reason: Difatha review of the Columbus Vehicles preview — testimonials read poorly, header was sticky/cramped with small type, the home hero was murky with a whitewashed accent word + visible grid, and the inventory hero was a tall empty dark band.
  - Notes: (1) Testimonials rebuilt from near-transparent glass towers in a fixed 3-col grid into a centred flex-wrap of solid dark cards (carbon-950 scrim, star rating, avatar-initial byline, hairline footer) that reflow cleanly for any review count; quote text moved to full-opacity light token so it stays legible over the forecourt photo. (2) Header un-stuck (`position: sticky`→`relative`); desktop nav spacing widened (gap 2px→10px, →20px ≥1280, →30px ≥1536) and type scaled up (0.875→0.9375rem base, 1.0625rem ≥1280, 1.125rem ≥1536) with underline offsets kept in sync. (3) Home hero brightened — photo opacity 0.35→0.92 behind a focused legibility scrim (not a flat blackout), grid `.heroAtmosphere` removed, accent word de-whitewashed (pale gradient → solid saturated brand colour with light-token floor), captions crisp via text-shadow; hero photo converted to a fixed-attachment background (parallax, scroll fallback on mobile). (4) Inventory hero (`PageHero`, shared by 8 inner pages) upgraded — shows the photo, killed the glitchy masked grid, better lead contrast; added optional `eyebrow` + hairline `assurances` trust strip that only /used-cars passes (new `usedCarsHeroEyebrow` recipe key, default "Quality Used Cars"); `.inner` switched to a centred flex column (fixes drifting lead), height cut hard (desktop padding 116→76px), lead width-cap removed so copy flows free. All changes tokens-only + brand-agnostic; not yet screenshot-verified against a live fbm brand (no fbm slug loaded locally this session).

- 2026-07-30: dashboard Theme Colors tab — compact redesign + generated swatches now hand-pickable (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `templates/update.html` (Colors tab markup, inline CSS, color-swatch JS).
  - Reason: Difatha review of the update-page Theme Colors tab — "too much text", generated colors weren't editable, wanted a tighter design. The tab was single-primary/always-auto with the 7 derived swatches hard-`auto-locked` (click blocked, no picker), and the verbose two-paragraph section hints had no compact style so they rendered at full body size.
  - Notes: (1) Trimmed both color section hints to one short line each and added a global `.form-section-hint` rule (0.82rem, muted, tightened margins) — improves every tab's density, not just Colors. Shortened swatch labels (`Secondary Color`→`Secondary`, etc.) and tightened `.form-section` h3 spacing. (2) Generated swatches are now pickable: dropped the `auto-locked` early-return in the card click handler, added the hover star overlay markup + inline CSS overrides (brand-form.css suppresses overlay/hover on `.auto-locked`, so inline rules re-enable them since they cascade later). Editing any generated swatch flips auto OFF (`setColorsAutoState(false)`) so the hand-pick sticks; the value is preserved because `setColorsAutoState` only re-derives when turning auto ON. (3) Replaced the static "Auto-generated from your primary" badge with a real auto-tune toggle (mirrors the create page's `colorsAutoToggle` model; toggle CSS inlined). `setColorsAutoState` now takes an `on` param instead of being always-true. (4) `colorsAuto` hidden input + toggle checkbox + `populateForm()` now read the record's `theme.colorsAuto` (missing flag → auto on, preserving prior always-derive behaviour); manual palettes are no longer silently re-derived on load. Verified by rendering update.html with a mock brand + brand-form.css and screenshotting the Colors tab at 1600px — compact hints, toggle on, 7 swatch pills, hover affordance intact.

- 2026-07-30: redgate-lodge-bespoke re-verify QA pass — 2 vehicle-detail cosmetic bugs found + fixed (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/redgate-lodge-bespoke/pages/used-cars/[slug]/GalleryMosaic.tsx`; `app/themes/redgate-lodge-bespoke/pages/used-cars/[slug]/parseDescription.ts`.
  - Reason: Difatha requested a full re-verify after today's vehicle-detail redesign. All 9 Stage-6 gates re-ran green and the full-page verifier (14 routes × both brands) returned 0 blocker/major, but surfaced 2 cosmetic minors on the just-redesigned detail page — both real regressions worth fixing rather than deferring.
  - Notes: (1) Gallery pill rendered `Gallery ()` with empty parens despite 32+ photos. Root cause: `resolveText()` runs `interpolate()`, whose `/\{([a-zA-Z]+)\}/g` blanks EVERY `{token}` it doesn't own — and `{count}` is a component-scoped per-vehicle token, so it was stripped to empty BEFORE `GalleryMosaic` could substitute it; the subsequent `.replace('{count}', …)` then had nothing to match. Fixed by mirroring `PageRibbon`'s existing seed-then-resolve pattern: seed the real photo count into the template first, then let `resolveText` resolve any surrounding brand tokens (`pillOverride || 'Gallery ({count})'` → seed → resolve). `PageRibbon` was the only other `{count}` consumer and already handled it correctly, so this was the sole broken site. (2) "About this car" prose clipped the engine size — "1.6 TDCi" rendered as "6 TDCi", "2.0 TDI" as "0 TDI ." Root cause: `RE_BOILERPLATE`'s `[^.]*\.` clause matcher stopped at the FIRST period, which is the decimal point in `1.6`, eating "1." and leaving "6 TDCi". Fixed by making the clause body decimal-safe — `(?:[^.]|\.(?=\d))*\.` matches non-dots OR a dot that's part of a decimal, so it terminates only on the real sentence-ending period; the leaked dealer-boilerplate opener is now stripped in full and the clipped fragment is gone. Verified: 4-case regex unit test all-pass, tsc exit 0, verifier re-shot both brands — Ford C-Max `Gallery (32)` + Audi A5 `Gallery (36)`, no clipped prose, dealer-name/phone/booking leak still absent, no x-overflow at 505/1440, light brand legible. All 9 gates remain green. Theme fully re-verified — zero outstanding issues.

- 2026-07-30: redgate-lodge-bespoke vehicle-detail REDESIGN — curated listing (highlights/prose parse + masthead + de-duped specs + premium sidebar) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: new `pages/used-cars/[slug]/parseDescription.ts`; `pages/used-cars/[slug]/{DetailClient.tsx, page.module.css}`; `recipes/text-recipe.json` (4 keys: detail.price_label/highlights_title/about_title/spec_title); design spec `tools/.theme-designs/redgate-lodge-bespoke/components/13-vehicle-detail.spec.md` + brief `fixes/vehicle-detail-redesign.md`.
  - Reason: Difatha review — the detail page "looks so poor". Root cause: the description was `vehicle.description.split('\n')` rendered as raw `<p>` lines, so the page dumped the raw feed blurb — leaking the dealer's name, a person's name ("call Abe"), a phone number and payment/booking instructions — plus the big Specification table just repeated the specs band, empty specs showed "—", and the title/price row was cramped.
  - Notes: `parseVehicleDescription()` classifies each fragment as highlight / prose / noise. Splits on newlines, bullets, pipes, " / " AND "+" (the real feed delimits features with " + ", e.g. "... + GREAT SERVICE HISTORY + HUGE SPEC + CLIMATE CONTROL + ..."). NOISE (dropped): phone numbers, call/ring/text/email, payment/bank-transfer, book/test-drive/appointment, "please…", bare price/reg, make/model identity repeats, ≤2-word spec tokens, and the leading "<Dealer> are pleased to offer…" boilerplate clause (strips the dealer-name leak while keeping the genuine sentence after it). HIGHLIGHTS: ≤6 words/≤48 chars, ALL-CAPS tidied to Title Case ("GREAT SERVICE HISTORY"→"Great Service History"), deduped, cap 8 → a hairline tick-checklist (2-col ≥768). PROSE → "About this car" block. Also: title masthead (title/subline left, right-aligned Price eyebrow/serif price/finance unit on a hairline); specs band filters empty facts (no "—"/blank ENGINE) with per-cell inset-box-shadow hairlines so a dropped cell can't leave a hanging block; Specification grid shows only non-band identity fields (Make/Model/Derivative/Colour/Doors/Registration), empties hidden; premium sidebar (claret ribbon rule, eyebrow-led price, hairline before the action group). Furnishing preserved (specs-band aurora, gallery ken-burns + mfx-spotlight, sidebar shimmer, data-aos reveals re-attached to the new blocks). Verified live on the Mercedes GLA: highlights = Great Service History / Huge Spec / Climate Control / Parking Sensors / Reverse Camera / …; prose = "Looks, drives great very well maintained for it's age"; dealer name + phone + booking gone. Gates green (tsc, theme-contract 1/1, color-contract 0, image-contract 0, audit 0 blockers); mobile-390 no overflow. Minor: acronyms lowercase in Title Case ("HPI"→"Hpi", "VGC"→"Vgc") — cosmetic, can add an acronym allowlist if wanted.

Submit CTA hover left as its existing single background-color darken (already the one refined token effect, coherent with the hero primaryCta). Guards: CanvasFX self-hides ≤640 + reduced-motion (static token wash) + pauses off-screen so no `*-decor-mobile-hide` needed; AOS auto-off ≤720 (shared driver) + single-column-per-row mobile layout keep mobile calm; reg-glow is a static focus indicator (no loop); LedgerSteps + regInput reduced-motion blocks in place. - 2026-07-30: /theme-builder furnishing conveyor belt — rich, archetype-gated, device-verified components (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: new `theme-furnisher` subagent (`.claude/agents/theme-furnisher.md`) inserted into Stage 4 as build→**furnish**→verify→fix; new shared `app/widgets/CanvasFX/{CanvasFX.tsx,drawings.ts,canvas-fx.css}` (guarded canvas backdrop — particle-drift/aurora-light/vector-grid); implemented the documented-but-missing `.mfx-spotlight` (cursor-reactive) in `app/widgets/MotionFX/styles.css` + a pointer tracker in `AnimateOnScroll.tsx`; `tools/theme-shots.mjs` gained real CDP device emulation (`--device iphone13,androids` @2x/@3x touch + full-page, `--reduced-motion`, x-overflow probe) via puppeteer-core; spec-format gained a per-component **## Furnishing** block + required Furnishing AC; restraint retuned from "governing constraint" to a quality FLOOR ("rich by default") in `build-rules.md` §14 (+ archetype→vocabulary table) and `new-theme/SKILL.md`; verifier gained a furnishing judgement + real small-device pass; design-director authors the Furnishing spec (archetype-gated) + furnish-fixes. Reference proof: redgate `Hero` furnished (aurora-light canvas + ken-burns + mfx-spotlight + hover-depth).
  - Reason: Difatha — components were shipping structurally-correct but visually PLAIN; wanted every component to pass a furnishing conveyor belt (imagery, entrance/scroll animation, neon/light, canvas drawings, micro-interactions) and be verified on real small devices. Archetype-gated so modern/tech get full neon/canvas while luxury/editorial (redgate) get rich+refined, not tacky neon; minimalist stays restrained.
  - Notes: all runtime is token-driven + self-guarding — CanvasFX no-ops to a static token wash under reduced-motion or ≤640px, pauses off-screen + tab-hidden, caps DPR at 2; MotionFX/AOS already freeze ≤720px + reduced-motion. Proven via puppeteer probe: desktop (motion on) = aurora canvas LIVE + painting + ken-burns running; reduced-motion = fully static; mobile 390 (even motion-on) = static wash, no ken-burns — mobile stays calm. Discovered + fixed: headless Chrome defaults to `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` (froze all furnishing in screenshots) — theme-shots device mode now forces `no-preference` by default so the rich state renders, `--reduced-motion` is the contrast. All contracts green after furnishing (tsc, theme-contract 1/1, color-contract 0, image-contract 0, audit 0 blockers). Agents register at session start — restart before the next /theme-builder run to pick up the furnisher. Follow-up: furnish the other redgate components (belt does this automatically on the next full run); add `.mfx-spotlight` to the MotionFX doc's "implemented" list (now real).

- 2026-07-30: Image contract — declarative, dashboard-driven theme images (system + redgate reference) (owner: Difatha)

New `tools/check-image-contract.mjs` + `npm run check:images` (hard gate). `tools/fetch-theme-images.mjs` reads slots from the manifest. `/theme-builder` build-rules §12 + Stage-6 gate updated.
  - Reason: Difatha — make every theme image contractual: declared once in the theme, auto-surfaced as a dashboard upload control, nothing static, enforced. Mirrors the proven `text-recipe.json` contract. Kills the ~8-place copy-pasted 7-slot list and the live drift (`brand.images.location` had no dashboard control).
  - Notes: one manifest slot now drives BrandStyles var + dashboard control + backend save + prospect sourcing + the gate. Verified end-to-end through the running app: adding a `financeAssurance` slot to the manifest made `--brand-image-finance-assurance` appear in the rendered HTML AND `load_theme_image_slots` surface it for the dashboard — zero code changes — then reverted. Backend returns redgate's 8 slots / legacy themes' 7 (shim); `check-image-contract` caught a real literal (annotated the inventory `/images/image.png` no-photo placeholder `// image-static-ok`) and passes clean; tsc + theme-contract + color-contract green; theme renders identically. NOT auto-verified (needs Difatha's authed dashboard login, external Carous API): the pixel-level Page Images tab render + a live upload persisting. Follow-ups: migrate the other 13 themes off the legacy shim (codemod); teach `tools/scaffold-theme-skeleton.mjs` + the /theme-builder design-director to emit a starter `image-recipe.json` + generic BrandStyles so new themes are born compliant.

- 2026-07-30: Dashboard Page Images tab is now data-driven from the per-theme image manifest (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `templates/update.html`, `templates/index.html` (Page Images tab markup + inline JS only; no backend touched).
  - Reason: The Page Images tab hardcoded a fixed 6-slot Jinja `page_image_slots` list, so a theme's `recipes/image-recipe.json` (already read by the backend save handlers + `GET /api/themes/<id>/image-recipe`) had no effect on which upload cards the dashboard showed. Now the tab mirrors the Component Text tab: it fetches the theme's image manifest and renders one `.page-image-card` per non-hero slot, matching the field-name contract (`{key}ImageFile`, `image{Key}Url`) the save handlers expect.
  - Notes: Replaced the `{% set page_image_slots %}` + `{% for %}` block with an empty `<div data-page-image-host data-page-images-existing='…'>`; added `initializePageImageTab()` (clones `initializeComponentTextTab`) that reads `#themeId`, filters `role:"hero"` out (the hero stays its own brand-asset card), renders the cards as a JS template string, then calls the existing DOM-driven `initializePageImagePicker()` to bind upload/URL/preview/reset/remove. Wired into the init sequence and re-runs on `#themeId` change. Logo/favicon/hero brand-asset cards untouched. `node --check` passes on the injected function in both files; no `page_image_slots` references remain.

- 2026-07-30: Fix dashboard 500 (`'None' has no attribute 'username'`) for unauthenticated visitors (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `templates/base.html` (header user-profile block).
  - Reason: The `/dashboard`, `/templates`, `/settings` routes have their `@auth_manager.login_required` commented out ("to fix redirect loop"), so an anonymous request reaches the template with `current_user = None` (set by `load_user` in `before_request`). The header unconditionally dereferenced `(current_user.name or current_user.username)[0]`, which raised `UndefinedError` on `None` and 500'd every dashboard hit while logged out.
  - Notes: Wrapped the user-profile block in `{% if current_user %}` with a `display_name = name or username or email or 'User'` fallback; the else branch renders just a Sign Out button (→ `auth.logout`), no guest state. Verified both render paths (None + logged-in dict) compile clean. Deeper issue — the disabled auth guard on the dashboard — left as-is since it was an intentional workaround; this only stops the crash.

- 2026-07-30: redgate-lodge-bespoke — Lenis smooth scroll + BrowseByMake CDN marque logos + proof-ledger column-padding fix (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: new `components/SmoothScroll.tsx` + `SmoothScroll.css` (canonical `<ReactLenis root>` pattern, mirrors cnhcars/classic-dealer/fbm/axis) mounted once in `shell.tsx`; `components/BrowseByMake.tsx` + `.module.css` (letter tiles → real marque logos via a plain `<img>` from the simple-icons jsDelivr CDN with make→slug normalisation + initial-letter `onError` fallback); `components/ProofLedger.module.css` (fixed the 1024 four-column layout). Pipeline hardening: `.claude/skills/theme-builder/references/build-rules.md` §3 (min-width breakpoints stack — re-assert nth-child resets at wider breakpoints), §12 (BrowseByMake uses CDN marque logos, plain img not css-mask).
  - Reason: Difatha review. (1) Wanted Lenis smooth scroll (platform-standard, already a dep). (2) BrowseByMake showed single-letter tiles — wanted real logos; used a plain `<img>` NOT a CSS mask because cross-origin simple-icons SVGs paint invisible through `mask-image` (the latent bug in the other themes' MakeLogo — see memory feedback_css_mask_cross_origin_svg). (3) The proof-ledger's 3rd column crammed against its divider: the 768 2×2 block's `nth-child` padding resets (specificity 0,2,0) leaked into the ≥1024 four-col layout and out-ranked the bare `.entry{padding:0 2rem}` (0,1,0) — re-asserted the per-column paddings/borders at 1024 at matching specificity.
  - Notes: gates green (tsc, contract 1/1, color-contract 0, audit 0 blockers). Verified: `html.lenis` class present + active; marque logos paint on the claret band (Audi/BMW/Ford/Mercedes/Skoda/… ; Lexus etc. fall back to the letter, all 12 CDN slugs 200); proof-ledger columns now evenly padded with no stray row hairlines. All three lessons baked into build-rules + memory `feedback_detail_gutter_and_inventory_hero` (earlier) so the class of bug can't recur.

- 2026-07-30: `/theme-builder` — fewer permission prompts + compaction checkpoints for long runs (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `.claude/settings.local.json` (added a scoped `permissions.allow` block for the pipeline's deterministic commands — `node tools/check-*`/`audit-theme`/`theme-shots`/`create-preview`/`extract-logo-colors`, `npm run theme:sync`/`dev`, `npx tsc`, `python tools/build-preview-from-theme.py`/`app.py`); `.claude/skills/theme-builder/SKILL.md` (new "Context hygiene (compaction checkpoints)" subsection + a required self-sufficient `digest` block in `state.json`).
  - Reason: a full `/theme-builder` run fired a permission prompt on every gate/build command, and the orchestrator's context grew unbounded across a long multi-component run. Pre-allowing the fixed command set removes ~90% of the prompts; the checkpoint rule makes the harness's mid-run auto-compaction safe by guaranteeing everything needed to resume lives in `state.json` on disk.
  - Notes: per-dealer dynamic prompts (WebFetch to the dealer's own domain, one-off logo URLs) are intentionally NOT wildcarded — for a fully hands-off run flip the session into accept/bypass mode (Shift+Tab) for that run. A "compaction subagent" was explicitly ruled out: a subagent has its own separate context and cannot shrink the orchestrator's — keeping the main thread lean is done via `digest` + disk, documented in the skill.

- 2026-07-29: `/theme-builder` — new multi-agent theme worker (design-director → scaffolder → per-component build/verify/fix loop → integrator) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: new `.claude/agents/theme-design-director.md`, `theme-scaffolder.md`, `theme-component-builder.md`, `theme-verifier.md`, `theme-integrator.md`; new `.claude/skills/theme-builder/SKILL.md` + `references/build-rules.md` (digest of the durable design rules) + `references/spec-format.md` (design-package / verdict / fix schemas); new `tools/theme-shots.mjs` (multi-width headless-Chrome screenshot primitive with route unmangling + server/route probes). `/new-theme` and all `tools/check-*.mjs` gates reused untouched.
  - Reason: `/new-theme` designs and builds in one context with no independent design authority, no per-component verification, and no visual check before ship — design/spec issues were being found by Difatha instead of the pipeline. The new worker gives each theme a researched, concept-locked design package (per-component specs with adjacency contracts + checkable acceptance criteria), then loops builder → screenshot-verifier → designer-fix (max 3 cycles, then escalate with evidence) per component until specs pass at 505/768/1024/1440, verifies full pages against BOTH the real brand and a light-palette brand, and only ships on a fully green gate suite.
  - Notes: agents register at session start — restart Claude Code before the first `/theme-builder` run. `theme-shots.mjs` smoke-tested end-to-end (Flask + next dev + lvh.me host-resolved brand `lighttest` → 4/4 captures; needs `PYTHONUTF8=1` when backgrounding `app.py`, else an emoji print crashes Flask under cp1252). Design-director role smoke-tested with a fake brief: concept locked non-colliding, uniqueness gate exit 0; its 7 feedback items (gate scripts need `--id`, coined-signature-move ownership, `primary-tint` recipe, minors-aggregation, topbar menu carve-out) were folded back into the agent/reference files. Gilded-drive's stale fingerprint-schema comment noted but not touched.

- 2026-07-28: cnhcars-clone — fixed invisible brand marquee (cross-theme CSS leak) + made top contact bar non-sticky (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/gilded-drive/styles/futuristic-homepage.css` (scoped `.brand-logo`), `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/styles/contact-bar.css` (position).
  - Reason: the cnhcars homepage brand-logo marquee rendered as empty white boxes — logos loaded but were painted invisible. Root cause was cross-theme CSS leakage, NOT a cnhcars bug: gilded-drive's `futuristic-homepage.css` declared a BARE, unscoped `.brand-logo { filter: brightness(0) invert(1) }` (its futuristic strip inverts monochrome marque SVGs to white on a dark band). `.brand-logo` is a class name cnhcars-clone also uses, and both themes' CSS land in the same dev/preview bundle, so the `filter` cascaded onto cnhcars's logos and turned the whole box white-on-white. Scoped the rule (and its `:hover` companion's base) to `.brand-item-futuristic .brand-logo` so it can only affect gilded-drive's own markup. Separately, per request, `.contact-bar` was changed from `position: sticky` (pinned under the preview banner) to `position: relative` so the top contact bar scrolls away with the page instead of sticking.
  - Notes: verified with a headless-Chrome render of the live `columbus-vehicles-preview` brand (themeId cnhcars-clone) — all eight marque logos (Audi/Toyota/Honda/VW/Ford/Nissan/Hyundai/Kia) now paint in the marquee, and the header/hero layout is unchanged. gilded-drive remains the repo's one un-migrated theme carrying literal-colour + unscoped selectors (see color-contract migration follow-ups) — worth a wider scoping sweep. Confirms the standing lesson: grep a class across ALL themes before trusting a per-theme render.

- 2026-07-28: buy4lessuk-bespoke — purged foreign (non-palette) hues; all decorative tokens now derive from the brand palette (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/buy4lessuk-bespoke/context/BrandStyles.tsx` (extras token block), `components/ValuationForm.module.css` (reg-plate GB flag).
  - Reason: the theme's `extras` block defined a wall of hardcoded literal hues — deep-navy display bands (`#10294f`/`#0b1d3a`/`#081630`…), light-blue hero-fallback gradient, UK-plate yellow (`#ffd400`/`#fbd826`), a blue/orange/green/sky body-type category rainbow, and a teal (`#25e6c5`) vehicle-detail slug accent — with a comment declaring them "intentionally NOT part of the 8-token brand palette." Because `BrandStyles.tsx` is exempt from `check-color-contract.mjs`, these evaded the gate, so ANY brand (red, green, etc.) still rendered navy bands, a yellow reg-plate and a rainbow of category icons — the exact loophole the palette contract forbids. Every foreign token is now DERIVED from the core-8 palette via `color-mix`: dark bands = brand-tinted dark (`color-mix(--color-primary X%, --color-text)`, ascending 10→38%); hero fallback = light brand tints over `--color-bg`; reg-plate = accent-driven (`--b4l-btn-yellow` a light `--color-accent` panel, `--b4l-plate-yellow`→`--color-accent`, GB flag→solid `--color-primary` + `--color-on-dark-strong` text); body-type categories = four distinguishable variants within the primary↔accent range (secondary is never a foreground, per the theme color policy); slug accents→`--color-primary`. The extended on-dark chrome tokens (`--color-surface-dark`, `--color-topbar-bg`, `--color-text-invert`, `--color-border-dark`, topbar/on-dark muted) were likewise repointed from fixed navy/bluish-rgba literals to `color-mix` over primary/text/bg. Each token still honours a dashboard override (`theme.colors.*`) first.
  - Notes: consumers were untouched — every page already read the tokens via `var(--b4l-*)`/`var(--color-*)`, so fixing the definitions recolours the whole theme (grep confirmed BrandStyles.tsx was the ONLY file carrying colour literals). For the seed brand the derivations reproduce the original navy/blue look almost exactly. Verified with a headless-Chrome render against a throwaway GREEN-primary/amber-accent brand (`themeId=buy4lessuk-bespoke`, host-resolver pipeline): home, used-cars, vehicle-detail and part-exchange all recolour fully green with zero navy/yellow/teal leakage — bands, spec-icon chips, hero gradients and the reg-plate all follow the palette. `check-color-contract.mjs --id buy4lessuk-bespoke` = 0 literals.

- 2026-07-28: repo-wide — removed homepage-hero theme-default fallback image + swapped `next/image` for plain `<img>` across all themes (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `context/BrandStyles.tsx` + homepage-hero CSS across auto-wow-uk-bespoke, auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02, axis-autos-bespoke, kain-motors-bespoke, queensbury-cars-bespoke, showroom-shine-cars-bespoke, dual-stock-modern-bespoke, cnhcars-clone, buy4lessuk-bespoke, classic-dealer, gilded-drive, fbm-motors, springalls-classic; plus `<Image>`→`<img>` conversions in dual-stock (Header/Footer/VehicleCard), classic-dealer (Services), gilded-drive (Hero), fbm-motors (Header/Footer + home hero), springalls-classic (Header/Footer). warwick-hall-cars-bespoke already shipped this pattern (used as the reference); `fbm` is an empty scaffold (no-op).
  - Reason: dealer previews with no uploaded hero were rendering the theme's curated stock photo (`/themes/<id>/images/hero.jpg`, or an Unsplash/logo/placeholder fallback) as the homepage hero, which reads as generic and off-brand on a prospect preview. Now the homepage hero shows ONLY the brand's own uploaded hero; when absent it degrades to the theme gradient + brand-colour panel + dark scrim (the warwick pattern). Implemented as full removal at BOTH layers: (1) BrandStyles emits the hero background var (`--brand-image-hero` + each theme's legacy `--<id>-hero-image` alias) as the literal `'none'` when neither `brand.heroImage` nor `brand.images.hero` is set — `optimizeImageUrl` is only called on a truthy source; (2) the homepage-hero CSS drops the second `url('/themes/<id>/images/hero.jpg')` background layer and keeps only `var(--brand-image-hero, none)` over a brand `background-color`. Separately, all remaining `next/image` `<Image>` usages were converted to native `<img>` (drop next-only props like `fill`/`priority`; `fill` replaced with an inline `position:absolute;inset:0;object-fit:cover` style where used).
  - Notes: strictly scoped to the HOMEPAGE hero — inner-page heroes (about/inventory/contact/sell-your-car) and per-page section images (about/services/finance/partExchange/sellYourCar/recentlySold) keep their theme defaults intentionally. Per-theme nuances handled: cnhcars BrandStyles already emitted `'none'` (only the CSS layer needed removing); classic-dealer OMITS the shared `--classic-hero-image` var rather than emitting `'none'` so about/contact keep their designed brand-gradient fallback; gilded-drive's futuristic hero baked `brand.logo`/`/favicon.svg` via `::before` — repointed to a new brand-only `--gilded-home-hero-image`; fbm-motors renders its hero as a conditional `<img>` in `pages/home/page.tsx` (dropped the `defaultHero` fallback) and its sell-your-car page was repointed off `--brand-image-hero` (CSS var fallback only fires on *undefined*, not the `none` token). Verified: zero `from 'next/image'` / `<Image>` remain in any theme; tsc clean on all changed files; no homepage hero CSS references its theme-default hero image.

- 2026-07-28: dashboard Colors tab — single-primary palette generation (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `backend/services/color_derive.py`, `static/modules/color-utils.js`, `static/modules/form_handler.py`, `templates/update.html` (Colors tab markup + JS + CSS).
  - Reason: the Colors tab asked dealers to hand-pick 4 brand colors (primary/secondary/accent/background); now they pick ONLY the primary and the entire palette is generated with colour-theory + WCAG rules — no manual intervention. New engine entry point `derive_from_primary()` (mirrored in JS as `ColorUtils.deriveFromPrimary()`, byte-for-byte identical — verified with a node/python parity harness across 6 hues): Background = primary at 3% into white (stays light so text derives dark); Secondary = deep monochromatic tone of the primary hue (sat×0.55 clamp 16–42, light×0.42 clamp 20–32); Accent = split-complementary (+150°, sat clamp 62–90) with lightness walked 56→24 until it clears 3:1 on the bg; Text/Surface/Border/Muted keep the existing 4-input WCAG derivation. Added HSL helpers to the Python engine that exactly mirror the JS `hexToHsl`/`hslToHex` (same integer rounding). `resolve_colors()` gained a `from_primary` mode (takes precedence over `auto`) that regenerates the whole palette from `primaryColor` alone; `form_handler` routes the dashboard save through it via the existing `colorsAuto` flag (now hardcoded `true` in the tab). UI: section 1 is a single editable Primary swatch; section 2 "Generated palette" shows the other 7 as AUTO-locked read-only swatches that regenerate live (`applyGeneratedPalette` on primary change + on load); the old auto-tune toggle + its CSS were removed and replaced with a static "Auto-generated from your primary" badge.
  - Follow-up (same day): compacted the Colors-tab layout. Swatch cards went from tall stacked blocks (80px preview + full-width) to a horizontal row: 42px colour chip + name/copy on line 1, hex chip + ellipsized description on line 2 (new `.swatch-meta` flex wrapper). Grid is now `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(210px,1fr))` (denser, self-reflowing — dropped the fixed 4/3/2-col breakpoints), gaps/padding tightened, card hover lift removed. Redundant per-card "AUTO" badges hidden (the section's "Auto-generated from your primary" badge already says it). Whole palette + Theme Fonts now fit one screen.
  - Notes: the `auto=True` (AI draft) and preview-create endpoints are untouched — they legitimately pass a full 4-colour palette and still use the non-destructive/4-input path, so `/new-theme` drafts keep their hand-picked secondary/accent. `from_primary` intentionally overrides any stored secondary/accent/background on the next dashboard save. Verified end-to-end: engine parity (JS==Python), 4-input + non-destructive paths unchanged, Jinja compiles, and a headless-Chrome render of the live tab against a1carshoplimited (primary #f2a900) regenerated Secondary #483c1e / Accent #0a9dc2 / Background #fffcf7 / Text #212220 and fired the correct "primary 1.96:1 on bg (below 3:1)" contrast advisory.

- 2026-07-28: Lenis smooth scroll added to Auto Wow theme (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/SmoothScroll.tsx` (new), `SmoothScroll.css` (new), `components/Shell.tsx`.
  - Reason: Auto Wow was the only recently-built theme missing site-wide Lenis smooth scrolling that cnhcars-clone / classic-dealer / fbm-motors / axis-autos / buy4less already ship. Added the same `<ReactLenis root>` wrapper (identical default options) plus the companion override sheet that forces `scroll-behavior:auto` and `height:auto` on `html.lenis` (native smooth-scroll and pinned body height fight Lenis's RAF-driven scrollTop). Mounted once in `AutoShell` above `<ThemeChrome>` inside a fragment, so it only activates when Auto Wow is the live brand.
  - Notes: mirrors the existing SmoothScroll pattern verbatim; tsc clean.

- 2026-07-28: mobile header layout fix — action cluster stranded in mid-row on 4 themes (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/Header.module.css`, `dual-stock-modern-bespoke/components/Header.module.css`, `cnhcars-clone/styles/header.css`, `warwick-hall-cars-bespoke/components/Header.module.css`.
  - Reason: on small screens the header actions (search/phone/hamburger) bunched in the middle of the row with a dead gap on the right instead of hugging the right edge (reported on autowow / Columbus Vehicles). Root cause is one pattern: the header row is a fixed 3-col CSS grid `auto 1fr auto` (logo · nav · actions), and the middle nav is `display:none` on mobile — which removes it from the grid entirely, so the two remaining items auto-place into cols 1 + 2 and the actions land in the middle 1fr track, leaving col 3 empty. Fix: collapse the grid to two real tracks (`1fr auto` + `justify-self:end` on the action cluster) at the SAME breakpoint where the nav hides. auto-wow-02 collapse added at ≤1080; dual-stock's collapse existed only at ≤640 but its nav hides <1024, so the 641–1023 band was broken → collapse now ≤1023; cnhcars kept the buggy 3-col template at ≤768 (a stale comment claimed the hidden nav was a "zero-width spacer" — it isn't, it's `display:none`) → collapse ≤768. warwick is flex, not grid, but had the same class of bug: it right-aligned the cluster purely via `.nav { flex:1 }`, so hiding the nav at ≤860 collapsed the cluster against the logo → added `.rightCluster { margin-left:auto }` in the ≤860 block.
  - Notes: audited all 14 theme headers. Safe (no change): auto-wow-uk-bespoke (explicit `grid-column` placement), classic-dealer (already collapses to `1fr auto` ≤768), fbm/axis/buy4less/gilded-drive (flex `space-between`), showroom (`.mainBarActions margin-left:auto`), springalls (≤960 `space-between` + `margin-left:auto`), kain (`.actionRail margin-left:auto`), queensbury (`.brand margin-right:auto`). All fixes are mobile-only media-query edits — desktop untouched; tsc clean. Verified the canonical -02 case with a before/after static replica at 400px (headless Chrome clamps to ~500px min so ≤480 states can't be shot live). NB: the local dev server had a stale brand→theme cache — it rendered Columbus as `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` while the preview API maps it to `-02`; a dev-server restart re-syncs it.

- 2026-07-28: modern cookie banner redesign — shared widget restyled, fixes all 14 themes at once (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/widgets/CookieBanner/CookieBanner.tsx` + `CookieBanner.module.css`. All 14 themes route through this one widget via `app/themes/lib/ThemeChrome.tsx`, so no per-theme edits were needed.
  - Reason: the banner rendered as a bland white max-width:1200px centered card that clashed on dark/gold brands (e.g. Plumtree) and looked like content, not chrome. Rebuilt to the documented QA-preferred pattern (slim full-width bottom strip): dark glassy surface (`color-mix(#0b0f18 90%)` + `backdrop-filter: blur/saturate`) that reads as chrome on any palette, a brand-accent top hairline, a brand-tinted cookie-icon chip, a bold inline "Cookies" title + single-line summary + policy link, and a right-aligned action row (Settings / Decline / Accept all). Primary CTA now uses `var(--color-on-primary)` so the label stays readable on both light-primary (gold → dark text) and dark-primary (blue → white text) brands. The settings side-panel was recolored to a cohesive dark glass surface (was white) so its "Reject all" secondary button — which shares the now-dark button styling — stays visible; toggles/accents use `--color-primary`.
  - Notes: banner slides up from the bottom edge (`transform: translateY(100%)→0`), respects `prefers-reduced-motion`, and adds `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` padding on mobile (≤720px stacks actions full-width, ≤420px drops the icon). Default copy shortened to "Cookies" / "We use cookies to keep the site running and improve your visit." Verified visually via headless Chrome against both a dark-gold and a light-blue brand palette. Per-theme `cookie={{ title, summary }}` voice in each Shell is untouched.

- 2026-07-27: repo-wide theme performance pass — code-split theme registries, image optimization, font preconnect (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `tools/sync-theme-contracts.mjs` (shell + page registry generators) + regenerated `app/themes/generated/theme-{shell,page}-registry.generated.ts`, `app/themes/theme-pages.server.ts`, `app/themes/page-runtime.server.tsx`, `app/lib/imageOptimize.ts` (new), `next.config.js`, `app/layout.tsx`, and every theme's card component + `context/BrandStyles.tsx` across all 14 themes.
  - Reason: autowow Lighthouse was FCP 7.7s / LCP 17.5s / 11.3MB payload (6.4MB images) / 4270ms render-blocking / 174KiB unused CSS / 437KiB unused JS. Three systemic root causes fixed:
    1. **Code-split theme registries.** `theme-shell-registry` + `theme-page-registry` statically imported all 14 themes, so every page shipped all 14 shells' CSS (~402KiB) + page components + framer-motion from other themes. Shell registry now emits `next/dynamic(() => import('../<id>/shell'))` per theme (default SSR — header/footer stay server-rendered, no LCP flash); page registry now emits lazy `() => import('../<id>/pages')` loaders awaited server-side in `getThemePageForBrand` (made async; `renderThemePage` awaits it). Verified: each theme's code now sits in its own 2-6 client chunks, not one shared monolith.
    2. **Image optimization.** New `app/lib/imageOptimize.ts#optimizeImageUrl()` routes card `<img>`/CSS-background URLs + BrandStyles raster hero/section CSS vars through Next's `/_next/image` optimizer (WebP/AVIF + resize). Safe by default — passes data-URIs, SVGs, and non-allowlisted hosts through untouched (never 400s a feed image). Applied to the card renderer + `BrandStyles.tsx` of all 14 themes: card images request width 640 (+ explicit `width`/`height` on bare `<img>`s to cut CLS — 640×480 for 4:3, 640×400 for 16:10), hero CSS vars 1920 (LCP), section image vars 1280. Removed cnhcars' blanket per-card `fetchPriority="high"` (defeated lazy-loading). `next.config.js`: added `formats:['image/avif','image/webp']`, `qualities:[60,70,75,80]`, `minimumCacheTTL:86400`.
    3. **Font preconnect.** `app/layout.tsx` now emits `<link rel="preconnect">` to fonts.googleapis.com + fonts.gstatic.com (React 19 hoists to `<head>`), warming the font connection off the critical path for every theme.
  - Notes: earlier build "failure" was a leaked 2.1GB `next dev` worker corrupting `.next/dev/types` mid-build (the known worker-leak) — `rm -rf .next && npm run build` = exit 0, tsc clean repo-wide. Deferred follow-ups: (a) convert render-blocking font `@import` (9 BrandStyles inline-style + 6 base.css) to hoisted `<link>`; (b) split the context registry (BrandStyles/BrandClientWrapper/AuthProvider/DynamicFavicon still static-import all 14 — lightest theme code, FOUC/hydration risk outweighs marginal KB); (c) autowow homepage section thumbnails (LatestArrivals/RecentlySold/FeaturedStock/ticker) not yet optimized. Detail-page galleries and logo/`next/image` components intentionally out of scope.

  - Scope: `components/{HeroSmall,FilterBar,VehicleCard}.tsx`, `styles/{hero-small,cars-filter,vehicle-card,inventory-bg,hero-modern}.css`.
  - Reason: /used-cars looked unstyled — bare text filters with a hardcoded US-market model list, washed borderless cards, and a gradient inventory band. Rebuilt: (1) hero → `cnh-inv-hero`, brand `--brand-image-hero` under a uniform flat dark scrim with eyebrow chip/title/lead (lead hides ≤480px); (2) filters → `cnh-flt` toolbar: title + live count chip row with Reset (shows active-filter count) and a mobile-only Filters toggle (collapsed by default), labeled field shells (border + focus ring on the shell, borderless control inside), real year range 2008→current, Model as a free-text input (passes the existing `model` API param) instead of the fake hardcoded select; (3) cards → shadow tiers + 14px radius, wishlist heart is now a transparent 22px glyph over the photo (no white circle), flat price band, colour chip hides when the feed has no colour (was an ellipsised "Colour on reque…"), grid gap 1.25rem. Home hero "Search used cars" button also went gradient→flat `--color-primary`/`--color-on-primary` (the accent gradient tail washed out the label).

Verified desktop + mobile-breakpoint via headless Chrome on `columbus-vehicles-preview`; tsc clean, check-color-contract 0.

  - Scope: `styles/used.css` (129→0 raw literals) and `styles/snackbar.css` (9→0). A prior entry/agent wrongly assumed these two files were already clean; check-color-contract actually flagged 138 literals across them (the dark inventory listing page + toast/snackbar + the light `#sellResult` box).

Routed each literal by role: white-on-dark text/glass/border (rgba(255,255,255,α)) → `color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-on-dark-strong) N%, transparent)` at the exact alpha; dark card/band/scrim fills (rgba(6,8,10), rgba(12,14,16), rgba(10,10,12), rgba(2,6,23) loading overlay) → `color-mix()` over `--surface-bg-dark`/`--surface-card-dark`; gold `#d1b57a` → `--color-primary`; text/glyphs on gold/accent fills (`#060606`, `#fff`, `#ffffff` on `.btn-cta-primary`/`.icon-btn`/`.page-btn.active`/`+N img-count`) → `--color-on-primary`; light buttons (`rgba(255,255,255,.98)`/cream gradients) → `--color-surface` + `--surface-bg-light`; dark text on light buttons (`#071018`) → `--text-on-light-strong`; the `.used-page` light var block (`#f6f7f9`/`#ffffff`/`#fbfbfd`/`#0b1720`/`#6b7280`) → `--surface-bg-light`/`--surface-card-light`/`--color-surface`/`--text-on-light-strong`/`--color-muted`; navy/black tints (rgba(16,24,32,α), `#1018200f`, rgba(0,0,0,α), rgba(6,8,10,.06) border) → `color-mix()` over `--color-text`; stock badges `#dc2626`→`--t-error`, `#ea580c`→`color-mix(--t-error 55%, --color-accent)`, `#ca8a04`→`--color-accent`, `#6b7280`→`--color-muted`; content modal backdrop (rgba(2,6,23) gradient) → `color-mix()` over `--color-text`; blue focus ring rgba(8,112,255) → `color-mix()` over `--color-accent`. snackbar.css: dark toast `#222`/`#2b2b2b` → `--surface-bg-dark`/`--surface-card-dark`, success greens → `--t-success`, error reds → `--t-error`, `#sellResult` light box → `--surface-bg-light`/`--border-on-light`/`--text-on-light-strong`.
  - Notes: only genuine dark photo scrim kept literal + `/* photo-scrim-ok */` (the `.slide-overlay-tertiary` rgba(0,0,0,.1) darken over the hero carousel image, matching its already-annotated siblings). Box-shadow/text-shadow rgba left untouched (exempt). Applied via a verified line-scoped replacement pass (124 edits on used.css, each asserted against its exact line). check-color-contract now reports 0 for both files; check-theme-contract gilded-drive PASS; substring-corruption grep clean.

- 2026-07-22: axis-autos-bespoke (Axis Autos) — livelier homepage: button micro-motion + staggered scroll reveals (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `styles/base.css`, `components/Header.module.css`, `components/{FeaturedStock,RecentlySoldPreview,ServiceHighlights,Directory}.tsx`
  - Reason: Difatha asked to make the homepage more lively with animations and interactive button animations. Two levers. (1) **Shared `.axis-btn` micro-motion** — added hover lift (translateY -2px + soft shadow), active press (settles to 0, faster), and a trailing-arrow nudge (`svg:last-child` slides +3px) to the base button primitive, so EVERY button across the homepage/site (Hero, CTABand, Header CTA, mobile) animates from one place with no per-component JS; leading phone glyphs (`svg:first-child`) deliberately don't slide. Removed the now-duplicate transform/glow from Header `.cta:hover` so the primitive is the single source of truth (pill radius stays). (2) **Staggered scroll cascades** — the card-grid sections (Latest arrivals, Recently sold, Services, Find us) moved from one section-level `data-aos` to a header reveal + per-card `fade-up` with incremental `data-aos-delay` (90ms stock/sold, 80ms services, 0/90/180 directory) so cards cascade in on scroll instead of the whole block popping at once.
  - Notes: uses the existing `AnimateOnScroll` driver (already mounted via ThemeChrome; disables reveals ≤720px per mobile-modest rule) — no new deps. All shadows are token-based `color-mix` (no literals); every micro-motion has a `prefers-reduced-motion` kill. contract PASS (14/14), color-contract clean (no new literals), tsc clean.

Interpreted "futuristic" within the theme's locked minimalist fingerprint (zero decorative layers — no glows/grids/shimmer): expressed it as technical precision + motion, not neon. **EditorialIntro**: the three statement bullets (Sourcing/Pricing/Finance) became interactive hairline spec-cards — glyph pill icon (lucide Search/BadgePoundSterling/Landmark), kicker, title, body, and a hover reveal (a brand edge bar that scales from a sliver to full height, a lift + elevated shadow, an arrow that slides in). Left column gained a vertical brand hairline thread down its edge. **Header**: added a visible **Home** link, redesigned nav links with a text-hugging animated underline (scaleX from left) that doubles as the active indicator, refined the icon actions into rounded brand-soft hover pills with a hairline divider before the CTA, made the phone CTA a pill with a lucide Phone icon + hover lift/glow, and gave the mobile nav links a leading brand marker on active.

(2) **modern polish** — converted the services page's 1px-gap wireframe card grid to the same editorial index as the homepage; rounded all squared brand-soft icon chips to the shared `.axis-icon` pill (services/about/finance/part-exchange); kept the sell-your-car step numbers (a genuine process sequence). Header/Footer/content pages were already natural-role token-driven, so they were dark-safe with no change.

  - Scope: `app/themes/axis-autos-bespoke/styles/base.css`, `components/Hero.{tsx,module.css}`, `components/EditorialIntro.{tsx,module.css}`, `components/ServiceHighlights.{tsx,module.css}`, `components/CTABand.{tsx,module.css}`, `components/Directory.module.css`, `components/{FeaturedStock,RecentlySoldPreview}.module.css`
  - Reason: the homepage read as "cheap / not modern" (flat white + 1px-hairline wireframe card grids everywhere, a small photo boxed inside padding) and could not survive a dark dashboard palette. Two fixes: (1) **dark-theme** — the CTA band was the only homepage surface using the inverted-role dark tier (`--surface-bg-dark: var(--color-text)`), which flips to *light* when a dealer picks a dark `--color-bg`. Repainted it as a palette-safe brand-primary band (`.axis-section--brand` = `var(--brand-primary)` + `var(--brand-on-primary)`, with new `--on-brand` / `--ghost-on-brand` button variants). Every other homepage surface already derives from the dashboard pickers in natural roles, so the page now renders coherently on light AND dark palettes. (2) **modern look** — hero photo now bleeds to the left viewport edge and runs full-height, melting into the content column via a palette-adaptive blend gradient, with a more confident type scale; EditorialIntro and ServiceHighlights dropped the wireframe grids for editorial layouts led by a categorical `.axis-kicker` (true category labels, not a false 01/02/03 sequence — the number markers were removed after the design pass); Directory's 1px-gap grid became top-hairline editorial columns; link hovers gained an arrow-slide micro-interaction.
  - Notes: stayed inside the locked fingerprint (`split-screen-photo-left` signature + `decorative-density-minimum` one-big-move + the 7-section order) — distinctiveness gate still passes, no collisions vs 9 registered themes. `check-theme-contract` PASS, `audit-theme` 0 blockers, `npm run lint` clean. Detail page (`used-cars/[slug]`) still uses the inverted dark specs band — out of scope for this homepage pass; a follow-up if dark palettes ship for real dealers. Verified visually via a light/dark palette-toggle preview built on the theme's real token-derivation formulas (couldn't stand up the full Flask preview stack + a seeded axis brand locally).
