- 2026-06-10: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` (Columbus Vehicles) — modern CTA band redesign (image kept + enhanced) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/CtaBand.tsx` + `.module.css`. Photographic background kept (brand-record `finance` image → theme default). Rebuilt the chrome around it: flat dark mask + linear-gradient grid noise → a directional left-to-right scrim (0.88 → 0.42 → 0.55 across the band so the photo breathes on the right) layered with a soft top/bottom vignette; image layer also gets `saturate(108%) brightness(0.85)` + a 1.04 scale so edges hide behind the panel shadow. Content moved into a glassmorphism panel — `backdrop-filter: blur(14px) saturate(120%)`, color-mix translucent fill, subtle border + interior highlight + drop shadow. Eyebrow flipped from bracketed `[ Ready to drive? ]` to dash-prefix `Ready when you are` with a brand-primary dash. Title gets italic brand-primary accent on the second clause + text-shadow for legibility over the photo. Added 3 trust pills (`Finance available` / `Part-exchange welcome` / `UK-wide delivery`) with backdrop-blur fills, and replaced `auto-btn--*` classes with local primary (brand-primary pill, brand-primary glow shadow) + ghost (translucent `--color-bg` with backdrop-blur) buttons. Brand-glow blob anchored to the left of the panel, 60px blur. Mobile (<880px) flips the scrim to a vertical gradient and recenters the glow above the panel; <540px makes buttons full-width.
  - Reason: Difatha called out the previous version had contrast issues but wanted the photo background preserved. Solution: keep the photo, drop the noisy grid-line overlay + flat scrim that were the actual culprits, and house the content in a clean glassmorphism panel so the type + buttons get controlled contrast without obscuring the showroom photo. Brand-primary stays as the action color rather than the panel surface — keeps the photo as the hero.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Glassmorphism uses `color-mix(--color-bg 4–10%, transparent)` so light-brand records still produce a translucent dark-tinted panel over the photo. `backdrop-filter` includes the `-webkit-` prefix for Safari coverage. Ghost button uses a `color-bg 55%` border so it remains visible on bright photos as well as dark.

- 2026-06-10: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` (Columbus Vehicles) — StatsBand contrast fix (light-surface redesign) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/StatsBand.tsx` + `.module.css`. Section flipped from `auto-section--dark` + `surface-bg-dark` + `rgba(255,255,255,0.04)` card fill → light `surface-bg-light` section with white `surface-card-light` cards. Each cell now renders a circular brand-tint icon (Car/Award/Star/Truck/ShieldCheck per stat), a bold dark numeral (`text-on-light-strong` 2.2–3.2rem 700-weight), an uppercase label, and a sub line. Cards carry a top brand-primary 3px accent bar and a soft `0 2px 4px` shadow that lifts on hover. Eyebrow flipped from bracketed `[ By the numbers ]` to clean dash-prefix `By the numbers`. Title rule replaced with the soft brand-tint radial backdrop pair so the section keeps visual interest without dropping contrast. Removed the dark-section grid overlay div entirely.
  - Reason: Difatha pointed at the screenshot — dark cards on the dark surface had 4%-opacity white fill, so the cards bled into the background; red brand-primary numerals on near-black couldn't carry the section weight; eyebrow text was indistinct from the background gradient. Per `feedback_dashboard_tokens_drive_all_surfaces.md` the natural light-surface tokens give the cleanest contrast and let the numerals become the visual hero.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Brand-primary survives as the accent (top bar + icon chip + eyebrow) so the band still reads on-brand; numerals are paired token-aware (`text-on-light-strong`) so dark-brand records won't break the value text. `4.9` replaces the previous `5★` star glyph so the stat reads as a real review average rather than a marketing emoji.

- 2026-06-10: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` (Columbus Vehicles) — modern "Who we are" redesign (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/EditorialIntro.tsx` + `.module.css`. Split 2-column layout: left column is a 5:4 photo frame (`brand.images.about` → `brand.aboutImage` → `brand.heroImage` → theme default) with a soft brand-tinted offset backdrop behind it, a brand-primary "12+ Years on the forecourt" stat badge floating on the bottom-left corner, and a small "4.9 · Google reviewed" 5-star pill anchored top-right. Right column drops the quote card + two-paragraph copy → eyebrow + headline with italic brand-primary accent word (`Honest cars, *priced to move.*`) + one paragraph + a 3-item trust-points list with circular brand-tint icons (ShieldCheck / BadgeCheck / Truck) + a primary brand-pill CTA + ghost underline CTA. Section also gains a brand-primary diagonal corner ornament top-right. Mobile (<980px) stacks; <540px repositions the floating badge/rating chip so they don't collide with the frame edges.
  - Reason: Difatha wanted a more modern look. Previous version was a magazine-style 1.4fr/1fr split with a dark quote card sidebar and bracketed `[ Who we are ]` eyebrow — it read as editorial-blog, not as a dealer trust panel. New layout pairs a photographic anchor (showroom image + on-brand stat + Google rating proof) with terse copy and explicit trust signals, which is the modern dealer-page pattern.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Stat number is hardcoded to `12+` and rating to `4.9` (placeholder copy — these become brand-record-driven when the dashboard exposes `yearsTrading` / `googleRating` fields per the text-recipe contract). Trust-icon list uses `color-mix(brand-primary 14%)` background tiles so dark-brand records still render the icon with adequate contrast.

- 2026-06-10: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` (Columbus Vehicles) — Featured Stock → "Recent stock" smooth slider (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/FeaturedStock.tsx` + `.module.css`. Header trimmed to eyebrow + short title only — eyebrow flipped from `[ Today's stock ]` to `Recent stock`, title swapped from "Hot off the forecourt" to "Just landed", lead paragraph removed. CTA shortened to "View all". Grid replaced with a horizontal scroll-snap track that bleeds to the page edge (`margin-inline` negative to undo the container padding, `scroll-padding-inline` so snap points respect the inset). Each slide is `flex: 0 0 calc((100% - container padding ×2 - gaps) / 4)` at desktop, `/ 3` ≤1200px, `/ 2` ≤900px, 78% basis ≤600px. Added a pair of circular prev/next icon buttons in the header that call `scrollBy({ behavior: 'smooth' })` by one slide width; buttons disable themselves at the rail boundaries (scroll listener updates `canPrev` / `canNext`). Card markup unchanged apart from extracting `featuredBadge` into the image and dropping the finance-from line (one less number in the card body so the slider reads cleanly).
  - Reason: Difatha asked to redesign the section — less text in the header, retitle to "recent stock", and turn the 4-up grid into a smooth slider. Cleaner header reduces the noise around the carousel; the bleed + snap pattern lets users peek the next card edge as a slider affordance.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Scrollbar hidden via `scrollbar-width: none` + `::-webkit-scrollbar`. `scroll-behavior: smooth` covers keyboard and trackpad swipes; the explicit `scrollBy` calls also use `behavior: 'smooth'` so the nav buttons match. Card minimum width 260px (240px on mobile) keeps spec icons + title + price legible on the smallest slide.

- 2026-06-10: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` (Columbus Vehicles) — header/hero/makes-rail refresh (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/TopContactBar.tsx` + `.module.css` — rebuilt to the C&S Motors layout (phone / email / address inline on the left, 4 socials on the far right) on a *plain* neutral surface (`--color-surface`) with a `--color-border` divider beneath; dropped the secondary-brand gradient and the live/closed status chip. `components/Header.tsx` + `Header.module.css` — nav items renamed to the C&S set (Home · Used Cars · Recently Sold · Sell Your Car · Finance · Our Services), each rendered with `|` divider spans between items, active state is now an underline accent (not a dotted before-mark), search collapsed to a circular icon button, primary CTA replaced with a brand-primary rounded-pill `Get in Touch` (Phone glyph + label) on the right; mobile breakpoint pushed to 1080px so the longer nav fits. `components/Hero.module.css` — inner column flipped from `align-items: flex-start` to `align-items: center` + `text-align: center`, eyebrow + lead get `margin-inline: auto`, CTA row `justify-content: center`. `components/HorizontalStockTicker.tsx` + `.module.css` + new `lib/make-logos.ts` — ticker band painted in `--brand-primary` (was brand-tinted dark surface), each chip now carries a real brand glyph rendered from `cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/simple-icons` (port of the warwick `MAKE_LOGO_SLUGS`/`simpleIconsLogoUrl` helpers), wrapped in a white circular badge so the SVG mark contrasts against the colored bg; label/count/CTA foregrounds flipped to `--color-bg`.
  - Reason: Difatha sent a screenshot of the Columbus Vehicles preview alongside the C&S Motors header for visual reference. Wanted (1) the C&S contact-bar + pipe-nav + Get-in-Touch CTA layout but on a plain surface, (2) hero contents centred for the simpler one-focal-area composition, (3) the makes ticker to read as a confident on-brand strip with real brand marks instead of the same generic `Car` glyph repeated.
  - Notes: tsc-clean. `make-logos.ts` duplicated into the theme rather than shared per the "each theme is self-contained" convention. `--color-surface` falls back to `--color-bg` if a brand record doesn't define it. Top contact bar collapses socials at ≤540px; header search icon hides at ≤480px; `Get in Touch` label hides at ≤720px (icon only). Hero `margin-inline: auto` on eyebrow + lead keeps the brand-primary left-border accent visually correct while the column is centred.

- 2026-06-09: `warwick-hall-cars-bespoke` — About Us section below the hero (3-up feature cards) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/warwick-hall-cars-bespoke/components/AboutUs.tsx` (new), `components/AboutUs.module.css` (new), `pages/home/page.tsx` (renders `<AboutUs />` after `<Hero />`). Also hardened the hero-image priority chain in `context/BrandStyles.tsx` so `brand.images.hero` (dashboard recipe-section picker) wins over `brand.heroImage`, then theme default — matches the queensbury canonical pattern and drops the broken `/images/hero-placeholder.jpg` 404 fallback.
  - Reason: Difatha sent the dealer's existing About Us screenshot — small accent-blue car glyph, brand-accent "ABOUT US" eyebrow, oversized bold Karla "Welcome To Warwick Hall Cars" heading, then three white cards each with: bold title, brand-primary indigo body copy, soft-grey footer ribbon stamped with the card's own glyph as a faint silhouette pattern, a circular brand-primary icon button straddling the body/ribbon seam, and a small "VIEW SHOWROOM →" CTA right-aligned. Implementation pulls dealer-custom content from `brand.whyChooseUs.features` (titles + descriptions) and falls back to the exact screenshot copy when the brand record has no features set (this is a bespoke theme for Warwick Hall Cars, so the screenshot copy IS the dealer's actual marketing language). Icons map to a 3-icon rotation (ThumbsUp / Target / Users) when reading from the dashboard; defaults pin each glyph to its matching feature. CTAs link `/used-cars` (canonical route).
  - Notes: Card icon positioning is `position: absolute; top: 0; transform: translateY(-50%)` inside the foot ribbon so it always sits centred on the body/foot seam regardless of clamp() math or card height. Foot ribbon's left padding reserves room so the silhouette pattern + CTA never overlap the icon. Hover lifts the card 4px; reduced-motion users get the static frame. Brand-primary indigo on the body copy mirrors the screenshot — this is the only dealer-uploaded screenshot in the audit where the body is intentionally brand-tinted rather than `--color-muted`.

  - Scope: `app/themes/warwick-hall-cars-bespoke/components/Hero.tsx` (new), `components/Hero.module.css` (new), `pages/home/page.tsx` (stub → renders `<Hero />`), `context/BrandStyles.tsx` (adds `buildGoogleFontsImport(fontUi, fontBrand)` so Oswald actually loads instead of silently falling back to a system serif per `feedback_brandstyles_must_load_google_fonts`).

Hero composition follows the hero-simplicity caps (one focal area, no eyebrow / lead / chips / search / stats) and the photo-chrome dark uniform-scrim rule (`rgba(8,16,32,0.62 → 0.82)`). Title is Oswald (loaded via a hardcoded `@import` in `styles/base.css` because the theme's brand-font override defaults to Cormorant Garamond — the serif heritage identity belongs on body/feature copy, the hero needs a condensed-bold sans for the photo-chrome composition). Clamp() sizing to 6.2rem max, `text-wrap: balance`, no max-width (per `feedback_no_max_width_on_titles`). Background image uses the standard layered pattern (`var(--brand-image-hero, none)` + theme default `/themes/warwick-hall-cars-bespoke/images/hero.jpg`).

Mobile (≤640px) drops `min-height` to 72vh max, full-width pill, darker scrim; tiny screens (≤420px) further shrink the title clamp and brush width.
: unified action-chip pills + horizontal scroll-snap rail (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/pages/used-cars/page.module.css` — `.searchInput` tightened (padding 10/14 → 9/12, font 1rem → 0.9rem, fixed icon size 16×16, `min-height: 42px`); `.topbarControls` flipped from `flex-wrap: wrap` to a nowrap horizontal scroll-snap rail (`overflow-x: auto`, snap-type x mandatory, scrollbar hidden); each chip (`.sortSelect` / `.priceRangeSelect` / `.yearRangeSelect`) restyled as a unified pill — border + bg around the whole `<label> + <select>` pair, label as a small `0.68rem / 0.14em` uppercase prefix inside the pill, native select stripped of its own border/bg and given a CSS-only chevron via stacked linear-gradients, `:focus-within` lifts the brand-primary ring up to the pill so the visual focus matches the click target. JSX intact — all changes are CSS-only.
  - Reason: Difatha sent a zoomed-in screenshot of the toolbar on a 750px viewport. The search box was rendering ~60px tall with the placeholder clipping ("…keywo"), and the sort/price/year row collapsed into stacked-label garbage — "SORT BY" broke onto two lines because the label and select were two separate visual elements wrapping at the chip boundary. The chip-pill pattern is what the existing `feedback_inventory_toolbar_chips.md` memory prescribes; this page never adopted it.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Pattern matches `feedback_inventory_toolbar_pattern.md` (scroll-snap rail on mobile) + `feedback_inventory_toolbar_chips.md` (chips don't stretch, labels always visible, native select rendered as part of the chip). Rail behaves identically at desktop widths — chips fit without scrolling so it looks like a normal pill row; if a brand's locale produces extra-wide labels the rail just becomes scrollable.

All three are pure contrast/density fixes — no copy or layout intent changed.

- 2026-06-09: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` (Autowow Performance) — chrome color theory aligned with Rugged Auto standard (topbar=secondary, header=bg, footer=primary) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02/components/{TopContactBar,Header,Footer}.module.css` rewritten. TopContactBar surface flipped from `color-mix(var(--brand-primary) 6%, var(--surface-bg-dark))` to `var(--color-secondary)`; Header surface flipped from `var(--surface-bg-dark)` to `var(--color-bg)` (foreground `var(--color-text)`, active-nav underline `var(--color-primary)`, Search CTA primary-filled); Footer main surface flipped from `var(--surface-bg-dark)` to `var(--color-primary)` and the bottom strip from a border-only divider to a full `var(--color-secondary)` band. All inner foregrounds on filled brand surfaces pair with `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-bg) X%, transparent)` so contrast follows the brand pickers (not theme-locked neutrals).
  - Reason: Difatha flagged that the Performance theme paints header + topbar + footer all dark off the inverted `--surface-bg-dark = var(--color-text)` token, so the brand-record's secondary and primary pickers never actually show up in the chrome. The sibling Rugged Auto theme has the right model (topbar=secondary, footer=primary, bottom strip=secondary, header on `--color-bg`) — Performance now matches.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Mirrors the natural-role policy in `feedback_dashboard_tokens_drive_all_surfaces.md` — `--color-bg` is the surface, `--color-text` is the foreground, filled brand surfaces use `--color-primary`/`--color-secondary` with bg-token foregrounds. No literals; brand records repaint the whole site chrome.

- 2026-06-09: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` (Autowow Performance) — preview-screenshot triage: gallery extraction + dark-band header leak + hero contrast (owner: Difatha)

  - Reason: Columbus Vehicles preview showed most "Hot off the forecourt" cards as "Image not available" placeholders (extractor only walked `item.gallery` / `vehicle.gallery` / `item.media` — missed `.images`/`.photos` plus the `advert` shape some feeds return); a white block leaked behind the `[ BY THE NUMBERS ]` eyebrow on the dark stats band (same root cause as the existing `feedback_dark_section_header_local_typography.md` memory — sibling theme already shipped the fix, the `-02` fork didn't inherit it); hero lead text washed out over a bright Jeep/sky photo because the top of the overlay was only 28 % black.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Gallery extractor now matches the kain-motors-bespoke pattern (the canonical full-walk). Defensive `header` reset excludes the site `Header_*` class via attribute match so the actual top-of-page header isn't affected. Hero overlay still gradient-shaped (home hero, not inner-page) but now reads as uniformly dark per `feedback_hero_overlay_dark_uniform.md`.

- 2026-06-09: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` — natural-role surface sweep (no token inversion) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke/styles/{base.css,color-policy.css}`, every `components/*.module.css` (Header, Footer, Hero, SpecsBar, ServicesSection, RecentlySoldPreview, ReviewsSection, DirectorySection, CtaBanner, LatestArrivalsSection, AutoWowCookieBanner), every page `*.module.css` that referenced dark-tier surfaces (about, compare, wishlist, finance, recently-sold, used-cars, used-cars/[slug]), `app/widgets/VehicleGallery/{VehicleGallery.module.css,VehicleGallery.tsx}`. Replaced ~120 occurrences of `background: var(--color-text)` / `color: var(--color-bg)` / `color-mix(... var(--color-text)/--color-bg ...)` with their natural-role equivalents (bg-token as surface, text-token as foreground, surface-token for slightly off-bg cards, border-token for dividers); brand-tinted-dark variants flipped to brand-tinted-light (`primary X% + text → primary X% + bg`); lightbox + media chrome moved to literal `rgba(10,12,16,X)` / `#fff` so scrims remain dark on any brand polarity. Updated the policy comment block in color-policy.css to describe the natural-role pattern.
  - Reason: Difatha rejected the inverted-role pattern twice as "absurd" — text color isn't a background token. The honest reading of the 8 dashboard tokens is to use each in its semantic role. Identity is now brand-driven: brands that pick a dark `--color-bg` get rugged-dark structural surfaces; brands that pick a light `--color-bg` get light surfaces throughout. Accepted trade-off: theme's locked-dark identity dissolves for light-brand records (e.g. Columbus bg=#FFFFFF).
  - Notes: tsc exits 0. `background: var(--color-text)` count across theme + VehicleGallery = 0. Remaining `color: var(--color-bg)` hits are all on `background: var(--color-primary)` rules (`.auto-btn--primary`, `.auto-sold-banner`, `.summaryBtnEnquire`, `.removeBtn`, `.option.selected`, social-icon hovers, etc) — the documented CTA exception. Scrim overlays (heroOverlay, cardImage::after, cardOverlay) use `color-mix(... var(--color-bg) X%, transparent)` so they invert with brand polarity; foreground tints (muted text, hover backgrounds) use `color-mix(... var(--color-text) X%, transparent)` consistently. Three semantic literals (`#0f5132`, `#b42318`, `#f0b400`) left in place per directive.

- 2026-06-08: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` vehicle detail — full FBM structural parity rewrite + VehicleGallery thumb-rail overlay (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/auto-wow-uk-bespoke/pages/used-cars/[slug]/VehicleDetailIsland.tsx` rewritten (~555 → ~408 lines) — dropped title strip, dark quick-specs band, `looksLikeUkPlate`, `FinanceQuoteStrip`, `quickSpecs`, the old `.contentGrid`/`.priceCard`/`.contactGrid`/`.keyFacts`/`.trustList` system; introduced new `.detailsMain` → `.detailsShell` 2-col grid (`minmax(0,1.65fr) minmax(320px,0.85fr)`) with `.detailsPrimary` (gallery card → overview → key spec → preparation/history) on the left and a sticky `.detailsSide` summary card on the right that owns title + price + finance-from + 6 facts in 2-col grid + Call/Email/WhatsApp stack + 4-item trust list. `page.module.css` re-authored (~1085 → ~770 lines). `app/widgets/VehicleGallery/VehicleGallery.module.css` — thumb rail moved from grid-below to absolute-positioned overlay at bottom-center of the main image (`left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%); bottom: 24px;`) with `rgba(10,12,16,0.58)` scrim + `backdrop-filter: blur(6px)`; controls/counter/expand-button hardened to pure dark literals (photographic chrome, not brand surfaces); each thumb is 62×~39px (16:10) with `::after` brand-primary 2px active border.
  - Reason: Previous layout had a stand-alone title strip, a wide flat gallery widget with thumbs in a 6-column grid below, and a dark quick-specs band — none of those exist in firstbuymotors. Difatha needed exact structural parity so the AutoWow theme reads like a real dealership detail page (title belongs IN the summary card, key facts belong IN the summary card, gallery owns its own chrome). The grid-below thumb rail was also pushing content too far down on mobile.
  - Notes: tsc clean. VehicleGallery widget API unchanged (`images`, `alt`, `className`). Forbidden-pattern grep on page.module.css surfaces only legitimate uses — `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-text) X%, transparent)` tints, the documented CTA exception (`.summaryBtnCall` `background: var(--color-primary); color: var(--color-bg);`), and a `linear-gradient` color-mix. Mobile (≤1023px) drops sidebar `position: sticky` so the summary stacks under the primary column; mobile bottom bar still owns Price + Enquire.

Forbidden patterns clean (no `background: var(--color-text)`, no `color: var(--color-bg)`). Out of scope: page.tsx unchanged; no new deps; no data-aos.

- 2026-06-08: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` — ported firstbuymotors vehicle detail page over the old single-column gallery + sidebar layout (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `pages/used-cars/[slug]/VehicleDetailIsland.tsx` (rewrote JSX while keeping `useBrand`/`useGarage`/`getBrandContactInfo`/`useEnquiryModal` wiring and the `DetailVehicle/DetailSimilarVehicle/DetailMakeTally` props), `pages/used-cars/[slug]/page.module.css` (full stylesheet rewrite).
  - Reason: The previous detail layout had the title and gallery wedged inside a 2-col cell (per the cheap-default pattern memory) and didn't surface key specs above the fold. firstbuymotors's structure (breadcrumb+actions → title strip → full-width gallery → dark quick-specs band → 2-col content+sticky-sidebar → similar) is Difatha's preferred design and matches `feedback_vehicle_detail_modern_structure`.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Forbidden token-abuse grep clean (no `background: var(--color-text)`, no `color: var(--color-bg)` outside `.auto-btn--primary` policy). Reg badge surface uses `var(--t-feature-bg)` instead of `var(--color-text)` to honor the role-token policy. Makes panel removed from the page since BrowseByMake lives in the Shell (per `feedback_browse_by_make_in_shell_default`). Mobile sticky bar = Price + Enquire only (FAB handles WhatsApp) per `feedback_vehicle_detail_mobile`. Description Read-More toggle, share/wishlist/compare/print utility actions, swipe gestures, lightbox with keyboard nav, and finance calculator strip all ported. Skipped firstbuymotors's YouTubePlayer + featureGroups (auto-wow payload doesn't expose `featureDetails`), engine capacity/BHP/owners (`vehicle.specs` array carries those generically when present), and the in-page enquiry modal form (uses the shared `EnquiryModal` widget).

- 2026-06-08: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` — ported firstbuymotors compact inventory filter pattern (drawer + 3-pill toolbar) over the old sidebar + always-visible toolbar (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: new `components/Combobox.tsx` + `.module.css` (port of `apps/firstbuymotors/app/components/Combobox.tsx`, retokenised to theme variables and switched to CSS Modules); new `pages/used-cars/InventoryFilters.tsx` (trigger pill + sliding left drawer with transmission/fuel chip groups, make/body combos, price/year range pairs via SelectPair, max-mileage combo, active-count badge + Clear-all + Esc-close + scrim); new `pages/used-cars/InventoryToolbar.tsx` (count pill + debounced 450ms search + sort combobox); `pages/used-cars/UsedCarsClient.tsx` swapped 200-line toolbar + sidebar JSX (~lines 716–905) for `<InventoryFilters>` + `<InventoryToolbar>` inside `.toolbarRow`, removed `filtersOpen` state and unused `makes/bodies/fuels/transmissions/priceRangeOptions/yearRangeOptions/selectedPriceRangeValue/selectedYearRangeValue/priceRangeSelectOptions/yearRangeSelectOptions` derived memos plus `Filter/LayoutGrid/RefreshCcw/Search` icon imports; `pages/used-cars/page.module.css` removed `.toolbar/.toolbarActions/.searchInput/.sortSelect/.priceRangeSelect/.yearRangeSelect/.filters/.filtersOpen/.filterToggle/.filterGroup/.filterControls/.filterRow/.filtersHeader/.filtersMeta/.filtersCount/.filtersTitle/.clearButton/.topbarControls/.skeletonFilterHeader/.skeletonFilterBlock/.skeletonButtonSmall/.skeletonInput` (and their old media-query rules), added compact-filter classes (`.toolbarRow/.invFilterTrigger/.invFilter{Open,Scrim,Body,Head,Title,Clear,Close}/.invGroup/.invChips/.invChip{,Selected}/.invRange{,Cell,Sep}/.invToolbar/.invCount{,Badge,Label}/.invSearch{,Icon,Clear}/.invSort/.srOnly`) using theme tokens only (`var(--color-bg/--color-text/--color-primary/--color-border/--color-muted)` + `color-mix` alpha overlays).
  - Reason: Old sidebar pattern wasted vertical space on tablet/desktop and the always-visible toolbar pushed cards below the fold; firstbuymotors's compact pattern keeps filters one tap away in a left-sliding drawer with active-count feedback while the toolbar collapses to 3 pills (count, search, sort) that fit on a phone in 38px-tall mobile-compact mode.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Verbatim port of Combobox keyboard/a11y (Esc / arrows / Home / End / outside-click) — only the class-name source changed (CSS Modules vs global `.fbm-cmb-*`). Sidebar `model` filter omitted (UsedCarsClient doesn't track make→model mapping; not previously supported in the sidebar either, so no regression). The drawer is a white surface (light-tier brand tokens) per the auto-wow color policy — `--t-feature-bg` reserved for the theme's intentional dark bands; brand-primary still drives the active chip / trigger badge / count badge / search focus ring. All existing URL-sync + fetch + pagination behaviour unchanged.

- 2026-06-08: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` — replaced inverted-role token abuse with proper feature-surface tokens on every dark band (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `styles/{base,color-policy}.css`, 9 component module CSS files (Footer, Header, Hero, HeroBackdrop.tsx, SpecsBar, LatestArrivalsSection, ServicesSection, RecentlySoldPreview, ReviewsSection, DirectorySection, CtaBanner, AutoWowCookieBanner), and 6 page module CSS files (about, compare, wishlist, finance, recently-sold, used-cars, used-cars/[slug]). ~110 inverted-role replacements: `var(--color-text)` used as background → `var(--t-feature-bg{,-soft,-deeper})`; `var(--color-bg)` used as foreground/text/border on dark → `var(--t-feature-fg{,-muted,-faint})`, `var(--t-feature-border{,-strong})`, or `var(--t-feature-surface{,-hover})`. color-mix expressions that anchored on text/bg-token-as-inverted-role re-anchored on the appropriate feature token.
  - Reason: Previous tokenization sweep used `var(--color-text)` as a background and `var(--color-bg)` as foreground — semantic abuse that broke when a brand record swapped its text/bg tokens (e.g. dark-on-light dealer). New feature-surface role tokens (`--t-feature-bg`, `--t-feature-fg`, etc) lock the theme's signature dark bands to literal dark values regardless of brand text/bg choices, while brand-primary/accent overlays still flow through. Foreground text on filled brand-primary buttons (the canonical `--t-action-fg` policy) intentionally kept on `var(--color-bg)` — that's not inverted abuse, it's the action-foreground pattern.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Light-tier card surfaces (`background: var(--color-bg)` for cards on light pages) left untouched. Brand-primary fill button foreground (`color: var(--color-bg)` on `.auto-btn--primary`, `.auto-sold-banner`, etc) left untouched per `--t-action-fg` policy. Skeleton-shimmer & frosted-pill-on-photo treatments using `color-mix(... var(--color-bg) NN%, transparent)` left untouched (those are light frosted glass on photos, not feature-surface inverted abuse).

- 2026-06-08: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` — tokenized every hardcoded color literal so dashboard brand-record overrides flow through every surface (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: 23 CSS files + 1 TSX (HeroBackdrop SVG) across `components/`, `styles/{base,color-policy}.css`, and all 10 `pages/*/page.module.css` files. Replaced literal navy/black dark surfaces (`#0a0e14`, `#050810`, `#101827`, `#1a1f2a`) with `var(--color-text)` (inverted-role pattern) or `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-text) NN%, ...)`. Light surfaces (`#ffffff`, `#f8fafc`, `#f6f7fb`) → `var(--color-bg)` / `var(--color-surface)`. Light borders (`#e2e8f0`, `#cbd5e1`, `#d8e1ec`, `#dbe4ee`, `#e5e7eb`) → `var(--color-border)`. Foreground text (`#111827`, `#0f1623`, `#243044`) → `var(--color-text)`. Muted text (`#596579`, `#526175`, `#687386`, `#8b95a5`, `#64748b`, `#475569`, `#94a3b8`) → `var(--color-muted)`. `rgba(255,255,255,X)` white-on-dark overlays → `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-bg) X%, transparent)`. `rgba(190,14,17,X)` brand-red overlays → `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) X%, transparent)`. Teal accents (`#22c7d4`, `#1f9aaa`) → `var(--color-accent)`. Dashboard hex anchors converted to color-mix step-darkers. Replaced `var(--color-primary-dark, #8c090c)` with `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 80%, #000)`.
  - Reason: A "rugged auto" theme shipped with `#0a0e14` literals everywhere ignored the dashboard's 8 brand color tokens. A dealer that picks light-bg + dark-text (e.g. Columbus Vehicles: bg=#FFFFFF, text=#272626) loaded the theme with hardcoded dark surfaces that fought the brand record. Now every dark surface derives from `--color-text` (inverted role pattern from `feedback_dashboard_tokens_drive_all_surfaces`), every light surface from `--color-bg`/`--color-surface`, and brand-tinted decoration uses `color-mix(... var(--color-primary)...)` so dashboard edits propagate without CSS rebuild.
  - Notes: tsc clean. Intentionally kept literals: pure black `rgba(0,0,0,X)` shadows + scrims + mask-images, star yellow `#f0b400`, semantic `--t-success: #0f5132` / `--t-error: #b42318`, color-mix `#fff`/`#000` palette-shifter anchors (these aren't surface colors, they're tonal endpoints). LatestArrivalsSection card kept its protective comments rewritten — the surface now reads `--color-surface` and the title reads `--color-text` (relying on the dashboard pairing those tokens correctly, per the inverted-role pattern). Rainbow per-icon `--accent` cycle in used-cars cards now cycles `primary → accent → secondary` instead of `#2563eb / #f97316 / #16a34a / #0ea5e9`. `BrandStyles.tsx` literal defaults left untouched — those are bootstrap fallbacks when the brand record itself is missing values, not theme color logic.

- 2026-06-08: `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` (Rugged Auto) — full sweep: all home-section headers converted to local typography (kills the recurring white-block bug); CtaBanner ghost buttons get readable contrast; defensive base.css rule blocks the bug for future sections (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `styles/base.css` (defensive rule: any `<header>` inside `.auto-section` gets `background: transparent !important; box-shadow: none !important;` — `:not([class*='Header_'])` lets the site Header stay styled); `components/ReviewsSection.tsx` + `.module.css` (local eyebrow + title, dropped `auto-eyebrow` / `auto-section-title` globals; teal-tinted radial accent; `data-aos` stripped); `components/DirectorySection.tsx` + `.module.css` (local eyebrow + title + viewAll, split header layout, `data-aos` stripped); `components/LatestArrivalsSection.tsx` + `.module.css` (local eyebrow + title + viewAll, `data-aos` stripped; viewAll moved off the global `.auto-cta-link` to a local rule); `components/CtaBanner.module.css` (added `.cta:global(.auto-btn--ghost)` overrides — frosted dark backdrop + `rgba(255,255,255,0.6)` border + solid white text + inverted white-on-brand hover).
  - Reason: Difatha showed the same white-block bug repeating on Reviews / Recently Sold / Services. Root cause was the global `.auto-eyebrow` / `.auto-section-title` / `.auto-section-lead` cascade leaking a white surface when nested inside a `<header>` on a dark section. Two-prong fix: (1) defensive base.css net so the bug can't surface anywhere a `<header>` lives inside an `auto-section`, (2) systematic rewrite of every home section's header to use local module typography — no global utility classes inside `<header>` JSX. Bonus: CtaBanner ghost buttons (Phone + WhatsApp) were illegible against the red gradient end of the hero — solid frosted bg + stronger border now gives proper contrast.
  - Notes: tsc clean. AOS isn't bootstrapped in this theme so `data-aos` attrs are dead weight — stripped from Reviews / Directory / LatestArrivals while in there. CtaBanner still has `data-aos` attrs on its inner copy/actions divs because those were there before this sweep and have no visual impact — kept to minimise diff. Saved the pattern as `feedback_dark_section_header_local_typography` memory so it doesn't recur on the next theme.

- 2026-06-01: `buy4lessuk-bespoke` — reference-match pass: serif typography, real automotive imagery, simplified header (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `tokens.ts` + `context/BrandStyles.tsx` (heading family swapped from `Oswald` to `Playfair Display` to match the Buy4Less reference's display-serif aesthetic); `styles/base.css` (added `@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:...&family=Inter:...&display=swap')` to actually load the family); `context/BrandStyles.tsx` (introduced a `FALLBACKS` table of curated Unsplash CDN photo URLs — hero / about / services / finance / partExchange / sellYourCar / recentlySold — so each per-page image slot has a real automotive photo when the brand record has nothing uploaded, instead of falling back to a grey placeholder); `components/Header.tsx` + `components/Header.module.css` (v4 — dropped the brand-accent strip, the glass-on-scroll, the sliding underline; matches the reference's flat white header with simple text nav, red filled-pill Contact CTA, and a clean hamburger drawer); `components/BrandLogo.module.css` (wordmark explicitly overridden to UI sans-serif + `text-transform: uppercase` + `letter-spacing: 0.04em` so the logo never picks up the serif heading family); `components/{ManufacturersBodySplit,PopularMakes,FixedCtaBar,Footer}.module.css` (the uppercase-tracked section labels overridden to UI sans-serif — Playfair Display in uppercase tracking would look amateur); `components/VehicleCard.tsx` + `VehicleCard.module.css` (re-designed the "no photo" placeholder — was a flat grey block, now a camera icon + "Image coming soon" caption over a brand-tinted gradient with a diagonal repeating texture so cards stay visually structured when stock photos are missing).
  - Reason: Difatha looked at the deployed Buy4Less reference again and asked for "exact 100% like this without contrast issues, find images from the internet." Two structural mismatches with the reference: (a) the reference uses serif display headings (clearly visible on "Welcome to Buy4Less UK", hero headline, vehicle titles, finance/sell card titles) — my theme was on Oswald (a condensed sans), so headlines looked wrong even when layout matched; (b) the reference is full of real automotive photography (twin-car hero, silver Mercedes welcome shot, showroom-interior + handshake + SUV image-trio cards) — my theme fell back to grey/dark squares when a brand had no images. Also rolled back the v3 "modern futuristic" header moves (accent strip, glass, sliding underline) because the reference is intentionally flat and restrained — the over-design was working against the brief.
  - Notes: tsc clean, audit 0 blockers / 137 advisories. 9/9 routes 200 in `next dev`. Unsplash URLs are CDN-hot-linked with `?w=…&auto=format&fit=crop&q=80` hints — they're hardcoded in `BrandStyles.tsx` (the `FALLBACKS` object), brand records override on a per-slot basis. If a particular Unsplash photo ID 404s in the future, the CSS gradient fallbacks I already had in place from earlier passes (ImageTrio repeating-line texture, HeroSplash scrim, FinanceSellSplit `::after` masked overlay) ensure the page still composes — no white voids. Open follow-up: switch the `<img>` tag in `VehicleCard.tsx` to `next/image` (currently `perf-raw-img` advisory) — would need to add `images.unsplash.com` + `images.pexels.com` to `next.config.js` `remotePatterns`. Deferred because it crosses theme boundaries and the wider brandstudio config.

- 2026-06-01: `buy4lessuk-bespoke` — header v3 rewrite, fixed broken grid layout from v2 (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `components/Header.tsx` (removed the duplicate "Open now / By appointment" status chip from the main header — TopBar already shows this; simplified the children tree to just `[brandLink, nav, actions]`; added `scrolled` state for glass-on-scroll); `components/Header.module.css` (full rewrite — switched from 3-column CSS grid to a clean horizontal flex bar, swapped `pill-group nav` for cleaner text-link nav with a sliding underline, demoted phone from a text pill to a 42px icon-only circle, kept the filled-pill Contact CTA, added `backdrop-filter` frosted-glass that emerges only on scroll, kept the 2px brand-color accent strip across the very top, kept the right-side drawer for mobile).
  - Reason: v2 header rendered visibly broken on Columbus Vehicles preview — the `statusChip` (which I'd put in the middle `1fr` grid column) stretched to fill the full column width because grid items default to `justify-self: stretch`, AND the `actions` cluster was a 4th child in a 3-column grid so it wrapped onto a second row underneath the logo. End result was a giant "OPEN NOW" pill spanning the whole header and the phone+CTA stacked under the logo. Two structural mistakes, one cosmetic mistake (duplicate status chip), all addressed by going back to a single-row flex layout and removing the duplicate.
  - Notes: tsc clean, audit 0 blockers / 137 advisories (down from 142 — fewer hardcoded colors after removing the status-chip styles). 9 routes still 200 via `next dev`. Modern signature retained: brand-color accent strip, frosted-glass header on scroll via `backdrop-filter: saturate(160%) blur(12px) + color-mix() opacity`, sliding underline that scales from left on hover, filled-pill Contact CTA with brand-tinted shadow and a small arrow that nudges +2px/-2px on hover. The phone is now an icon-only ghost circle (42px) at desktop, hidden on the smallest viewports. Lesson for next theme: never use a default-stretch CSS grid for header chrome with variable visibility children — flex is safer because items don't stretch by default, and child count doesn't affect column layout.

- 2026-06-01: `buy4lessuk-bespoke` — header redesign + slider fix + sell-my-car route correction (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `components/Header.tsx` + `components/Header.module.css` (full rewrite — new asymmetric layout: 3px brand-accent strip across the very top, diamond logo + tagline lockup on left, live "Open now / By appointment" status chip with pulse-dot, pill-grouped horizontal nav with hover dot + active filled-pill state, ghost phone pill + filled arrow-suffixed Contact CTA on the right, hamburger-only on narrow viewports; mobile drawer rewritten as a right-side full-height panel with brand-color leading edge, numbered link rows that stagger-fade in, drawer scrim is a proper `<button>` for a11y, contact info pinned to the bottom of the drawer); `components/FeaturedStock.module.css` (switched `grid-auto-columns` from `minmax(0, calc(25% - 12px))` to a fixed `calc(25% - 11px)` per breakpoint so the track scrolls horizontally instead of squashing 13 cards into the visible viewport — the `minmax(0, ...)` lower bound let cards shrink past target width and never trigger overflow); `components/{Header,Footer,FinanceSellSplit,QuickSearchTabs,Shell}.tsx` (s/`/sell-your-car`/`/sell-my-car`/ — brandstudio's canonical route).
  - Reason: Difatha's second spot-check on the Columbus Vehicles preview showed the slider visibly broken (13 cards crammed in a single viewport, no horizontal scroll triggered) and asked for a more distinctive header — "more unique, modern and futuristic". The slider issue was a mistake in my prior `minmax(0, ...)` fix — the `0` minimum meant cards shrunk past target instead of forcing overflow. The header redesign moves the theme away from the generic "centered logo + horizontal nav + red button" Buy4Less reference and onto a more recognisable signature: top brand-color strip, status chip integrated with the wordmark, pill-grouped nav with both hover + active states, and a numbered-row drawer that telegraphs IA on mobile rather than a generic vertical link list.
  - Notes: tsc clean, audit 0 blockers / 142 advisories (small increase from defensive fallback hexes and a few more hardcoded-color advisories inside the new header's status-dot + drawer-scrim — accepted trade-off for guaranteed legibility). All 9 routes tested via `next dev` return 200 (`/`, `/finance`, `/contact`, `/compare`, `/wishlist`, `/cookie-policy`, `/used-cars`, `/about`, `/sell-my-car`). Header is mobile-first by construction: every visual layer starts at the narrow viewport and progressively enhances via min-width media queries at 480/720/1024/1280. The desktop sticky-on-scroll affordance from the previous design was deliberately not reintroduced — brandstudio's PreviewBanner already occupies the `top: 0; sticky` slot and would conflict.

- 2026-06-01: `buy4lessuk-bespoke` — fixed contrast + horizontal-overflow issues caught in the Columbus Vehicles preview (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/buy4lessuk-bespoke/styles/base.css` (added `overflow-x: clip` on theme root + body + main, introduced `--b4l-header-text` CSS var with a hard `#0f1623` fallback, switched the bottom-pad-for-CTA-bar from main padding to `footer { margin-bottom }` so the footer stays visible above the sticky bar); `components/Header.module.css` (removed `position: sticky; top: 0` — it was colliding with PreviewBanner which is also `sticky; top: 0` at higher z-index; switched to `position: relative` so the header sits BELOW the PreviewBanner in normal flow); `components/Header.module.css` + `components/BrandLogo.module.css` (use `var(--b4l-header-text, var(--color-header-text, #0f1623))` instead of trusting the brand-record-overridable token alone — defensive against brands with light/unset textColor); `components/FeaturedStock.module.css` (added `overflow: hidden` on `.section`, replaced track `margin: 0 -4px` with `margin: 0`, switched `grid-auto-columns` from percent-of-track to `minmax(0, calc(N% - gap))` so cards fit the track exactly at 1/2/3/4-up breakpoints rather than peeking past the right edge); `components/HeroSplash.module.css` (rebalanced the scrim — top half is now transparent so the hero image is actually visible, only the bottom 30% darkens for headline contrast); `components/ImageTrio.module.css` (added a brand-tinted gradient + repeating-line texture as `.card` fallback for brands with no `--brand-image-*` set, plus stronger `.label` contrast with `#fff/#0f1623` + a small drop shadow); `components/FinanceSellSplit.module.css` (dialed the dark-finance-card's `::after` brand-image overlay from `opacity: 0.42` down to `0.18` with a mask-image fade — was blocking the budget slider readability when a finance image was present); `components/FixedCtaBar.module.css` (added `padding-right: clamp(80px, 10vw, 110px)` so the CTA bar's content stays clear of the WhatsApp FAB, dropped z-index from 50 → 35 so WhatsApp keeps its primary-action priority, tightened mobile breakpoint padding).
  - Reason: Difatha spot-checked the deployed theme rendering against the Columbus Vehicles preview brand (green primary, brand has no `images.*` uploaded) and reported: (1) Header text + COLUMBUS wordmark were almost-invisible because the green sticky PreviewBanner was covering my also-sticky header, AND because the header text token chain bottomed out at `--color-header-text` which a brand record could override to a light value; (2) the FeaturedStock slider's rightmost card extended past the viewport edge causing page-level horizontal scroll; (3) the ImageTrio cards rendered as solid black squares because Columbus has no brand images set; (4) the FixedCtaBar collided with the WhatsApp FAB at the bottom-right. All four were structural mistakes in the first cut, not Columbus-specific — every brand without those exact image slots would have hit the same dark-box / overflow bugs.
  - Notes: tsc clean, audit still 0 blockers (125 advisories — up from 118 because the defensive `#0f1623` and `#b71623` fallback hexes inside `var(--token, #fallback)` count as `brand-hardcoded-color` advisories; trade-off for guaranteed legibility regardless of brand record). All 6 refactored pages (home, finance, contact, compare, wishlist, cookie-policy, used-cars) still respond 200 from `next dev`. Header sticky-on-scroll was a non-essential affordance — losing it is the right call given brandstudio's PreviewBanner already occupies the `top: 0; sticky` slot for every theme.

- 2026-06-01: Added `buy4lessuk-bespoke` theme — bespoke dealer-signage clone of the Buy4Less UK landing page reference (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/buy4lessuk-bespoke/*` — new theme scaffolded via `tools/scaffold-theme-skeleton.mjs` with DNA at `tools/.dna/buy4lessuk-bespoke.json` (primary `#e11d2a`, Oswald headings + Inter body, dealer-signage radii/shadows). 13 home-page components written fresh (`BrandLogo`, `TopBar`, `Header`, `Footer`, `HeroSplash`, `FinanceSellSplit`, `FeaturedStock`, `VehicleCard`, `WelcomeBlock`, `ManufacturersBodySplit`, `QuickSearchTabs`, `ImageTrio`, `PartnersStrip`, `PopularMakes`, `FixedCtaBar`, `ValuationForm`, `PageHero`, `PageShell`, `Shell`); home + 13 inner pages composed (`home`, `about`, `contact`, `services`, `sell-your-car`, `finance`, `part-exchange`, `recently-sold`, `compare`, `wishlist`, `privacy-policy`, `cookie-policy` — the kept springalls `used-cars` list + `used-cars/[slug]` detail page from the skeleton plumbing). New server-side data loader at `lib/home-data.server.ts` fetches featured inventory + makes list once on the home page and prop-drills to client components (Server Component pattern). `theme/theme-manifest.json` got the new entry. `context/BrandStyles.tsx` retoned to Buy4Less reds + diamond-mark defaults (still brand-record-overridable). `styles/base.css` updated with token-driven baseline + bottom padding so the sticky CTA bar doesn't overlap content.
  - Reason: User asked for "a new app as a theme (100% plug and play) in brandstudio exactly like the screenshot given for the landing page". Reference: `https://carous.co.uk/app/uploads/2025/10/screencapture-buy4lessuk-co-uk-2023-12-06-14_14_04-450x1536-1.webp`. Landing page mirrors the screenshot section-for-section: top utility bar (compare / opening hours / get in touch), header with red diamond + wordmark + Contact-Us red CTA, twin-car splash hero with "Welcome to..." + Read more, dark-card finance + red-card sell-your-car split panel with budget slider and yellow UK reg input, horizontal featured-stock carousel with diamond badge + yellow "You're in safe hands" strip + price/finance row, welcome block with image left + body right, dark Manufacturers + Body-Types split panel, Find-a-Car / Sell-Your-Car tabbed quick search, Showroom / Finance / Vehicle-Sourcing image trio, finance-partner row, popular-makes grid, dark Contact / Quick Links / Opening Hours footer, and the sticky "Car Finance in 60 Seconds → APPLY FOR FREE" CTA bar dismissible per-session. Brand-driven (uses `brand.theme.colors`, `brand.name`, `brand.location`, `brand.openingHours`, `brand.images.*`) so swapping brand records re-skins the theme; "Buy4Less" copy/wordmark only shows when that brand record is loaded.
  - Notes: Theme passes the audit (`node tools/audit-theme.mjs --id buy4lessuk-bespoke` → 0 blockers, 118 advisories — mostly hardcoded fallback hex inside `var(--color-*, #xxx)` defaults and `<img>` tags inside the kept springalls `used-cars` plumbing). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. `npm run theme:sync` registered the theme in all four generated registries. Dev server compile succeeded (`GET /` returned 200 in 4.7s) but full browser preview was not exercised because the Flask brand-records backend (`app.py`) wasn't running locally — `[brandApi] Failed to fetch brand springalls [TypeError: fetch failed] ECONNREFUSED`. Verification will land once a brand record is wired up via the dashboard. Deviations from `/new-theme` formal flow: the skill wasn't surfaced as a Claude Skill in this harness session, so the scaffold tool was invoked directly with a hand-crafted DNA file rather than going through Phase A1–A11. Distinctiveness / fingerprint registry steps from the skill were skipped — should be backfilled before this theme ships to a real prospect preview (run `tools/check-theme-uniqueness.mjs` + add fingerprint entry).

- 2026-05-27: `cnhcars-clone` — fixed "Gallery image unavailable" on every inventory card + widened the inventory page container (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/components/VehicleCard.tsx` (`buildOrderedGalleryImages` now falls back to `vehicle.media[].href||.url`, `vehicle.images[]`, and `vehicle.image` when `vehicle.gallery` is empty; `useMemo` + reset-effect dep arrays widened to match); `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/styles/inventory-bg.css` (new `.inventory-page .inventory-content.container { max-width: 1640px }` override of the global 1400px `.container` cap).
  - Reason: Columbus Vehicles preview spot-check showed every inventory card rendering the "Gallery image unavailable" placeholder despite the API returning photos. Root cause: `buildOrderedGalleryImages` only read `vehicle.gallery` (array of `{url, href}`) even though the props interface declared it would accept `image`/`images`/`media` — the brandstudio inventory endpoint returns photos under `images[]` for some dealer feeds (including Columbus), so the card got an empty array and rendered the empty state. Same `useMemo`/effect closures were keyed only on `vehicle.gallery`, so once fixed they'd still stale-render until paginate; widened deps to keep them honest. Layout: the page used the global `.container { max-width: 1400px }` from `styles/base.css` which left visible white margins either side of the 3-card row on wider monitors — page-scoped override to 1640px so 3-card rows breathe and 4-card rows become reachable past ~1500px viewports without changing the per-card `minmax(300px)` grid math.
  - Notes: Same bug exists upstream in `carous-platform/apps/cnhcars/app/components/VehicleCard.tsx` (which this theme was cloned from). NOT fixed here — that's a separate repo + separate deploy; flagged for follow-up so the next clone doesn't regress. Layout override is page-scoped (`.inventory-page` ancestor) so the rest of the theme's `.container` usage (about, legal, contact, hero) stays on the existing 1400px / per-page caps. No grid changes — the `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))` math is unchanged; just more horizontal room.

- 2026-05-27: `cnhcars-clone` theme made brand-driven + Mode B cloner hardened with 9 fixes so future clones don't repeat the same issues (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/components/{Header,ContactBar,Contact,ContactPageForm,Footer,About,Stats,Testimonials,HeroSmall,Hero,SellYourCarMount,FeaturedCars}.tsx`; `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/pages/{contact,sell-your-car,used-cars}/page.tsx`; `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/hooks/useInventory.ts`; `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/lib/{contact.ts,brand-slug.server.ts,inventory.ts}` (new + updates); `tools/clone-app-to-theme.mjs` (cloner patches).
  - Reason: Difatha spot-checked the deployed `cnhcars-clone` theme rendered through a non-CNH brand record (Columbus Vehicles preview) and found 5 user-visible issues — (1) Header had hardcoded CNH logo path + phone + trailing-slash nav slugs, (2) homepage Contact form had a visible honeypot + showed "This field is required" without user interaction + hardcoded CNH contact details, (3) inventory page 404'd because `apiUrl('/inventory')` hit the wrong endpoint (no /api prefix, no brand slug), (4) `/sell-your-car` 404'd because the Header link didn't use brandstudio's canonical `/sell-my-car` route, (5) `/contact` page rendered an empty `<div id="contact-form-widget" />` mount-point waiting for a script that doesn't exist in brandstudio. Difatha asked the fixes to land in the cloner so future Mode B runs don't repeat them.
  - Notes: Created `lib/contact.ts` + `lib/brand-slug.server.ts` helpers (copied from `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` patterns). Every visible component now reads from `useBrand()` instead of `companyProfile`/hardcoded strings. Honeypots styled via inline `clip: rect(0,0,0,0)` so they're invisible. Inventory fetches now go to brandstudio's same-origin `/api/inventory?brand=<slug>` with the slug threaded from `useBrand().slug` (client) or `getBrandSlugFromRequest()` (server). `/contact` page widget mount replaced with `<ContactPageForm />` (the cloned form component, also brand-driven). Sell-your-car JSON-LD + metadata stripped (brandstudio runtime owns it). Footer link list normalized to brandstudio canonical routes (no trailing slashes, `/sell-my-car` not `/sell-your-car`, `/cookie-policy` not `/cookies`). **Cloner patches in `tools/clone-app-to-theme.mjs`**: (i) `discoverRoutes` now requires exact `page.tsx` basename match — prevents `lib/vehicle-detail-page.tsx` from being misclassified as a route (cnhcars-clone had route count 14 → 13 after fix); (ii) `rewriteImports` extended with a depth-2+ pass that converts source's `../<seg>/X` to `../../<seg>/X` for pages at `pages/<route>/X.tsx` depth; (iii) page-sibling pickup expanded from `Island.tsx`/`Mount.tsx` to also include `Client.tsx` (catches `UsedCarsPageClient.tsx`, `WishlistPageClient.tsx`); (iv) cloner now copies the source's `hooks/` folder + `context/<other>Context.tsx` files automatically; (v) `emitPagesTs` maps source kebab routes to brandstudio's `ThemePageRegistry` enum keys via a `SOURCE_ROUTE_TO_ENUM` table (cookies→cookiePolicy, sell-your-car→sellYourCar, used-cars/[id]→vehicleDetail, etc.) — routes without an enum slot are kept on disk but unregistered; (vi) `emitTokensTs` now exports `themeTokens: ThemeTokenMap` (named export) and resolves `var()` refs to real hex via new `pickHex()` helper; (vii) `emitShellTsx` exports `themeShell: ThemeShellComponent` (named) and no longer double-wraps in BrandClientWrapper (context-registry does that); (viii) `emitBrandClientWrapper` now types brand as `BrandConfig` (not loose `{[k]: unknown}`) so consumers like `DynamicFavicon` type-check; (ix) added new emitters for `sections/index.tsx` + `recipes/index.ts` (empty stubs satisfying the contract registry) and `context/AuthContext.tsx` + `context/DynamicFavicon.tsx` (stubs copied from springalls-classic); (x) new `tokenizeRoutes()` rewrites `/sell-your-car` → `/sell-my-car` and strips trailing-slash hrefs from canonical top-level routes in every `.tsx`/`.ts` file. Verification: tsc clean repo-wide, audit 0 blockers (advisories 1556 → 1547 thanks to fewer hardcoded CSS imports), similarity baseline 0.003 vs springalls. **Still deferred**: bulk `:where(body[data-theme-id='cnhcars-clone'])` CSS scoping pass (~1500 advisories from the source app's pre-scoped global styles); rewiring `data/profile.ts` reads to `useBrand()` across the remaining vehicle-detail / valuation-form components; adapting `pages/used-cars/[id]/page.tsx` from `({ params })` to `({ brand, vehicleSlug })` signature before vehicle detail will render correctly.

- 2026-05-26: Added `cnhcars-clone` theme — Mode B clone of `carous-platform/cnhcars` (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/cnhcars-clone/*` (full theme contract — 14 source routes copied, 31 components, 33 styles, 10 lib helpers, 2 data files; 3 page-sibling client components + 2 hooks + 1 WishlistContext copied separately because the cloner's route discovery skips them). `docs/FEATURE_LOG.md` entry. CLONE_FOLLOWUPS.md updated with deferred Phase 8 work.
  - Reason: `/new-theme --app-name cnhcars` invocation. Mode B faithful clone is the new (2026-05-26) flow — produces a theme that renders like the source app with brand-record-overridable colors/fonts/images.
  - Notes: Several cloner gaps surfaced and were patched by hand: (a) `pages.ts` keys had to be rewritten to match brandstudio's `ThemePageRegistry` enum (cookies→cookiePolicy, privacy→privacyPolicy; disclaimer/terms/testimonials/lib have no enum slot and stay on disk unregistered); (b) the cloner mis-classified `lib/vehicle-detail-page.tsx` as a `pages/lib/page.tsx` route — deleted; (c) `theme.json` + `tokens.ts` + `BrandStyles.tsx` had `var(--primary-hover)` CSS-var refs as values — replaced with real hex (#4169E1 / #5680E9 / #f8f9fa / #0f1623); (d) `pages/*/page.tsx` and the two page-sibling Client files had off-by-one relative-import paths (`../components/X` → `../../components/X`) — fixed by one-shot script `tools/.tmp/fix-cnhcars-imports.mjs` (47 imports across 13 files); (e) `@carous/hooks` `useLeadsForm` bridged with a 90-LOC shim at `lib/use-leads-form.ts` (adds top-level `validate`, `handleSubmit`, `successMessage`, `reset(nextValues?)`); `@carous/vehicle-views` `VehicleViewTracker` stubbed to a no-op; `@carous/seo` `createSeoBuilder` inlined into `lib/seo.ts` (~50 LOC); `@carous/sell-your-car` swapped for brandstudio's `<SellYourCarWidget />`; (f) added `context/AuthContext.tsx` + `context/DynamicFavicon.tsx` stubs (copied from springalls-classic) to satisfy the context-registry contract; (g) `tokens.ts` renamed `tokens` → `themeTokens` and `shell.tsx` exposes `themeShell` named export. Verification: tsc clean repo-wide, audit 0 blockers / 1556 advisories (vast majority are `std-css-unscoped-global-rule` from the source app's pre-scoped global stylesheets — Phase 8 followup), similarity baseline pass clean (0.003 max vs springalls-classic). **Known deferred work**: page wrappers use `({ params })` Next-router signature instead of `({ brand, vehicleSlug })` ThemePageProps — Phase 8 must adapt at minimum the vehicleDetail page; `data/profile.ts` + `data/vehiclePurchaseTerms.ts` hardcoded dealer info should be rewired to read from `brand` prop / `useBrand()` for the theme to be "highly configurable like usual preview themes"; ~1556 advisories from unscoped CSS rules awaiting bulk `:where(body[data-theme-id='cnhcars-clone'])` wrapping pass.

- 2026-05-26: `/new-theme` Mode B reshaped — `--app-mode <app>` page-for-page clone via new `tools/clone-app-to-theme.mjs` (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `tools/clone-app-to-theme.mjs` (new, ~600 LOC — discovery, copy, import rewriter, color/font/image tokenizer, theme-contract emitter, follow-ups checklist). `.claude/skills/new-theme/SKILL.md` (Invocation section rewritten; Prerequisites updated; Mode B section fully rewritten with B1–B5 phases + `--app-mode` flag; Tool reference table updated — `scaffold-theme.mjs` marked deprecated for Mode B, new row for `clone-app-to-theme.mjs`).
  - Reason: Old Mode B (`--from <app>`) only extracted DNA and ran the skeleton scaffolder (which strips the visual layer) — the result was effectively a recolored springalls clone, not the source app. Difatha asked for a new mode that produces a theme "similar to the specified app in carous-platform repo, same design, maybe better styles but similar components from homepage to last page, but a theme, highly configurable like the usual preview themes." New flow: faithful clone of pages + components + styles + lib + data, then tokenize colors/fonts/image-URLs so brand records can override. Tested on `lancashirecarsalesltd`: 12 routes, 43 components, 38 styles, 8 lib helpers, 2 data files copied; 364 hex tokens / 241 font tokens / 4 image tokens applied.
  - Notes: `--from` flag retired in favour of `--app-mode` (older docs referencing `--from` should be treated as the legacy behaviour). The cloner skips `api/`, `dashboard/`, `login/`, `data/` (as a routed dir — still copied as a folder), `hooks/`, `search/`, `in/`, `out/` during route discovery. Color tokenization resolves `var(--name)` references in the DNA via the source's `globals.css` `:root` block — most source apps store their palette as CSS custom properties so the DNA file alone has only var references. Theme contract emitted: `theme.json` (with `mode: "app-clone"`), `pages.ts`, `shell.tsx`, `tokens.ts`, `context/BrandClientWrapper.tsx`, `context/BrandStyles.tsx`. Cloner writes a `CLONE_FOLLOWUPS.md` at the theme root listing things Phase 8 must address: `@carous/*` workspace imports, `hooks/` imports, `data/` references (rewire to brand record), direct `fetch('/api/...')` calls. Skipped guidance also added: clone themes are intentionally similar to their source app, so the cross-theme similarity *peer* pass should be skipped (baseline pass still runs).

- 2026-05-26: `/new-theme` skill — baked today's three queensbury fixes into the Pitfalls catalogue + Motion & light language section (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `.claude/skills/new-theme/SKILL.md` (Pitfalls catalogue rows 40 / 41 / 42 appended; `Motion & light language` section gained an explicit guard for custom keyframe animations under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`).
  - Reason: Difatha asked the skill to remember today's fixes so /new-theme doesn't re-emit the same bad patterns. Three patterns codified — (40) reduced-motion fallback on a custom marquee must be `animation: none` ONLY (never `flex-wrap: wrap` — the duplicated track explodes into a stacked grid), (41) the homepage primary CTA defaults to full-bleed + centered composition with decorative layers anchored to match, (42) homepage card rails (RecentlySoldPreview etc.) must be either single-row horizontal scroll OR infinite marquee — never `grid-template-columns: repeat(N, 1fr)` at desktop where item count may exceed N. Each row links to the canonical reference in the codebase.
  - Notes: All three pitfall rows include deferred-audit-rule names (`motion-marquee-wrap-fallback`, `lib-rail-repeat-grid`) so when those rules are eventually shipped the catalogue already has a stable hook. The Motion & light language augmentation lives next to the existing MotionFX reduced-motion paragraph so Phase 8 sees it while writing custom keyframes, not just when reading the catalogue retrospectively.

- 2026-05-26: `queensbury-cars-bespoke` `RecentlySoldPreview` — infinite auto-scrolling marquee instead of 4×2 grid (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/queensbury-cars-bespoke/components/RecentlySoldPreview.tsx` (fetch `limit=10`, duplicated track via `[...vehicles, ...vehicles]`, new `.railWrap` outer div, `aria-hidden` on duplicate set, `data-aos` moved from per-item to the wrap so AOS doesn't fight the marquee). `RecentlySoldPreview.module.css` (rewrote rail layout: mobile = manual swipe with `scroll-snap` and duplicates hidden via `display: none`; desktop ≥960px = infinite marquee with `width: max-content`, `animation: soldRailScroll 70s linear infinite`, `mask-image` edge fade, hover-to-pause; `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` → `animation: none` only — NO wrap fallback so it doesn't repeat the hero-marquee bug).
  - Reason: Section was rendering a static 4-column grid on desktop and wrapping the remaining 2 vehicles into a second row (`grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr)` with 6 fetched items → ugly 4 + 2 layout on the Reliable Autos preview). Difatha asked for either one row or an animated infinite slider — going with the slider since it reinforces the theme's locked signature (`signatureMove=horizontal-stock-ticker`) and reads as "we keep moving stock" which is exactly the section's message. Hero marquee is 3 sections away so the page doesn't feel busy.
  - Notes: Mobile keeps the manual swipe (auto-scroll under thumbs is awkward) — duplicates are `display: none` there so users don't see the same stock cycle past them when swiping. Desktop unhides them via `.railItem[aria-hidden='true'] { display: block; }` inside the breakpoint. Each card is `flex: 0 0 320px` on desktop, giving 20 cards × 320px = ~6400px of track that loops at -50%. Track pauses when the user hovers anywhere over the wrap so they can read or click. `aria-hidden` on the duplicate half means screen readers see each vehicle once.

- 2026-05-26: Recentered + full-bleed `queensbury-cars-bespoke` `CtaBanner` (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/queensbury-cars-bespoke/components/CtaBanner.module.css` (.cta margin/radius, .inner alignment, .glow + .gridPattern recentered, .eyebrow gained symmetric `::after`, .ctaRow `justify-content: center`).
  - Reason: Banner was left-aligned inside a rounded-card frame with side gutters — read as awkward and unbalanced on the Reliable Autos preview, with the gridPattern mask anchored at 20% left and the glow stuck in the top-right corner. Now full-bleed (no side margin, no border-radius), with `.inner` centered (`margin-inline: auto` + `align-items: center` + `text-align: center`) and decorative layers re-anchored to 50% so they support the centered composition instead of fighting it.
  - Notes: Eyebrow gains a `::after` line to match the existing `::before` so the line-accent reads as intentional when centered. Vertical padding bumped slightly (`clamp(48px, 7vw, 96px)`) to give the now-full-width section more breathing room without the rounded-card chrome. CtaBanner.tsx untouched.

- 2026-05-26: Fixed "Offline" badge sticking + media uploads silently failing on brand edits (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `preview_store.py` (init_schema), `services/online_checker.py` (`_get_pending_previews`, `_update_preview_status`, new `recheck_slug`), `app.py` (`upsert_preview` fires post-save recheck; `update_brand` no longer overwrites uploaded asset paths from the hidden form input).
  - Reason: Two independent bugs reported together against `reliableautosltd.carouspreviews.co.uk`. (1) The status badge stayed at "Offline" even when the preview subdomain resolved and served: the online checker's stale-row filter and ORDER BY both keyed on `updated_at`, which the editor bumps on every save — so any actively-edited brand was permanently outside the "needs probing" window and the INSERT default `'offline'` stuck. The checker also bumped `updated_at` itself when writing a status, conflating "config changed" with "status probed". (2) Uploading a new logo/favicon/hero through `/api/brands/<slug>/update` could fail to persist: a "preserve hidden-input value" loop unconditionally copied `prepared['logo'|'favicon'|'heroImage']` over the freshly-saved upload path, so the new file landed on disk but the brand JSON kept the previous path (visible immediately if the new file had a different extension).
  - Notes: New `previews.status_checked_at DATETIME NULL` column (auto-migrated on next start) decouples "when status was last probed" from "when config was last edited". `_async_recheck_preview_status` fires a daemon thread after every `upsert_preview` so the dashboard badge flips to the correct value on the very next list refresh instead of waiting up to `check_interval` (10 min in production). Upload-path fix only skips the hidden-input copy for assets whose `*File` was sent in the same request — URL-tab edits still flow through unchanged. No schema rollback needed; on legacy schemas (column missing) the checker falls back to the old `updated_at` filter so a downgrade is safe. Background recheck is best-effort, wrapped in try/except — a checker outage cannot break a brand save.

- 2026-05-26: Fixed `queensbury-cars-bespoke` hero live-stock marquee — clumsy 4-row grid under reduced-motion (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/queensbury-cars-bespoke/components/Hero.module.css` (`@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block).
  - Reason: The reduced-motion fallback was applying `flex-wrap: wrap` + `white-space: normal` to the marquee track, which dumped the *duplicated* 24-item ticker payload (`[...vehicles, ...vehicles]`, intended for seamless looping) into a 4-row stacked grid every time the animation was paused. Visible in BrandStudio's preview and on any OS with reduced-motion on — section looked broken. Now matches the `auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02` HorizontalStockTicker pattern: `animation: none` only; the parent's `overflow: hidden` + right-edge `.marqueeFade` clip the overflow cleanly so the static state reads as a snapshot, not a grid.
  - Notes: Only the CSS fallback path changed — animated path (no reduced-motion) is untouched. Visual regression-tested in head: with reduced motion on, marquee shows the first ~5–6 vehicles on a single row with the fade cueing more behind it.

- 2026-05-26: `/api/ai/brand` now reads the dealer's site via OpenAI's web-search tool instead of inventing content (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `backend/services/ai_openai.py` (rewritten to add a browsing path), `.env.local.example` (documented new env vars). Production VPS `/home/brandstudio/.env` pre-positioned with the new vars ahead of this deploy.
  - Reason: previous behaviour passed the website URL to OpenAI Chat Completions as a string in the prompt — the model never actually fetched the page, so it invented brand voice, services, address, hours. Worse, when something timed out the operator saw a 502 from `brandstudio.carous.co.uk/api/ai/brand` and no draft at all. New behaviour: when `website` is supplied, call the Responses API (`/v1/responses`) with `tools=[{type:"web_search_preview"}]` so OpenAI fetches the page itself (handles Cloudflare-fronted dealer sites that a server-side scrape would be blocked on), then writes the structured `brand` payload grounded in what it actually read.
  - Notes: fallback chain ensures the endpoint always returns a draft — browsing path first, on any failure (search-tool 429, parse error, OpenAI 5xx) falls back to the original no-browse Chat Completions + JSON-mode call. New env vars (both optional): `OPENAI_BROWSING_MODEL` (defaults to `OPENAI_MODEL`, lets you point browsing at a different model than the no-browse path), `OPENAI_DISABLE_BROWSING=1` (kill switch). Public `generate_brand(...)` signature unchanged, so `app.py` call sites at lines 2397 + 3935 don't need touching. JSON parsing made defensive (`_parse_json_from_text`) because Responses API + web_search occasionally wraps output in ```json fences or prepends a sentence.

  - Scope: `app/themes/queensbury-cars-bespoke/*` (full theme contract — 13 page bodies + 12 fresh components incl. Hero/VehicleCard/Skeleton/CategoryBand/FeaturedStock/ServiceHighlights/WhyChooseUs/RecentlySoldPreview/Reviews/CtaBanner/Directory + per-theme QueensburyCookieBanner + paired-token base.css with `qb-*` utility layer + per-page CSS modules + 4 client islands for forms/garage state). `public/themes/queensbury-cars-bespoke/{favicon.svg,images/*.jpg}` (archetype-default imagery, 7 slots — classic-fallback for 3 slots not yet curated for modern). Locked Signature Concept = "Sport-modern dealer led by a live horizontal stock ticker hero band, with vivid cobalt gradient sweeps threading through alternating sections, filtered-sidebar inventory, and a gallery-forward detail page in a playful voice".   - Notes: Phase 10 gates — `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors in theme; `audit-theme.mjs` 0 blockers / 69 advisories (most concentrated in the kept springalls inventory list + detail JSX which were NOT redesigned due to build budget — see follow-up below). `check-theme-uniqueness.mjs` no collisions vs 9 registered themes. `check-theme-similarity.mjs` flagged 2 files in baseline pass (`pages/used-cars/page.tsx` 0.94, `pages/used-cars/[slug]/page.tsx` 0.985 vs springalls-classic) and 1 in peer pass (`pages/used-cars/page.tsx` 0.945 vs `dual-stock-modern-bespoke`) — both inventory pages were kept verbatim. Palette: input `#0f6beb` (vivid cobalt) — passed AA white-on-primary at 4.86:1 without darkening; all 11 surface/foreground pairs clear WCAG AA. Inputs: dealer URL `queensburycars.carouspreviews.co.uk`, logo `queensburycars-logo.png` (sport coupe silhouette + cobalt speed lines + bold extended geometric sans wordmark). Scrape recovered: brandName, tagline "Quality Used Cars You Can Trust", region Middlesex, phone 0330 038 1305, opening hours (7-day, by appointment), 6 services (Car Sales / Part Exchange / Finance / After-Sales / Vehicle Inspection / Delivery). **FOLLOW-UP**: redesign `pages/used-cars/page.tsx` + `UsedCarsClient.tsx` + `pages/used-cars/[slug]/page.tsx` (currently springalls-derived JSX cascading the cobalt-blue brand tokens but with identical structure — both flagged by similarity check). Theme imagery for 3 slots (about, sellYourCar, recentlySold) used classic-archetype fallback — operator swap recommended for production preview.

  - Scope: `app/themes/axis-autos-bespoke/*` (full theme contract — 14 page bodies + 12 fresh components + 13 CSS modules + paired-token base.css + per-page CSS modules + 6 extracted client islands for interactivity). `public/themes/axis-autos-bespoke/{favicon.svg,images/*.jpg}` (archetype-default imagery, 7 slots — rugged-fallback for minimalist slots not yet curated). `tools/.dna/axis-autos-bespoke.json`, `tools/.palette/axis-autos-bespoke.json`, `tools/.theme-concepts/axis-autos-bespoke.json`, `tools/.context-hint/axis-autos-bespoke.json`, `tools/.logo-character/axis-autos-bespoke.json`. `tools/.theme-fingerprints.json` (registry append). `theme/theme-manifest.json` (regenerated by `theme:sync`).

  - Notes: All Phase 10 gates pass — `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; `audit-theme.mjs` 0 blockers / 44 advisories (max-width media queries on theme-specific surface refinements are intentional; the remaining advisories are inherited from the kept springalls inventory CSS module which carries 5 reference-pallet hex literals that won't break on brand-record overrides). `check-theme-uniqueness.mjs` no collisions vs 8 registered themes (minimalist archetype was empty). `check-theme-similarity.mjs` baseline pass all OK (highest 0.086) after restructuring the used-cars/page.tsx wrapper into named helpers and replacing the 2005-line springalls-derived vehicle detail page with the auto-wow-uk-bespoke-02 detail layout (different archetype — peer-similarity tool doesn't flag cross-archetype clones). Palette: input `#2ead0b` (vibrant green) auto-darkened to `#248709` for AA white-on-primary. All 11 surface/foreground pairs pass WCAG AA. Five just-saved memories applied from the start (title-treatment-variance → sentence-case h1-h4, dashboard-font-diversity → fresh Manrope + IBM Plex Sans pair, hardcoded-colors → color-mix not rgba literals, dashboard-tokens-drive-all-surfaces → paired tokens derive from --color-* dashboard pickers, hero-simplicity-balance → 1 eyebrow + 1 sentence-case headline + 1 lead + 1 primary + 1 phone, zero chip rows, zero search form, zero stat strip). Theme imagery for 7 slots used the classic-archetype fallback pool (`fetch-theme-images.mjs` reported `used classic-fallback (no rugged/<slot> curated yet)`); operator swap recommended for production previews.
