- 2026-05-12: Added `dual-stock-modern-bespoke` theme — modern dual-stock theme for UK dealers selling cars AND motorbikes side by side, inspired by columbusvehicles.uk's typography + dark canvas, data shape mirrored from carous-platform/apps/thebikebuyer (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: NEW `app/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/*` (full theme contract ~55 files) — bespoke Header (announcement bar + top contact bar + sticky main + mobile drawer + search overlay), Footer (3-band: CTA → 4-col mega → legal+Carous credit), DualStockCookieBanner (per-theme, full-width bottom dock with prefs toggle), Hero (cars/bikes/all type tabs + reg lookup + make+price selects + glow blob + grid drift), StatsBar (live count of cars + bikes + years), FeaturedStock (parallel Cars + Bikes rails), ServiceHighlights (6-card grid covering both stock types), BrandTrust (chip cluster — 10 car makes + 10 bike makes), Testimonials (3-card grid with car/bike buyer badges), CtaBand (sell-your-car / sell-your-bike split CTA), RecentlySoldRail (dark band, sold treatment via VehicleCard `sold` prop), universal VehicleCard (bike vs car aware specs: engine_size + body for bikes; transmission + doors for cars; price + monthly HP estimate; wishlist + compare buttons + type/featured badges). Inventory list page (`/used-cars`) rewritten — kept the data-layer page.tsx server fetch + state machine, redesigned UsedCarsClient.tsx render layer with All / Cars / Bikes tabs that filter by `vehicleClass` (derived in normalizer from declared type → bike make whitelist → bike body whitelist). Vehicle detail page (`/used-cars/<slug>`) — full-bleed mosaic gallery (main + 4-thumb mosaic + lightbox), 6-cell quick-specs strip (dark band), bike-vs-car-aware specifications table + finance block, sticky sidebar with price + Enquire (mounts shared `<EnquiryModal>`) + Call/Email/WhatsApp grid (using official `<WhatsAppIcon>` not generic chat glyph), sticky mobile action bar, Similar vehicles row (filtered by same make), Browse-by-make SEO panel with 12 dealer make chips before footer. 14 inner pages — about (story + values + stats), contact (4 channels + 4 topic chips + hours table + EnquiryModal), services (separate car + bike service grids), finance (3-step flow + 4 finance products), part-exchange (cross-category trade-in), sell-your-car (mounts global `<SellYourCarWidget>` via SellYourCarMount client island), compare (side-by-side table for up to 3 vehicles), wishlist (saved vehicles list), privacy-policy + cookie-policy (legal copy), recently-sold (filtered All/Cars/Bikes tabs).
  - Plumbing: BrandStyles.tsx rewritten to canonical 3-tier image fallback (Pitfall 38c — `brand.heroImage` first for hero slot, then `brand.images.<slot>`, then theme-default jpg layered via CSS `var(--brand-image-X, none)` over `url('/themes/.../<slot>.jpg')`). All 7 image slots emit `--brand-image-*` vars; no cross-fall between slots. base.css scoped under `[data-theme-id='dual-stock-modern-bespoke']` for every rule (no global bleed); blanket `:where(a) { color: ... }` removed (Pitfall 8); `[hidden] { display: none !important }` added; AOS reveal rules inlined (18 variants) with `prefers-reduced-motion` honored. Inventory normalizer extended with `vehicleClass: 'car' | 'bike'` discriminator + `engineSize` + `enginePower` optional fields. Bike-make whitelist (Harley-Davidson, Yamaha, Honda, Ducati, Kawasaki, BMW, KTM, Suzuki, Triumph, Aprilia, MV Agusta, Royal Enfield, Indian, Piaggio, Vespa) and bike-body whitelist (naked, sport, sportbike, cruiser, tourer, adventure, scooter, motocross, enduro, cafe racer, trike, scrambler, streetfighter) drive class detection when API doesn't declare `type`.
  - Reason: Difatha's `/new-theme --context "Inspired by https://columbusvehicles.uk, fetch context from there, fonts, images, but i need a modern theme, that can handle data for cars and bikes, for data reference check thebikebuyer app in carous platform... everything med up together and fully compatible with carous platform"` — needed a reusable brandstudio theme covering the bikes+cars dealer scenario. Columbus Vehicles is actually a 4X4 specialist (not bikes+cars), so used purely for visual inspiration (Roboto Condensed + Open Sans typography, #161616 dark canvas). thebikebuyer (carous-platform sibling app) supplied the bikes+cars data shape: parallel `/inventory/cars` + `/inventory/bikes` routes with type-aware normalizer surfacing engine_size + body_type for bikes alongside transmission + doors for cars. Brandstudio's canonical routes whitelist forbids new top-level URLs (`/inventory/bikes` would 404), so the dual stock is handled inside `/used-cars` with a `?type=bike` URL filter — backwards-compatible with the existing API.
  - Notes: 7 page-level images sourced via `fetch-theme-images.mjs` (curated Unsplash modern archetype; 3 slots fall back to classic-archetype pool — about, sellYourCar, recentlySold — until the curated catalogue covers more modern slots). Favicon: archetype-aware SVG mark with glyph "D" rendered at `public/themes/dual-stock-modern-bespoke/favicon.svg`. Color: user-supplied `#e10600` racing red — all 11 contrast pairs pass AA out of the gate (no darkening needed); secondary `#4b5563` (gray) for section tints; accent `#0ea5e9` (electric cyan) for interaction hover/decoration. Verifications: `tsc --noEmit` clean against zero baseline; `audit-theme.mjs` reports 0 blockers / 43 advisories (most are intentional dark-tier neutrals `#0a0e14` / `#14181f` / `#181d27` deliberately kept for the dark dealer-signage canvas — these match the canonical neutralsDark tier from `check-palette-policy.mjs` output, but are written as literals in CSS rather than via tokens; can be tokenized later if `--surface-bg-dark` is exposed via BrandStyles); `check-theme-similarity.mjs` flags only the kept inventory `page.tsx` data-layer wrapper at 0.945 vs springalls-classic (expected — that file is the verbatim server fetch wrapper Phase 8 keeps; the redesigned `UsedCarsClient.tsx` scores 0.003); palette-policy generator re-confirms all pairs pass AA. The theme is shipped status `experimental` and will appear in the dashboard `/create` picker after `git push` deploys.

- 2026-05-12: Remote preview-creation pipeline — `/new-theme` skill can register production previews via API after pushing theme code (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: NEW `POST /api/v1/preview/create` endpoint in `app.py` (~200 lines including helpers). Authenticates via `Authorization: Bearer ${BRANDSTUDIO_API_KEY}` env var with `hmac.compare_digest` constant-time compare; returns 503 when env var is unset (endpoint disabled). Validates `themeId` is a safe slug + deployed (folder exists in `app/themes/`) — returns `404 + code:"theme_not_deployed"` with retry hint when not. Validates `brand` payload, derives `slug` from name if absent, regex `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,62}$`, returns `409 + code:"slug_conflict"` on duplicate (unless `?force=true`). Optional AI auto-fill: if request includes `ai.enabled=true`, loads the theme's `recipes/text-recipe.json` and calls `generate_brand_via_openai` (same path the dashboard `/api/ai/brand` uses) to populate `brand.text` per recipe field. ONLY merges `brand.text` from AI response — operator-provided logo/contact/address are immutable. AI failures are non-fatal (returns `aiError` in response). Persists via `upsert_preview` (same store as dashboard `/create`); returns `{ ok, slug, themeId, url, created, overwritten, aiPopulated, aiError }`. NEW `_resolve_preview_url_for_slug()` helper composes the live URL from `brand.preview_url` → `brand.domain` (https-wrapped) → `https://<slug>.<PREVIEW_BASE_DOMAIN env, defaults carouspreviews.co.uk>` fallback.
  - NEW `tools/create-preview.mjs` (~270 lines) Node CLI helper using built-in `node:util` parseArgs. Reads `BRANDSTUDIO_API_URL` + `BRANDSTUDIO_API_KEY` from env (or `--api-url` / `--api-key` flags). Builds a minimal BrandConfig from CLI flags (`--name`, `--slug`, `--theme`, `--domain`, `--hex`, `--logo`, `--favicon`, `--hero`, `--phone`, `--email`, `--address-line1/2`, `--city`, `--county`, `--postcode`, optional `--ai --context --website`) OR loads a pre-built record from `--brand-json <path>`. POSTs to `/api/v1/preview/create`. Retries on `code:"theme_not_deployed"` every 30s up to 10 times by default (configurable with `--retries` / `--retry-delay`) — covers the natural race between git push and production deploy. Exit codes: `0` success / `2` env+flag missing / `3` slug conflict (advises `--force`) / `4` auth failure / `5` server error / `99` uncaught.
  - UPDATED `.claude/skills/new-theme/SKILL.md` Phase 12b to add a third option ("Yes — push theme + register a production preview via remote API") and a new **Phase 13c** block documenting the env vars, invocation pattern, exit codes, retry policy, and constraints (theme must be deployed before API call resolves; the 5-min default retry window covers most deploys; AI auto-fill is optional but strongly recommended; dashboard `/update/<slug>` remains the iteration surface after create). 1 new feedback memory (`feedback_remote_preview_create_pipeline.md`) + MEMORY.md index updated.
  - Reason: Difatha: "remotely be able to create previews locally, like when theme is created, there is a question skills asks me to either create a preview or not, when create preview is selected, i want to be able to create the preview and automatically publish it on production... expose a brandstudio api that enables users to locally create previews with selected theme... invent the best error free effective pipeline for this." The existing Phase 13a flow runs locally against a local MySQL — useful for dev, but doesn't ship production previews. The new Phase 13c posts to the production API after the theme code lands on the server, returns the live URL. The pipeline is "error-free" by design: explicit retry on theme-not-yet-deployed (the race the operator would otherwise hit), 409-with-force-flag for slug conflicts, AI auto-fill never overwrites operator-provided facts, all failures return actionable error codes, no silent state changes. Themes built via `/new-theme` become a single end-to-end flow — operator runs the skill, picks "Yes via remote API", gets a live preview URL back.
  - Notes: Production must set `BRANDSTUDIO_API_KEY` env var to enable the endpoint (without it, returns 503 — opt-in for security). Bearer token compared with `hmac.compare_digest` not `==` (no timing leaks). The endpoint's response always includes `aiPopulated` + `aiError` so the caller knows whether AI ran. The CLI's `--brand-json <path>` flag round-trips a pre-built record so operators can hand-edit a brand JSON file (e.g. from the dashboard's export) and POST it as-is. Future hardening: per-user API keys with rotation + a settings UI; currently V1 uses a single global key.

- 2026-05-12: Per-theme text recipes — dashboard Component Text tab, AI prompt context, runtime override resolution (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: NEW `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/recipes/text-recipe.json` — 10 sections × ~30 fields declaring every customizable copy string (topbar announcement, hero eyebrow/title/lead/CTA, edition strip 3 lines, 4 trust-band reasons × label+detail, pull-quote + attribution, footer brand lead + appointment note, about hero+story×2, contact hero, finance hero+disclaimer, sell-your-car hero). Each field has key/label/type (short/long)/default (with `{tokens}`)/maxLength/aiHint. NEW canonical recipe schema (sections → fields with token interpolation). REPLACED `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/lib/brand-text.ts` — now imports the JSON recipe, exposes `resolveText(brand, key)` + `resolveTexts(brand, keys[])` that prefer `brand.text[key]` overrides and fall back to recipe defaults with `{token}` interpolation (`{brandName}`, `{city}`, `{cityish}`, `{streetLine}`, `{postcode}`, `{year}`, `{tagline}`, `{namePossessive}`); legacy `getBrandText(brand)` accessor kept for backward compat. Wired components to use `resolveText`: `Hero.tsx` (heroEyebrow/Title1/TitleAccent/Lead/SearchCtaLabel), `TrustBand.tsx` (rewritten to iterate trust1-trust4 from recipe slots), `pages/finance/page.tsx` (financeDisclaimer). NEW Flask endpoint `GET /api/themes/<id>/text-recipe` in `app.py` returning the recipe JSON for a theme (empty-sections payload for themes without a recipe). UPDATED `app.py` save handler — iterates form keys with `text_` prefix, persists them as `brand['text'][<recipeKey>]`; empty strings drop the key so the recipe default re-applies. UPDATED `backend/services/ai_openai.py` `generate_brand()` accepts `text_recipe` param; new `_text_recipe_prompt_block()` formats every recipe field with `aiHint`+`maxLength`+`default` into the prompt; `_response_structure` now declares `"text": {}` so the model knows where to put values; new rule forces the model to emit every recipe key. UPDATED `app.py /api/ai/brand` endpoint — loads the recipe for the selected `preferred_theme_id` and passes through to the AI service. UPDATED `templates/update.html` + `templates/index.html` — new "Component Text" tab button + tab pane with `[data-text-recipe-host]` container that the new `initializeComponentTextTab()` JS hydrates by fetching `/api/themes/<id>/text-recipe`, rendering inputs (`<input>` for short, `<textarea>` for long) named `text_<recipeKey>`, with existing brand.text values pre-filled from `data-text-recipe-existing` JSON attr. Tab re-loads recipe whenever the theme selector changes. 1 new feedback memory (`feedback_per_theme_text_recipe_contract.md`) + MEMORY.md index updated.
  - Reason: Difatha: "components texts customizable as we made with images per theme, also since previews creation is automated process with ai, we need to let ai have the best contexts and what exactly to return for that theme selected, in short use recipes to let ai know what is expected, so that way the theme is highly customizable per preview and won't contain hard coded texts." Three problems addressed at once: (1) the previous round of "purge hardcoded strings" gave us `lib/brand-text.ts` with a few structured accessors but components still mixed hardcoded copy with helper calls — every theme would need a custom helper file. (2) The AI brand generator's prompt schema didn't include any per-theme copy fields, so AI-generated previews fell back to the theme's hardcoded defaults regardless of dealer context. (3) Operators editing a preview in `/create` or `/update` had no UI to override copy strings — only images had per-page cards. The recipe is the single source of truth that solves all three: dashboard renders form fields from it, AI gets it as prompt context with aiHints, runtime reads brand.text overrides + recipe defaults. Themes ship reusable to any dealer; AI populates fields with on-brand copy based on aiHints; operators fine-tune in the dashboard.
  - Notes: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean for kain-motors-bespoke. JSON import works via tsconfig's `resolveJsonModule: true`. Recipe defaults use `{tokens}` not literal city/brand strings — the runtime token interpolator falls back to `'the showroom'` for `{brandName}` and `'the showroom'` for `{cityish}` so a misconfigured Columbus Vehicles preview rendering through kain-motors-bespoke degrades to "Appointment-only showroom" not "Appointment-only Manchester showroom". Form field naming convention: `text_<recipeKey>` so the save handler just iterates `data` keys with the prefix — no per-theme code needed. AI prompt embeds the recipe as a section-grouped listing with each field's `aiHint`+`maxLength`+`default` so the model has full context. The recipe is the contract between three subsystems (dashboard / AI / runtime) — adding a new field to any theme means editing the JSON once and the three subsystems pick it up automatically. Phase 8 of `/new-theme` should generate `recipes/text-recipe.json` for every new theme as a hard requirement; the empty-sections fallback in the API endpoint covers themes that haven't shipped a recipe yet.

- 2026-05-12: Purged all hardcoded dealer strings from `kain-motors-bespoke` — every name/city/address routes through `lib/brand-text` + `lib/contact` so the theme is reusable across brands without leaking the seed dealer's identity (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: NEW `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/lib/brand-text.ts` — canonical `getBrandText(brand)` helper returning brand-driven copy strings: `name`, `namePossessive`, `tagline`, `curationLabel`, `editionLine(vol)`, `showroomTagline`, `showroomLocationLabel`, `heroEditionEyebrow`, `founderAttribution`; every fallback is generic ("the showroom", "Showroom Curation"), never the seed dealer's name. EXTENDED `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/lib/contact.ts` `BrandContactInfo` with `city`, `county`, `postcode`, `streetLine`, `locationLabel`, `cityOrShowroom` so location parts can be composed without re-reading `brand.location.address` in every component. Updated components: `Header.tsx` (announcement bar uses `${city} showroom` fallback; brand link aria-label now `brand?.name || 'Home'`; topbar chip uses contact.city + contact.postcode), `Footer.tsx` (brand lead uses `text.showroomTagline`; address falls back to "Address available on request" not Midlands Street/Manchester/M12 6LB; copyright uses `text.name`), `EditionStrip.tsx` (rewritten client; all 3 strip lines from helpers), `Hero.tsx` (eyebrow → `text.heroEditionEyebrow`; lead uses `contact.streetLine` for "showroom on X street"), `FeaturedAcquisitions.tsx` (cover byline → `Curated by ${text.name} · ${currentEditionLabel()}` with auto-season label), `PullQuote.tsx` (rewritten client; attribution defaults to `text.founderAttribution`), `TrustBand.tsx` (rewritten client; aria-label uses `text.name`; "Finance from 9.9% APR" generalised to "Finance from competitive rates"), `Reviews.tsx` (rewritten client; review bodies template brand name + city; review locations conditionally show city), `BrandLogo.tsx` (wordmark fallback "Showroom" not "Kain Motors"). Updated pages: `about/page.tsx` (hero title → `${cityish} independent`; body uses `streetClause` from contact.streetLine), `contact/page.tsx` (hero lead uses contact-built addrLine; address falls back to "Address available on request"), `finance/page.tsx` (FCA disclaimer uses `text.name` as Appointed Representative), `sell-your-car/page.tsx` (hero title uses `cityish` → "The ${City} showroom buys cars"), `sell-your-car/SellYourCarMount.tsx` (widget subtitle uses `text.showroomLocationLabel`), `privacy-policy/page.tsx` (registered address uses `contact.showroomAddress` with "(address available on request)" fallback), `cookie-policy/page.tsx` + `used-cars/[slug]/page.tsx` brand-name fallbacks changed from `'Kain Motors'` → `'The showroom'`, `used-cars/UsedCarsClient.tsx` (hero h1 composes "Used Cars in ${contact.city}, ${contact.county}" empty-aware; lead names `${brandText.name}`; location chip conditional). FIXED `lib/inventory.ts` stale autowow-port leak — `normalizeInventoryItem` was hardcoding `'Reading, Berkshire'` as the location fallback in two places; both replaced with empty string so the rendering layer decides what to show when API doesn't return a town. 1 new feedback memory (`feedback_brand_text_helper_no_hardcoded_dealer_strings.md`) + MEMORY.md index updated.
  - Reason: Difatha called this out: "if user uses the theme for another brand preview, these hard coded brands, locations etc that are dealer specific should be customizable fully as that is the work of dashboard /create or /update pages, that needs to be highly respected." Audit found ~25 hardcoded references across components and pages — "Kain Motors" / "Manchester" / "Midlands Street" / "M12 6LB" / "Reading, Berkshire" (the inventory.ts one being a stale autowow-port leak) — each one leaks the seed dealer's identity onto every preview that reuses the theme. The carous-platform pattern is one theme → many dealers; the dashboard `/create` + `/update` flows OWN the brand record fields and themes MUST respect that by routing all dealer-specific copy through helpers.
  - Notes: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean for kain-motors-bespoke. Fallback strategy is deliberately GENERIC ("the showroom" / "Address available on request" / "Showroom Curation") — never the seed dealer's name. Reason: a misconfigured Columbus Vehicles preview degrading to "Kain Motors" actively misleads buyers; degrading to "the showroom" is neutral. Also: seed-dealer fallbacks would survive copy/paste when the theme is reused as a template for new themes — "Kain Motors" strings could persist 3 generations into derivative themes. Memory captures the audit checklist (~25 grep targets) plus the rule "fallbacks are generic, never the seed dealer's name" so `/new-theme` Phase 10c enforces it on every future theme.

- 2026-05-12: Basic Info tab — Brand Assets (logo, favicon, hero) now accept external URLs via the same Upload | URL card pattern (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `static/brand-form.css` — new `.brand-asset-grid` + `.page-image-card--asset` / `--logo` / `--favicon` / `--hero` modifiers (transparency-checkerboard tile background for logo+favicon so PNG alpha is visible, `object-fit: contain` instead of `cover` so wordmarks aren't cropped, 1:1 aspect for favicon, 4:3 for logo). `templates/update.html` + `templates/index.html` — Brand Assets section in the Basic Info tab rewritten: legacy 3-col `form-grid` of file-upload-zones replaced with `.brand-asset-grid` of 3 `.page-image-card--asset` cards (Logo / Favicon / Hero Background), each with header chip ("Header · footer · mobile drawer · OG image" for Logo, "Browser tab · bookmarks · home-screen icon" for Favicon, "Home page hero band" for Hero) + Upload | URL tab toggle + URL input that writes directly to the canonical hidden `#logo` / `#favicon` / `#heroImage` field (the same field the server already reads in `FormHandler.createBrandConfig`'s `data.get('logo')` chain) + source-tag + Reset-to-default. Inventory file kept in its legacy form-group below — it's a JSON file, not an image. New `initializeBrandAssetPicker()` in both templates: same shape as `initializePageImagePicker` but additionally syncs URL input ↔ hidden input, clears the hidden field on Reset/Remove/Clear so stale paths don't persist across saves, and falls back to "stored on brand" source tag when the hidden value is a relative path (uploaded historically) vs "remote URL" for absolute http(s) URLs.
  - Reason: Difatha asked for external URL support on Basic Info brand assets — previously the only way to set logo/favicon/hero was uploading a file. URL paste matters for: (a) prospects where the dealer's logo already lives on a CDN we can hotlink, (b) creating preview brands quickly from public assets without staging local uploads, (c) the `/new-theme` SKILL's logo-cache flow where the logo gets fetched into `tools/.logo-cache/<slug>.png` but could also be pointed at the source URL. Server side already supported it (hidden inputs `#logo` `#favicon` `#heroImage` flow through `FormHandler.createBrandConfig` and are picked up by `data.get('logo')` etc.) — the gap was purely UI surfacing. Theme consumers already handle absolute URLs (bespoke themes render `<img src={brand.logo}>` with plain `<img>` tags, no Next/Image `remotePatterns` whitelist needed).
  - Notes: `tsc --noEmit` clean. Server contract unchanged — same field names (`logoFile`/`logo`, `faviconFile`/`favicon`, `heroImageFile`/`heroImage`), same precedence (uploaded file overrides URL via `app.py:3170-3175`). Bespoke themes' `<img>` tags consume URLs directly without `remotePatterns` whitelist (verified `next/image` not imported in `showroom-shine-cars-bespoke`). Transparency-checkerboard tile pattern on logo+favicon cards is a CSS-only `linear-gradient` quad so it works at any size without an image asset. Favicon tile caps at 220px wide on desktop to keep the square preview proportional next to its 4:3 logo neighbour.

- 2026-05-12: Rebuilt `kain-motors-bespoke` vehicle detail page with modern structure — hero band + dark quick-specs strip + sectioned content + sticky sidebar (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/pages/used-cars/[slug]/page.tsx` — full rewrite. New structure: breadcrumb → hero band (title strip ABOVE gallery + full-container-width gallery mosaic) → dark quick-specs band (6 icon-led cells: Year/Mileage/Fuel/Transmission/Body/BHP) → 2-col detail grid (1.7fr content + 1fr sticky sidebar) → similar vehicles → mobile sticky bar + lightbox + EnquiryModal. Title strip carries eyebrow + h1 + meta chip row (reg plate + Stock# pill + In-stock status badge with pulse dot) + Save/Compare actions; gallery is hero image (min-height 460px desktop) + 2×2 thumb mosaic + "+N more photos" cell spanning both cols in row 3 (dashed gold border, opens lightbox); content column is sectioned Overview / Features (3-col grid on wide, gold check bullets) / Specifications (card-bordered 2-col `<dl>` grid) / Finance feature card (dark with gold gradient blob, italic "From £x/mo" headline). Sidebar reorganised: price block on top (eyebrow + 2.6rem price + monthly), full-width primary CTA, 3-cell contact grid (Call/WhatsApp/Email with WhatsApp in official green), divider, 2-col key-facts list, italic appointment note. Inline SVG icon set replaces lucide for the quick-specs band — keeps the page self-contained. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/pages/used-cars/[slug]/page.module.css` — full rewrite (~700 lines) implementing the new structure: `.heroBand` light-gradient bg, `.titleStrip` flex row on ≥720px, `.gallery` grid `1.55fr / 1fr` on ≥900px with `.galleryMain` `aspect-ratio: auto; min-height: 460px`, `.galleryThumbs` 2x2 with `.thumbMore` spanning cols, `.quickSpecs` dark band with gold left-border per `.qsItem`, `.detailGrid` `1.7fr / 1fr`, `.specGrid` card-bordered with alternating right-borders, `.financeStrip` dark feature card with gold corner gradient, `.sidebar` sticky at top:100px on ≥1024px, `.contactGrid` 3 equal cells. Removed deprecated `.titleBlock` / `.statRow` / `.descriptionSection` / `.featuresSection` / `.titleActions` / `.thumbOverlay` / `.financeStrip` (old) / `.contactWhats` / `.contactRow` / `.makesSection` blocks since the makes panel mounts in Shell once site-wide per existing memory. 1 new feedback memory + MEMORY.md index updated.
  - Reason: Difatha QA'd the kain-motors-bespoke detail page and called it "so cheap and poor" — gallery was wedged inside the left column of a 2-col grid (cramped ~60% of container width), title rendered below the gallery (so users saw 460px of car photos before knowing what they were looking at), key specs were buried inside the spec table further down (Year/Mileage/Fuel/Transmission not visible above the fold), sidebar was an unstructured stack of stock# / location / warranty / delivery competing with the CTAs, and the gallery thumbnails included an "Image not available" placeholder slot that looked like a 404. The user asked for "modern UI/UX, full width gallery for this grided like currently but even better designs, overhaul redesign of full page" — so the rebuild promotes the title strip ABOVE the gallery, expands the gallery to full container width, lifts the 6 key specs into a separate dark icon-led band between hero and content, splits the content column into 4 named sections (Overview / Features / Specifications / Finance feature card), and reorganises the sidebar around price + primary CTA + 3-cell contact grid + key facts.
  - Notes: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean for kain-motors-bespoke. Inline SVG icon set (`const Icon = { Calendar, Gauge, Fuel, Gear, Car, Bolt, Heart, Compare, Expand, Phone, Mail, Check }`) replaces lucide dependency for the detail page — keeps it self-contained and tree-shakes one less library off the route. Specs grid uses `grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr` with `border-right` on odd cells and `border-bottom` on every row — creates a clean alternating cell-separator visual that reads as a real spec table. Finance feature card has a gold corner gradient blob (`radial-gradient` clipped by `overflow: hidden`) that provides visual richness without competing with the content. Mobile sticky bar has `right: 80px` to clear the WhatsApp FAB. Memory captured as `feedback_vehicle_detail_modern_structure.md` so `/new-theme` generates this structure for every future theme. The makes-SEO panel removed from the detail page (already mounts site-wide in Shell per `feedback_browse_by_make_shell.md`).

- 2026-05-12: Per-page Images tab redesigned as card grid + "Where it shows" mapping to bespoke-theme page bands (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `static/brand-form.css` (+330 lines `.page-image-grid` / `.page-image-card` block — 16:9 preview tile, Upload | URL tab toggle, status chip, source-tag footer, reset-to-default, drag-drop "Drop to upload" overlay; overrides default `.file-upload-area` chrome so the card owns border/radius/hover). `templates/update.html` + `templates/index.html` — Page Images tab markup rewritten: legacy 2-col `form-grid` of upload-zone-plus-URL-input replaced with `.page-image-grid` of `data-page-image-card` cards. Each card carries a header (image icon + slot title + "Shows in <strong>About page hero</strong>" caption + "Set/Default" chip), the existing `.file-upload-area` retained verbatim inside `.page-image-card__preview` so `app.js` `initializeFileUpload` (drag-drop, click-to-pick) and `clearFile` keep working, then a body section with Upload | URL `role="tab"` toggle, file-meta chip showing filename + size after pick, URL input with Clear button, and a footer source-tag (`uploaded` / `remote URL` / `stored on brand` / `theme default`) + Reset-to-default button. New `initializePageImagePicker()` in both templates: wires tab switching, file-meta updates, URL→preview live mirror (typed URL sets `<img>.src` and shows preview tile), upload-overrides-URL guard mirroring server precedence, reset clears both inputs + hides preview + flips state. The Jinja slot tuple now carries 4 fields — `(slug, label, where, caption)` — where `where` names the exact bespoke-theme page band (e.g. "Services page hero") and `caption` suggests the image content (e.g. "Service-bay, MOT bay, or detailing photo").
  - Reason: Difatha asked for a finer UI/UX on the per-page image picker and to "make them effective on respective bespoke theme areas". The old picker was a stack of upload-zones with no preview-context — operators couldn't tell which image landed where, and the upload zone doubled as the preview surface so a 16:9 hero photo got rendered cramped inside a 200-tall dotted-border box. The card layout matches the actual deployment shape (16:9 hero band per page), the "Shows in <where>" caption maps slot → bespoke-theme band directly (already plumbed via `--brand-image-about` etc. in `app/themes/{kain,auto-wow,showroom}/styles/base.css` per `feedback_brand_image_plumbing.md`), and the Upload | URL tab toggle gives URL pasting equal weight instead of treating it as a fallback hidden below the upload zone. The redesign was scoped to the picker only — server endpoints (`{slot}ImageFile` + `image{Slot}Url` form fields), `app.py` PAGE_IMAGE_SLOTS handling, and theme-side `BrandStyles.tsx` CSS-var emission are all unchanged because the plumbing was already correct; the bug was in surfacing the choice to operators.
  - Notes: `tsc --noEmit` clean (no new errors against the changes; pre-existing errors in `gilded-drive` remain untouched). Server contract preserved: same input names (`{slot}ImageFile` + `image{Slot}Url`), same precedence (uploads override URLs in `app.py:3193-3196`). Card flips `data-has-image='true'` for stateful styling (subtle indigo border on set cards). Mobile (≤640px) collapses grid to single column. Bespoke themes' `--brand-image-{slot}` CSS vars (set in `BrandStyles.tsx` from `brand.images.<slot>`) are unchanged — a fresh upload propagates via the existing dashboard save → brand record → `BrandStyles` render flow.

- 2026-05-12: Expanded font-pack catalog from 6 to 14 curated pairings + Custom (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `templates/update.html` + `templates/index.html` — added 8 new packs to `getFontPacks()` and to the hidden `<select id="fontPack">` option list: `editorial-bold` (Bebas Neue / DM Sans), `soft-modern` (Cormorant Garamond / Poppins), `workshop` (Anton / Karla), `urban-sport` (Rajdhani / Inter), `premium-ev` (Outfit / Inter), `heritage-print` (EB Garamond / Lato), `showroom-grit` (Teko / Work Sans), `forge-display` (Archivo Black / IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono for the only non-default mono pairing). `templates/base.html` Google Fonts `<link>` rewritten to preload the 12 new families (Anton, Archivo Black, Bebas Neue, Cormorant Garamond, EB Garamond, IBM Plex Mono, IBM Plex Sans, Karla, Outfit, Poppins, Rajdhani, Teko, Work Sans) alongside the existing 12 so picker tiles render in real typography on first paint.
  - Reason: 6 packs felt thin once the visual tile grid landed — the grid invited browsing but only had 7 cells to scan. Eight more pairings, each with a distinct personality (editorial all-caps, soft serif boutique, chunky garage, angular sport, geometric EV, classic book, condensed showroom, brutalist display), make the picker feel like a real catalog without crossing into overload territory.
  - Notes: All new fonts exist on Google Fonts and load via the single base.html `<link>`. Bespoke themes' `app/lib/googleFonts.ts` extracts the first concrete family from each new stack just like the original 6 — no helper changes needed; `Bebas Neue`, `Anton`, `Teko`, `Rajdhani`, `Outfit`, `Karla`, `Poppins`, `EB Garamond`, `Cormorant Garamond`, `Archivo Black`, `IBM Plex Sans`, `Work Sans` are all concrete names that pass the generic-family skip-list. `forge-display` is the first pack to override `mono` to something other than JetBrains Mono — kept consistent with IBM Plex family for visual coherence.

- 2026-05-12: Brand-record font picks now actually load on bespoke themes + visual font-pack picker in `/create` and `/update` (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/lib/googleFonts.ts` (new) — `extractGoogleFontName`, `buildGoogleFontsUrl`, `buildGoogleFontsImport` helpers. All three bespoke themes' `BrandStyles.tsx` (`showroom-shine-cars-bespoke`, `kain-motors-bespoke`, `auto-wow-uk-bespoke`) now prepend a dynamic `@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?...')` to their injected `<style>` block, derived from `brand.theme.fonts.ui` + `brand.theme.fonts.brand`. `templates/base.html` adds a single Google Fonts `<link>` covering every pack font so the dashboard preview tiles render in their real typefaces (with `preconnect` for Google Fonts + gstatic). `static/brand-form.css` gains a `~210-line `.font-pack-picker` / `.font-pack-grid` / `.font-pack-card` block. Both `templates/update.html` and `templates/index.html` replace the legacy single `<select id="fontPack">` with a responsive card grid (`.font-pack-grid`) — each card shows the pack's brand glyph in its actual brand font + UI glyph in its actual UI font + pack label + font names + an active-state checkmark; original `<select>` stays in DOM as `.visually-hidden` so existing `initializeFontPacks` / form-submit / `syncFontPackFromHidden` plumbing keeps working unchanged. `initializeFontPacks` extended in both templates with `renderFontPackCard`, `primaryFontFromStack`, card click + Enter/Space + arrow-key navigation, ARIA `radiogroup` semantics, and an active-summary band that lives-quotes the resolved Brand / UI / Mono CSS stacks.
  - Reason: Difatha reported that even after switching the font pack in `/create` or `/update`, bespoke themes (`showroom-shine-cars-bespoke`, `kain-motors-bespoke`, `auto-wow-uk-bespoke`) ignored the choice. Root cause: brand-record fonts were being written to `theme.fonts.ui/brand/mono` and the bespoke themes' `BrandStyles.tsx` already exposed them as `--font-ui-family-override` / `--font-brand-family-override` CSS vars (and `base.css` already consumed those vars), but the **Google Fonts CSS for the chosen family was never loaded** — `showroom-shine-cars-bespoke` had no `@import` at all, `kain-motors-bespoke`/`auto-wow-uk-bespoke` only imported their hard-coded archetype fonts. So `--font-ui-family-override: "Roboto, ..."` resolved against a font the browser had no `@font-face` for and silently fell back to `system-ui`. The new dynamic `buildGoogleFontsImport` extracts the first concrete family from each stack (skipping generics like `system-ui`/`sans-serif`/`serif`), de-dupes UI+brand, and emits a single `@import` so whatever the operator picks actually paints. Second half of the request — the dropdown was the dullest input on the page; the redesign turns it into a tile grid where each pack previews its own typography, which is the kind of decision-aid font pairings deserve.
  - Notes: `tsc --noEmit` produced only pre-existing errors (none from new files / changed BrandStyles). The `<select id="fontPack">` is kept in the DOM as the canonical data store — every existing call to `select.value` / `select.dispatchEvent('change')` keeps working, so no form-submit changes were needed. Card grid is keyboard-navigable (Enter/Space activates, arrow keys move focus, only the selected card holds `tabindex=0`). Mobile breakpoint at 640px tightens grid `minmax` from 220px to 160px. Custom card stripes the background so it's visually distinct from the curated packs and surfaces the manual CSS-stack inputs below.

- 2026-05-12: Fixed `kain-motors-bespoke` — inventory cards not clickable, cookie banner oversized, sidebar counter misplaced (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/pages/used-cars/UsedCarsClient.tsx` — added a stretched `.cardLink` anchor at the top of each `<article>` so the entire card surface navigates to `/used-cars/<slug>`; removed the hover-only `.cardOverlay` "View details" pattern; quick-actions buttons (wishlist/compare) moved out of the overlay into a top-right pill with `e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation()` so clicks don't bubble to the Link; counter span (`{resultsCount} vehicles available`) removed from `.filtersHeader` and added to a new `.toolbarTopRow` above search/sort; toolbar restructured into 2 rows — top is `count + Filters toggle`, bottom is `search + Sort + Price + Year`; dropped unused `ArrowUpRight` import. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/pages/used-cars/page.module.css` — new `.cardLink` + `.cardLinkLabel` (visually-hidden AT text) styles, `.cardSpecs` switched from `flex-wrap` to `grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr` (2-col equal width, chips ellipsis at min-width:0), `.cardQuickActions` repositioned top-right with translucent dark pill + z-index:3, `.toolbar` rebuilt as flex-column with `.toolbarTopRow` + `.toolbarBottomRow`, `.topbarControls` switched to `grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))` so Sort/Price/Year are visually equal, search input got an absolutely-positioned magnifier with padding-left clearance on the input. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/KainCookieBanner.tsx` — body shrunk to a single 1-line lead (dropped the eyebrow + italic Playfair title pair); toggle group flattened to horizontal flex row; button labels shortened ("Manage / Save / Reject / Accept all"). `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/KainCookieBanner.module.css` — entirely rewritten as a slim full-width bottom strip (left:0, right:0, no max-width, no margin-left:auto), horizontal flex layout, ~56-80px tall when collapsed, ~100-140px expanded; small "Cookies" gold pill replaces the italic Playfair title; toggle inputs shrunk from 44×24 to 32×18; buttons reduced to 0.74rem font / 8-14px padding / 36px min-height. 3 new feedback memories saved + MEMORY.md index updated.
  - Reason: Difatha QA'd the kain-motors-bespoke preview (rendered through Columbus Vehicles brand record) and surfaced three categorical issues in the same screenshot: (1) inventory cards visually looked clickable but only the hover-only `.cardOverlay` "View details" button was actually an anchor — touch devices had no way to navigate, and even on desktop the title/image/specs all looked like dead pixels. (2) Vehicle counter `12 vehicles available` was buried in the sidebar `.filtersHeader` block where it competed with the "FILTERS" title and "Reset" link, leaving the cards area without a clear "what am I looking at" signal; topbar filters were also disorganised — search on left + actions on right with no labels visible and 3 unequal-width selects free-flowing. (3) Cookie banner was a 400×600px vertical card pinned to the bottom-right (because `max-width: 760px` + `margin-left: auto` at narrow widths) with an italic Playfair-Display title "We use cookies to keep the showroom running smoothly." that wrapped to 6+ lines — looked like a modal dialog or feature box, not chrome.
  - Notes: All three bugs are categorical recurrences for the next `/new-theme` run if not memorised — saved as `feedback_inventory_card_link_and_spec_grid.md` (stretched Link pattern + 2-col specs grid), `feedback_inventory_topbar_counter_placement.md` (2-row toolbar pattern with count + Filters on top, search + 3 equal selects below), `feedback_cookie_banner_compact_strip.md` (slim full-width bottom strip; never tall narrow cards with italic editorial titles for chrome). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean for kain-motors-bespoke. The cookie-banner anti-pattern memory applies retroactively to every existing theme — AutoWowCookieBanner, ShowroomCookieBanner, etc. should be audited next pass.

- 2026-05-12: Fixed `kain-motors-bespoke` — inventory page hero invisible, vehicle card images blank, missing card body CSS, oversized stacking titles across every page (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/pages/used-cars/page.module.css` — `.heroInner` now `position: relative; z-index: 1` so the heroInner content sits above the HeroBackdrop SVG's darken-pass overlay; `.heroTitle` clamp dropped from `clamp(2rem, 5vw, 3.4rem)` to `clamp(1.6rem, 3.6vw, 2.4rem)` and `max-width: 22ch` removed; `.heroLead` clamp tightened to `clamp(1rem, 1.6vw, 1.15rem)` and `max-width: 56ch` removed; `.cardImage` now declares `background-size: cover; background-position: center; background-repeat: no-repeat` (was missing — defaults tiled the photo at natural pixel size); added full CSS for `.cardBody`, `.cardTitleRow`, `.cardMeta`, `.cardPrice`, `.cardSpecs`, `.cardFooter` that the verbatim-ported UsedCarsClient JSX references but the prestige page.module.css never defined (price was running into meta with no separator; specs were unstyled plain text + icons). `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/styles/base.css` — `.kain-page-hero-title` clamp from `(2.2rem, 5vw, 3.6rem)` to `(1.6rem, 3.6vw, 2.4rem)` + dropped `max-width: 18ch` (this was the source of the "FINANCE FROM REGULATED LENDERS, NOT PRESSURED SALESPEOPLE" 5-line stacker); `.kain-page-hero-lead` lost its `max-width: 58ch`; `.kain-section-title` reduced to `(1.4rem, 3vw, 2rem)`; `.kain-section-lead` lost `max-width: 64ch`; `.sps-section-title` reduced to `(1.5rem, 3.2vw, 2.2rem)` + lost `max-width: 56ch` on its subtitle. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/Hero.module.css` — home hero `.title` clamp from `(2.4rem, 5.6vw, 4.4rem)` to `(2rem, 4.4vw, 3rem)` + dropped `max-width: 18ch`; `.lead` lost `max-width: 56ch`; `.figureNumber` reduced to `(2.2rem, 4.4vw, 3rem)`. Section components — `CtaBanner.module.css` (title `(1.6rem, 3.6vw, 2.4rem)`, dropped `max-width: 640px` on copyBlock + 56ch on lead), `ServicesPreview.module.css` (title `(1.6rem, 3.6vw, 2.4rem)`, dropped 22ch + 56ch caps), `FeaturedAcquisitions.module.css` (title `(1.5rem, 3.2vw, 2.2rem)`, dropped 52ch lead cap), `AcquisitionsRow.module.css` (title `(1.5rem, 3.2vw, 2.1rem)`, dropped 52ch description cap), `Reviews.module.css` (title `(1.5rem, 3.2vw, 2.2rem)`, dropped 24ch cap). Pages — `about/page.module.css` title `(1.5rem, 3.2vw, 2.2rem)`, `recently-sold/page.module.css` title `(1.5rem, 3.2vw, 2.2rem)` + lead 1.08rem upper, `used-cars/[slug]/page.module.css` title `(1.4rem, 3.2vw, 2rem)` + dropped 22ch on title + 56ch on financeLead, `contact/page.module.css` lost 56ch on formLead, `finance/page.module.css` lost 56ch on applyLead, `part-exchange/page.module.css` lost 56ch on formLead, `components/Footer.module.css` lost 360px on brandColumn. 3 new feedback memories + updated `feedback_no_max_width_on_titles.md` with stricter 2026-05-12 ceilings.
  - Reason: Difatha QA'd the kain-motors-bespoke preview against the Columbus Vehicles brand record (which paints `--color-primary` as teal) and surfaced four serious bugs in one screenshot: (1) finance page hero stacked into a 5-line wall of text "FINANCE FROM REGULATED LENDERS, NOT PRESSURED SALESPEOPLE" that filled the entire viewport — root cause was `clamp(2.2rem, 5vw, 3.6rem)` at 1280px = 3.6rem on a title with `max-width: 18ch` forcing wrap. (2) Inventory page hero rendered with the title invisible — the HeroBackdrop SVG (which fills with brand-primary teal and ends with a `<rect fill="#000000" opacity="0.22" />` darken-pass) was painting on top of the heroInner content because the inner div had no `position: relative; z-index: 1` to sit above the absolute-positioned SVG. (3) Vehicle cards rendered with the photo area showing as a faint light gradient — the `.cardImage` div sets `style={{ backgroundImage: ... }}` inline but the CSS class only declared `background: var(--surface-bg-dark)` without `background-size: cover/position: center/repeat: no-repeat`, so even valid URLs tiled at natural size and the dark fallback bled through. (4) Card body had no CSS at all for `.cardBody`, `.cardTitleRow`, `.cardMeta`, `.cardPrice`, `.cardSpecs`, `.cardFooter` — the verbatim-ported UsedCarsClient.tsx references those classes but the prestige page.module.css only styled `.card` + `.cardImage`. Result: price slammed into the meta text ("Black£16,299") with no separator, specs row was unstyled plain text + icons. The user's directive was clear: "avoid this big title that stacks and avoid using max-width property and reduce title fonts on all pages" — so the fix is theme-wide, not just the broken page.
  - Notes: All four bugs are categorical recurrences for the next `/new-theme` run if not memorised — saved as `feedback_hero_backdrop_z_index.md` (z-index pair on heroInner), `feedback_card_image_background.md` (background-size:cover trio on cardImage), `feedback_used_cars_client_css_completeness.md` (every styles.X needs CSS), plus an update to `feedback_no_max_width_on_titles.md` adding the 2026-05-12 stricter ceilings (hero ≤ 3rem, page hero ≤ 2.4rem, section ≤ 2.2rem; ALL max-widths on text elements removed, not just titles). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean for kain-motors-bespoke. The kept-JSX-verbatim contract from the SKILL doesn't excuse incomplete CSS — Phase 8 must scan `styles.<X>` references and verify each has a corresponding rule.

- 2026-05-12: Fixed `kain-motors-bespoke` — Browse-by-Make, Hero make dropdown, Recently-Sold rail, Featured-Acquisitions masonry, social-icon disabled treatment (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/BrowseByMake.tsx` (+ `.module.css`) — normalise `meta.available.makes` from `string[] | object[]` to `string[]`; redesigned chips (initial-letter italic disc + name + gold arrow). `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/Hero.tsx` — same data-shape fix for the make `<select>` dropdown. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/VehicleCard.tsx` (+ `.module.css`) — new `sold` prop adds grayscale image + diagonal SOLD stamp + struck-through Playfair price + "Sold price" eyebrow + hides wishlist/compare buttons + hides CTA row; compact variant now keeps spec chips (capped at 3) + monthly price visible. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/AcquisitionsRow.tsx` (+ `.module.css`) — passes `sold={endpoint === 'recently-sold'}` to cards; fixed desktop rail (was leaking `grid-auto-flow: column` past `grid-template-columns`) to a proper 4-up grid with `:nth-child(n+5){display:none}`. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/FeaturedAcquisitions.tsx` — fetch limit reduced from 6 to 5 and sliced to match the 5-cell masonry layout (1 feature + 2×2 small); first card now uses `variant="standard"` while the four companions use `variant="compact"`; skeleton renders 5 placeholders with the first marked as featureCell. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/FeaturedAcquisitions.module.css` — desktop ≥1024px uses `grid-template-rows: minmax(300px, auto) minmax(300px, auto)` and `align-items: stretch` so feature cell visually balances the 2×2 stack. `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/components/Header.tsx` + `Footer.tsx` — every social icon now renders as an `<a>` at full opacity even when `brand.socialLinks[platform]` is empty; href falls back to the platform homepage (facebook.com / instagram.com / youtube.com / x.com / linkedin.com); `socialMuted` class no longer applied anywhere in JSX. `MEMORY.md` + 4 new feedback memories saved (`feedback_inventory_meta_shape.md`, `feedback_recently_sold_rail_treatment.md`, `feedback_compact_card_dont_strip.md`, `feedback_masonry_grid_balance.md`); updated `feedback_topbar_essentials.md` to require the fallback-URL pattern in all 3 surfaces (top bar + mobile drawer + Footer).
  - Reason: Difatha QA'd the kain-motors-bespoke prospect preview and surfaced four broken sections at once — (1) Browse by Make rendered 8 blank chips because the component assumed `meta.available.makes` was `Array<{key,count}>` when the API actually returns `string[]`; same wrong assumption left the hero's make `<select>` empty. (2) Recently Sold rail looked identical to Recent Acquisitions — same teal "View details / Reserve" CTAs, same gold action icons, normal pricing — so buyers would attempt to reserve sold cars. (3) Featured Acquisitions masonry was lopsided: `limit=6` items into a 5-cell layout pushed a 6th card into a phantom row 3, and `grid-auto-rows: minmax(0, auto)` left feature/companion heights misaligned. (4) Social icons in the Footer + mobile drawer still rendered as `<span class="socialMuted">` for missing brand URLs even though the top bar had been fixed earlier — the rule applies on every surface where socials appear.
  - Notes: All four bugs are categorically the kind that would recur on the next `/new-theme` run if not memorised. Saved as feedback memories with `Why:` and `How to apply:` blocks so future invocations of the skill catch them before the audit gate. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean for `kain-motors-bespoke` after the changes. No new audit advisories. Behavioural rule recap for the SKILL: `meta.available.makes` and `meta.available.bodies` are always `string[]`; "recently-sold" rails must visually distinguish sold cars (grayscale + stamp + struck price + hide wishlist/compare/CTAs); compact card variants only hide CTA buttons, never spec chips; "1 feature + 2×2" masonry layouts must slice to exactly 5 items and use constrained `minmax(300px, auto)` row heights; social icons always render as `<a>` at full opacity (fallback to platform homepage URL) in every surface — top bar, mobile drawer, and Footer.

- 2026-05-12: Added `kain-motors-bespoke` theme — bespoke prestige preview for Kain Motors (Manchester) via `/new-theme` (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `app/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/*` (full theme contract — Shell, Header w/ top contact bar + mobile drawer + search overlay, Footer w/ Carous attribution + mega-footer, Hero w/ mixed-media editorial layout + animated brand glow + figure-panel, EditionStrip, TrustBand, FeaturedAcquisitions, AcquisitionsRow, ServicesPreview, PullQuote, CtaBanner, Reviews, BrowseByMake, VehicleCard, PageHero, BrandLogo, KainCookieBanner, KainA11yToolbar); `public/themes/kain-motors-bespoke/*` (7-slot archetype imagery + favicon SVG + logo.png cached from dealer site); fresh inner pages (about/contact/services/finance/part-exchange/sell-my-car/compare/wishlist/privacy-policy/cookie-policy/recently-sold); inventory list page module.css rewritten for prestige; vehicle detail page wholly rewritten with full-bleed mosaic gallery + EnquiryModal-triggered enquiry CTAs + similar-vehicles row + makes-SEO panel + lightbox + UK number-plate styled reg chip; co-located client islands for ContactForm/FinanceForm/FinanceCalculator/PartExchangeForm/WishlistClient/CompareClient/SellYourCarMount; `theme/theme-manifest.json` updated.
  - Reason: prospect preview for Kain Motors Ltd, a Manchester independent appointment-only used-car & van dealer (Midlands Street, M12 6LB). The gold-on-black hexagonal badge + condensed-bold wordmark + sleek sports-car silhouette maps to the **prestige** archetype with magazine-style editorial composition (Oswald headings + DM Sans body + Playfair Display Italic for editorial flourishes). Operator-supplied primary `#d1b67a` was validated by `tools/check-palette-policy.mjs` and auto-darkened 36% to `#86744e` to clear AA white-on-primary; the original gold is preserved as `--kain-accent-warm` for dark-tier accent surfaces.
  - Notes: Generated via `/new-theme`. Logo: `tools/.logo-cache/kain-motors.png` (Cloudflare-fronted CDN, fetched via curl + browser UA — WebFetch returned 403). URL: https://kainmotors.co.uk. Dealer DNA captured from JSON-LD structured data (phone +44 161 399 0508, email info@kainmotors.co.uk, hours Mon-Sat 09:30-17:30, socials facebook+instagram+twitter+youtube+linkedin, services: Finance/Warranty/Delivery/Sell-your-car/Part-exchange/Valuations). Fonts: Oswald (500;600;700) + DM Sans (400;500;600;700) + Playfair Display Italic. Primary: `#86744e` (darkened from #d1b67a). Accent-warm: `#d1b67a`. Archetype: `prestige`. Audit: 0 blockers, 79 advisories. Similarity vs springalls baseline: 0.015 – 0.107 across all 5 checked files (well below 0.85 threshold). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. Imagery: classic-fallback Unsplash photos for all 7 slots (no curated prestige catalogue yet — operator should swap per slot via `/update/<slug>` for the real pitch). Favicon: archetype-aware SVG with glyph 'K' on charcoal disk with metallic ring.

- 2026-05-12: `/new-theme` SKILL — added optional free-text `contextHint` 4th input so themes can reflect non-default business mixes (bikes + cars, EV-only, classics, commercial vans) (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `.claude/skills/new-theme/SKILL.md` — frontmatter `description` now advertises "3 required + 1 optional context hint" with examples; Mode A overview paragraph; Invocation block adds `--context "<text>"` flag and a worked example; **Phase A1** adds Step 2 parser rule (`context:` label OR any leftover free-text line, multi-line joined with `; `), Step 3 validation (trim to 240 chars at sentence boundary, optional, never re-prompts), and new Step 4 persisting sidecar at `tools/.context-hint/<slug>.json` for cross-tool resilience; **Phase A3** WebFetch prompt now conditionally embeds the hint with explicit instructions to extract sub-section nav (/bikes, /vans, /classics), category-specific services/finance, split inventory counts, and category imagery — treated as authoritative even when the home page leads with cars; **Phase A5** DNA `notes.contextHint` field added to the schema + a dedicated callout block listing every Phase 8 place it must apply (hero copy, inventory category chips, nav labels, About/Services copy); **Phase 8** "Before designing, read these" gets a new **Step 0** putting `notes.contextHint` ahead of the per-theme CHECKLIST.md with a "if you find yourself writing 'used cars in <city>' while the hint says 'bikes AND cars', you've missed step 0" sentinel; Tested-defaults inputs tally updated from "3" to "3 required + 1 optional".
  - Reason: Difatha asked for an optional 4th input on `/new-theme` so prospects with non-default business mixes (e.g. dealers selling motorbikes AND cars under the same brand, EV-only specialists, classic-car-only outfits, commercial-van dealers) get a theme whose hero copy, inventory chips, and nav labels reflect their actual mix instead of defaulting to generic "used cars". Today's flow assumed every dealer is a generic used-car dealer; the scrape would miss /bikes sub-sections, hero copy would always say "used cars in <city>", and inventory chips would always be body-type defaults — even when the operator knew better. The hint lets one extra line of input fix that without making the simple-dealer case any more friction (the field is fully optional, never re-prompted, and silently drops if empty).
  - Notes: No code/scaffolder changes — the hint is metadata that travels through DNA notes + downstream phase instructions. Parser is defensive: explicit `context:` label OR any leftover free-text line (after URL/path/hex matching) is treated as the hint. The Phase A3 WebFetch prompt embed is conditional — omitted entirely when the hint is null, so the existing simple-dealer scrape behaviour is unchanged. Phase 8 Step 0 sentinel ("if you find yourself writing 'used cars' while the hint says 'bikes AND cars'…") is deliberate — it's the kind of regression that's easy to drift back into when the kept inventory data-layer wrappers are all generic. Slack updated.

Palette policy passed AA white-on-primary at 7.39:1 with zero darken needed; full 11-pair contrast matrix AAA except muted text at AA 5.91:1. Audit: 0 blockers, 30 advisories (mostly intentional — universal colors like #facc15 review stars / #25d366 WhatsApp green / #4ade80 status dot, mobile-simplification max-width queries, Server-Component page wrappers with no data-aos since islands are co-located). Similarity check flagged `pages/used-cars/page.tsx` at 0.943 against springalls baseline — expected, that's the kept data-layer wrapper per SKILL contract; the redesigned render layer is in `UsedCarsClient.tsx` (rebuilt with sticky toolbar, active-chip strip, brand-tinted hero band, VehicleCard grid).

- 2026-05-11: `/new-theme` SKILL — `Checklist.md` is now the canonical component source; workpackage emits a per-theme `CHECKLIST.md` to tick off (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: `tools/generate-theme-workpackage.mjs` (parses `.claude/skills/new-theme/Checklist.md`, ingests every required `[1]` item as a `checklist-component` work item, writes a marked copy to `app/themes/<id>/CHECKLIST.md` alongside the existing `tools/.theme-work/<id>.json`; added `--name` arg, expanded summary, accepts/falls back to `dna.brand.displayName`); `.claude/skills/new-theme/SKILL.md` (rewrote Phase 7.5c to document the dual output + the canonical-source rule; reordered Phase 8 "Before designing, read these" to put per-theme CHECKLIST.md first; added a pre-flight completeness gate to Phase 10c — every required item must be `[x]` before the static audit runs; added a "Things the skill must NOT do" entry forbidding inventing components from anywhere other than Checklist.md).
  - Reason: Before this, Phase 8's "what components must exist" set was scattered across hardcoded JS arrays in the workpackage tool + the archetype specs in `docs/theme-archetype-specs.md` + Claude's head. Different runs produced different completeness gates. Difatha asked for `Checklist.md` to be the single source of truth so nothing gets omitted and every shipped theme carries a permanent build-completeness record.
  - Notes: First run against `auto-wow-uk-bespoke` produced 99 work items (65 checklist-components + 15 stubs + 6 kept-redesigns + 6 archetype-required + 7 quality-bar); per-theme CHECKLIST.md has 65 required + 130 optional bullets organised under the source file's section hierarchy. Optional `[ ]` items in the source render with an `_(optional)_` suffix in the per-theme copy. Phase 8 ticks each required item as the corresponding component lands; the file ships with the theme as the build record.

- 2026-05-11: `/new-theme` SKILL audit fixes — 10 recommendations shipped end-to-end (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: **5 new audit rules** in `tools/audit-theme.mjs` (`lib-hero-title-too-big`, `lib-image-not-brand-driven`, `std-unrouted-href`, `lib-missing-carous-credit`, `lib-missing-home-link`); tightened `lib-missing-cookie-banner` semantics (now flags global-widget mounts unless explicit `audit-ignore-file: lib-missing-cookie-banner — using global` is set, requiring a per-theme `<Name>CookieBanner.tsx` by default); **2 new tools** — `tools/generate-theme-workpackage.mjs` (Phase 7.5c: emits a 30–40-item checklist to `tools/.theme-work/<id>.json` covering stubs + kept-redesigns + archetype-required components + Quality Bar items; Phase 8 entry rule reads it into TodoWrite) and `tools/check-theme-similarity.mjs` (Phase 10d: Jaccard-shingle similarity check against `springalls-classic` baseline on Hero/home/used-cars/recently-sold; threshold 0.85 catches "another theme, same palette" regressions); `tools/check-theme-contrast.mjs` gained `--write-corrected <path>` flag that patches the on-disk logo-colors JSON when contrast suggestions land; `tools/extract-logo-colors.mjs` gained `--character "<phrase>"` flag persisting the A2b vision-derived character into the artifact; `tools/scaffold-theme.mjs` lost the dead `ARCHETYPE_TO_TEMPLATE` table (every entry mapped to `springalls-classic` — misleading); `.gitignore` adds `tools/.theme-work/` + the other tools/.* caches. **SKILL.md**: expanded DNA schema (added `notes.logoCharacter` + `notes.archetype` as required must-have fields); Phase A2a documents `--character` flag; Phase A2c documents `--write-corrected`; **new "Kept-vs-stubbed file map" table in Phase 7** (closes the Pitfall row 30 regression class — "kept" does NOT mean "don't touch"); new Phase 7.5c documentation (work-package generator + TodoWrite entry rule); new Phase 10d documentation (similarity check); new "Phases safe to run in parallel" table (Phase 2 vs 3, Phase 7.5a/b/c, Phase 10a/b/c/d — saves ~15–30s wall clock); added canonical-home cross-references on Motion + Inventory sections; fixed direct contradiction between Motion §"≥4 AOS per page" and Restraint §"2–3 NOT 4+" (Motion now defers to Restraint with the audit floor `motion-aos-min-count` clarified as a floor not a target); Required Widgets header now points readers back to Quality Bar for policy.
  - Reason: Yesterday's purge of 5 `-bespoke` themes traced to Phase 8 having no definition of done — every theme deletion was a Phase 8 regression. The audit was the right backstop in principle but ~10 Quality Bar rules existed as paper only (no tool enforcement); contradictions between Motion and Restraint counts made the contract ambiguous; `scaffold-theme.mjs` had dead archetype-routing code; the DNA schema didn't persist the `logoCharacter` field Claude was carrying in head across phases; the contrast checker suggested fixes but didn't write them back; there was no cross-theme similarity check to catch "renamed identifiers, same JSX" clones. The 10 recommendations from the audit report all addressed these gaps; this commit ships them.
  - Applied: Each new audit rule has a focused regex + a precise message linking to its Quality Bar section. `std-unrouted-href` carries the canonical-route whitelist inline and immediately surfaced 10 real bugs across the retained themes (`/sell-your-car`, `/contact-us`, `/terms`, `/privacy` — all 404s waiting to happen). `lib-missing-cookie-banner` now distinguishes per-theme mount (✓) vs global-only mount (advisory unless explicitly ignored) vs no mount (advisory). The work-package generator hardcodes the scaffolder's STUB_FILES + KEEP_PATTERNS + per-archetype required components (mirrored from `docs/theme-archetype-specs.md`'s "Components Phase 8 must redesign" lists for modern/rugged/luxury/prestige) — kept in the tool rather than parsed at runtime so the schema stays stable. The similarity check uses 32-char shingle Jaccard on normalized text (identifiers lowercased, whitespace squashed, comments stripped) — proven robust: classic-dealer/gilded-drive both score < 0.01 vs springalls-classic baseline (genuinely different); the threshold (0.85) only fires on near-verbatim clones.
  - Verified: `audit-theme.mjs` runs on all 3 retained themes without crash; new rules return 0–6 findings per theme (no false-positive flooding); `std-unrouted-href` catches 10 real route bugs across retained themes that the team can now fix. `check-theme-similarity.mjs` correctly classifies all 3 retained themes as < 0.01 similar to baseline (well below 0.85 threshold). `check-theme-contrast.mjs --write-corrected` smoke-tested with a synthetic logo-colors JSON: failing primary `#fc9801` was correctly patched to suggested `#404040` with `primaryDark` recomputed and a `warnings[]` entry recording the change. `generate-theme-workpackage.mjs` emits a 34-item checklist for the rugged archetype (15 stubs + 6 kept-redesigns + 6 archetype-required + 7 Quality Bar items).
  - Notes: New audit rules are advisory (no blocker escalation) so they nudge future builds without rejecting in-flight work. The cookie-banner rule severity stayed advisory deliberately — embedded admin/intranet previews legitimately don't need consent. Similarity-check threshold (0.85) is empirical; can be tuned with `--threshold`. Work-package generator's per-archetype list mirrors `theme-archetype-specs.md` and must be updated in lockstep if that file changes. The DNA `logoCharacter` field is documented as required but is set by Claude (not enforced by a runtime check) — the persistence is the win, not gate-keeping. Tools' `.theme-work/` cache directory joins the other gitignored caches under `tools/.*`.

- 2026-05-11: Cleared the 5 experimental `-bespoke` themes + sharpened `/new-theme` SKILL mobile-first rules (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: deleted `app/themes/{auto-wow-uk-bespoke, chesterfield-motor-empire-bespoke, columbus-vehicles-bespoke, ele-car-sales-bespoke, ncr-van-sales-bespoke}/` (full theme contracts — pages, components, contexts, styles, public images); removed their entries from `theme/theme-manifest.json` (now 3 themes: classic-dealer + gilded-drive + springalls-classic); regenerated `app/themes/generated/theme-{context,contract,page,shell}-registry.generated.ts` via `npm run theme:sync`; expanded `.claude/skills/new-theme/SKILL.md` §"Mobile-first responsive (must-have)" with a new core-principle preamble + a 13-rule "Simplification rules" sub-section (mobile is NOT a shrunken desktop — drop columns to one, hide decor ≤640px, single primary CTA, sticky bottom bar, full-screen nav overlay, one-field-per-row forms, full-screen sheet modals, fluid clamp() rhythm, mobile DevTools test discipline); added feedback memory `feedback_mobile_simplification.md` + indexed in `MEMORY.md`.
  - Reason: Two-part clean-slate. (a) The 5 `-bespoke` themes were prospect-preview generations from earlier `/new-theme` runs — they shipped before the recent SKILL hardening (autonomous page designs, hero title cap, top-bar essentials, brand-image plumbing, mobile simplification) and were no longer representative of the current quality bar. Keeping them in the repo as references risked future builds copying their patterns. (b) Difatha's mobile-responsiveness directive — "optimize mobile responsiveness so finely and mobile first, not too complex in mobile as it is in the desktop screens" — needed to be encoded in the SKILL so Phase 8 outputs treat mobile as a *simplified* experience, not just a shrunken desktop.
  - Notes: `classic-dealer`, `gilded-drive`, `springalls-classic` retained. Future `/new-theme` runs start clean with the upgraded mobile-first contract. `gilded-drive` has pre-existing tsc errors unrelated to this change (form `value` type mismatches on `main` before this commit). Hidden caches `tools/.theme-dna/`, `tools/.logo-colors/`, `tools/.logo-fixtures/`, `tools/.theme-images/` remain untracked.

- 2026-05-11: Vehicle detail page — 6 must-haves added to the SKILL, two new shared widgets, four new audit advisory rules (owner: Difatha)
  - Scope: new `app/widgets/WhatsAppFab/WhatsAppIcon.tsx` (extracts the official WhatsApp glyph SVG into a reusable component, re-exported from `app/widgets/WhatsAppFab/index.ts`); new `app/widgets/EnquiryModal/{EnquiryModal.tsx, EnquiryModal.module.css, index.ts}` (modal-based vehicle enquiry widget mirroring the carous-platform pattern — two-column layout with form left + Call/Email/WhatsApp side panel using the official glyph, brand-token-driven, `useEnquiryModal()` hook for trigger management, focus management + Escape close + body-scroll lock + `prefers-reduced-motion` support); `app/widgets/WhatsAppFab/WhatsAppFab.tsx` switched to import the shared icon (single source of truth); `tools/audit-theme.mjs` adds 4 new advisory rules (`inv-detail-no-similar-vehicles`, `inv-detail-no-makes-seo`, `lib-whatsapp-icon-generic`, `inv-detail-enquiry-not-modal`); `.claude/skills/new-theme/SKILL.md` adds new Quality Bar §"Vehicle detail page composition (must-have)" with 6 mandates (gallery layout variety, official WhatsApp icon, modal-based enquiry, balanced columns, similar-vehicles row, SEO makes-list), expands Required Widgets section with `<WhatsAppIcon />` and `<EnquiryModal />` entries, adds Pitfall row 33 documenting the regression class, updates audit-rule prefix table to include `inv-` and `motion-`; `docs/inventory-design-library.md` adds two new detail patterns (G. Full-bleed mosaic gallery — the 1+4 mosaic from Difatha's Hunts Motors reference screenshot — and H. Modal-led enquiry — composition pattern that pairs with any of A–G), new "Required detail-page sections" section listing the contract, rotation-table updated with chesterfield-motor-empire flagged for retrofit.
  - Reason: Difatha review of `chesterfield-motor-empire-bespoke` 2026-05-11 surfaced 6 missing must-haves on the vehicle detail page: (1) gallery used the same "main + thumbnail strip" as every other theme — should rotate (full-bleed mosaic, sticky thumb-rail, scroll-stack, etc.), (2) generic `<MessageCircle />` from lucide on the WhatsApp link instead of the official brand glyph, (3) enquiry rendered as a 4-field inline form mid-page (buyers had to scroll past it to reach specs) — should be a modal triggered from multiple call-outs, (4) sidebar dwarfed the gallery on large screens, content hierarchy on small screens prioritised gallery over decision info, (5) no similar-vehicles row after the spec/finance section — buyers who didn't pick this car hit a dead end, (6) no SEO makes-list panel before the footer — missed internal-linking surface for search and a useful pivot for buyers. The theme felt like "another listing template" rather than a deep-funnel commit surface.
  - Applied: Built `<WhatsAppIcon />` as a tiny reusable component (currentColor-inheriting SVG, sized via prop) so themes drop it in alongside the iconic green button surface — and updated `WhatsAppFab` itself to import the same icon so there's no risk of drift between FAB and inline usages. Built `<EnquiryModal />` as a `'use client'` component that consumes `useLeadsForm` for validation parity, with focus management (saves and restores focus on open/close), body-scroll lock, Escape-to-close, brand-token-driven styling that retints per-brand. The companion `useEnquiryModal()` hook returns `{ isOpen, open, close, toggle }` so theme designers don't reimplement modal state per page. Audit rules use heuristic regex matches on file content: `lib-whatsapp-icon-generic` fires when `MessageCircle` from lucide-react sits in the same file as a `wa.me` URL or `whatsappUrl` reference without an `WhatsAppIcon` import; `inv-detail-no-similar-vehicles` looks for "similar vehicles" / "more like this" / "more from this make" markers; `inv-detail-no-makes-seo` looks for "browse by make" / "popular makes" markers; `inv-detail-enquiry-not-modal` fires when a detail-page file has `<form onSubmit>` or `useLeadsForm` without importing `EnquiryModal`. All four new rules are advisories — they nudge future builds without blocking ship.
  - Verified: All 4 new rules fire correctly on `chesterfield-motor-empire-bespoke` (which doesn't yet meet the new bar) — `lib-whatsapp-icon-generic` × 3 (Header.tsx, Footer.tsx, sell-your-car page reference WhatsApp + import MessageCircle), `inv-detail-no-similar-vehicles` × 1, `inv-detail-no-makes-seo` × 1, `inv-detail-enquiry-not-modal` × 1. Audit summary: 0 blockers, 81 advisories (up from 75 — the +6 are exactly the new rules firing). `tsc --noEmit` clean for both new widgets. Existing themes are unaffected (rules only target detail pages and WhatsApp-icon contexts).
  - Notes: chesterfield-motor-empire-bespoke is intentionally NOT retrofitted — these improvements are progressive enhancements for *future* `/new-theme` builds. The next bespoke theme will inherit all 6 must-haves automatically via the SKILL's Phase 8 design phase. The chesterfield rotation entry is annotated as "due for retrofit" so it's clear the bar tightened after it shipped, not that it failed the bar at the time. EnquiryModal honors `prefers-reduced-motion` (animations skip); both new widgets are tree-shakeable (themes that don't import them pay zero bundle cost).
